1import HRS.Prob.Metrics

3/-!
4# Gaussian measures on Hilbert/Banach spaces: Edgeworth expansion, Stein equation, chaos
5
6Sources (HRS periodic main paper):
7* Lemma 7.1 `M:O:lem:iid-edge` (source lines 7729-7736): stated in `HRS.Prob.Edgeworth`, which
8  imports this file so that its proof can use the Stein lemmas below.
9* Lemma 7.2 `M:F:lem:Stein` (7774-7789): Stein equation without a dimension factor.
10* Lemma 7.3 `M:F:lem:contractions` (7805-7819): Banach-valued Gaussian contractions.
11* Proposition 7.18 `M:E:prop:chaos` (9782-9820), first display `E:eq:chaos-isometry`:
12  Gaussian quadratic chaos of a Hilbert–Schmidt operator.
13* Proposition 2.4 `M:H:prop:field-singularity` (701-710): mutual singularity of a
14  Gaussian field law and empirical-measure laws.
15
16## Representation
17* A centered Gaussian random variable `G : Ω → H` is one whose law satisfies
18  `IsCenteredGaussian` (Mathlib `IsGaussian`, all one-dimensional projections centered).
19* Covariance of an `H`-valued random variable: Mathlib's `covarianceBilin (law P X)`.
20* The pairing `⟨D³F(G), M₃⟩` with the third-moment tensor `M₃ = E[X^{⊗3}]` in the projective
21  tensor product is represented by its evaluation `E'[D³F(G)[X', X', X']]` with `X'` an
22  independent copy of `X` (see `docs/ISSUES.md` §G1: for `X` with finite third moment this
23  evaluation equals the pairing with the Bochner integral `E[X^{⊗3}]` in `H^{⊗̂π 3}`, so no
24  content is lost; the projective-tensor formulation is deferred to EDGE-02).
25* Completed projective tensor powers `B^{⊗̂π k}` (Lemma 7.3, `F:eq:firstcontract`): `projTensor B k`,
26  the closed span of the elementary tensors in the dual `TensorDual B k = (B [×k]→L[ℝ] ℝ) →L[ℝ] ℝ`
27  of the bounded `k`-linear forms (see the comment before `TensorDual`).
28* Gaussian chaos (Prop. 7.18): `G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ` i.i.d. standard Gaussians and a symmetric
29  Hilbert–Schmidt matrix `A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ`; `Q(A)` is the `L²` limit of the finite sections
30  `½ ∑_{i,j<n} A_ij (G_i G_j - δ_ij)`.
31-/
32

40section Hilbert

42variable {H : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup H] [InnerProductSpace ℝ H] [CompleteSpace H]
43  [MeasurableSpace H] [BorelSpace H] [SecondCountableTopology H]
44variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
45
46/-- `G` is a centered Gaussian random variable in `H` with covariance bilinear form `C`. -/
47def IsCenteredGaussianRV (G : Ω → H) (C : H → H → ℝ) : Prop :=
48  AEMeasurable G P ∧ IsCenteredGaussian (law P G) ∧
49    ∀ x y, ProbabilityTheory.covarianceBilin (law P G) x y = C x y
50
51/-- Independent copies: `X i` are i.i.d. with the law of `X 0`. -/
52def IsIIDCopies {ι : Type*} (X : ι → Ω → H) : Prop :=
53  ProbabilityTheory.iIndepFun X P ∧ ∀ i j, law P (X i) = law P (X j)
54
55/-- Finite `p`-th moment. -/
56def HasMoment (p : ℝ) (X : Ω → H) : Prop := Integrable (fun ω => ‖X ω‖ ^ p) P
57
58/-- The trilinear-form pairing `⟨D³F(G), E[X^{⊗3}]⟩` represented by evaluation on an
59independent copy `X'` of `X` (living on a product space). -/
60def thirdMomentPairing (F : H → ℝ) (G : Ω → H) (X : Ω → H) : ℝ :=
61  ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 F (G p.1) ![X p.2, X p.2, X p.2] ∂(P.prod P)
62
63
64end Hilbert
65
66section Stein
67
68variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
69  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
70variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
71
72/-- The Stein solution `f(x) = -∫_0^∞ (E[Φ(e^{-u}x + √(1-e^{-2u}) G)] - E[Φ(G)]) du`
73(`F:eq:stein-solution`, lines 7777-7780). -/
74def steinSolution (G : Ω → B) (Φ : B → ℝ) (x : B) : ℝ :=
75  -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ),
76    (∫ ω, Φ (Real.exp (-u) • x + Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) • G ω) ∂P - ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P)
77
78/-- The contraction `⟨D²f(x), C_G⟩ = E[D²f(x)[G, G]]` of a second derivative with the Gaussian
79covariance (representation by expectation over `G`). -/
80def covarianceContraction (G : Ω → B) (f : B → ℝ) (x : B) : ℝ :=
81  ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 f x ![G ω, G ω] ∂P
82
83/-- `Φ ∈ C_b^k(B)`: `C^k` with all derivatives of order `≤ k` bounded. -/
84def MemCb (k : ℕ) (Φ : B → ℝ) : Prop :=
85  ContDiff ℝ k Φ ∧ ∀ j ≤ k, BddAbove (Set.range fun x => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ x‖)
86
87end Stein

95variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]

97lemma contDiff_of_chain (f : B → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (D : ∀ j : ℕ, B → (B [×j]→L[ℝ] ℝ))
98    (h0 : ∀ x, D 0 x = (continuousMultilinearCurryFin0 ℝ B ℝ).symm (f x))
99    (h1 : ∀ j < N, ∀ x, HasFDerivAt (D j)
100      (continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ (D (j+1) x)) x)
101    (h2 : Continuous (D N)) :
102    ContDiff ℝ N f ∧ ∀ j ≤ N, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j f = D j := by
103  have heq : ∀ j ≤ N, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j f = D j := by
104    intro j
105    induction j with
106    | zero =>
107      intro _
108      ext x : 1
109      rw [h0]; rfl
110    | succ j ih =>
111      intro hj
112      have ih' := ih (Nat.le_of_succ_le hj)
113      ext x : 1
114      rw [iteratedFDeriv_succ_eq_comp_left, Function.comp_apply, ih',
115        (h1 j (Nat.lt_of_succ_le hj) x).fderiv]
116      simp
117  refine ⟨?_, heq⟩
118  rw [contDiff_nat_iff_continuous_differentiable]
119  constructor
120  · intro m hm
121    rw [heq m hm]
122    rcases Nat.lt_or_ge m N with h | h
123    · exact continuous_iff_continuousAt.2 fun x => (h1 m h x).continuousAt
124    · have : m = N := le_antisymm (by exact_mod_cast hm) h
125      subst this; exact h2
126  · intro m hm
127    rw [heq m (by exact_mod_cast hm.le)]
128    exact fun x => (h1 m (by exact_mod_cast hm) x).differentiableAt
129
130
131lemma integral_curryLeft_comm {S : Type*} [MeasurableSpace S] (ν : Measure S) (j : ℕ)
132    (φ : S → (B [×(j+1)]→L[ℝ] ℝ)) (hφ : Integrable φ ν) :
133    ∫ p, continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ (φ p) ∂ν =
134      continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ (∫ p, φ p ∂ν) := by
135  let L : (B [×(j+1)]→L[ℝ] ℝ) →L[ℝ] (B →L[ℝ] B [×j]→L[ℝ] ℝ) :=
136    (continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ).toContinuousLinearEquiv.toContinuousLinearMap
137  exact ContinuousLinearMap.integral_comp_comm (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B [×(j+1)]→L[ℝ] ℝ)
138    (Fₗ := B →L[ℝ] B [×j]→L[ℝ] ℝ) L hφ
139
140lemma contDiff_integral_dominated {S : Type*} [MeasurableSpace S] (ν : Measure S)
141    (g : S → B → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (hN : 1 ≤ N)
142    (hcd : ∀ p, ContDiff ℝ N (g p))
143    (hint : ∀ x, Integrable (fun p => g p x) ν)
144    (hmeas : ∀ j ≤ N, ∀ x, AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x) ν)
145    (b : ℕ → S → ℝ) (hb : ∀ j, 1 ≤ j → j ≤ N → Integrable (b j) ν)
146    (hbd : ∀ j, 1 ≤ j → j ≤ N → ∀ p x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x‖ ≤ b j p) :
147    ContDiff ℝ N (fun x => ∫ p, g p x ∂ν) ∧
148      ∀ j ≤ N, ∀ x, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (fun x => ∫ p, g p x ∂ν) x =
149        ∫ p, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x ∂ν := by
150  set D : ∀ j : ℕ, B → (B [×j]→L[ℝ] ℝ) := fun j x => ∫ p, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x ∂ν with hD
151  have hF_int : ∀ j ≤ N, ∀ x, Integrable (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x) ν := by
152    intro j hj x
153    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos j with h0 | h0
154    · subst h0
155      refine (hint x).norm.mono' (hmeas 0 (Nat.zero_le _) x) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
156      simp
157    · exact (hb j h0 hj).mono' (hmeas j hj x) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => hbd j h0 hj p x)
158  have h0 : ∀ x, D 0 x = (continuousMultilinearCurryFin0 ℝ B ℝ).symm (∫ p, g p x ∂ν) := by
159    intro x
160    simp only [hD]
161    have := ContinuousLinearEquiv.integral_comp_comm (μ := ν)
162      (continuousMultilinearCurryFin0 ℝ B ℝ).symm.toContinuousLinearEquiv (fun p => g p x)
163    exact this
164  have h1 : ∀ j < N, ∀ x, HasFDerivAt (D j)
165      (continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ (D (j+1) x)) x := by
166    intro j hj x₀
167    have hjN : j + 1 ≤ N := hj
168    have key := hasFDerivAt_integral_of_dominated_of_fderiv_le (μ := ν) (s := Set.univ)
169      (F := fun x p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p) x)
170      (F' := fun x p => continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ
171        (iteratedFDeriv ℝ (j+1) (g p) x))
172      (x₀ := x₀) (bound := b (j+1)) Filter.univ_mem
173      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => hmeas j (by omega) x)
174      (hF_int j (by omega) x₀)
175      (((continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ).continuous.comp_aestronglyMeasurable
176        (hmeas (j+1) hjN x₀)))
177      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p x _ => by
178        rw [LinearIsometryEquiv.norm_map]; exact hbd (j+1) (by omega) hjN p x)
179      (hb (j+1) (by omega) hjN)
180      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p x _ => by
181        have hd : Differentiable ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (g p)) :=
182          (hcd p).differentiable_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast hj)
183        exact (hd x).hasFDerivAt)
184    have e : ∫ p, continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ
185        (iteratedFDeriv ℝ (j+1) (g p) x₀) ∂ν =
186        continuousMultilinearCurryLeftEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin (j+1) => B) ℝ (D (j+1) x₀) := by
187      simp only [hD]
188      exact integral_curryLeft_comm ν j _ (hF_int (j+1) hjN x₀)
189    rw [← e]
190    exact key
191  have h2 : Continuous (D N) := by
192    refine continuous_of_dominated (bound := b N) (fun x => (hmeas N le_rfl x))
193      (fun x => Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => hbd N hN le_rfl p x) (hb N hN le_rfl)
194      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
195    exact (hcd p).continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast le_rfl)
196  obtain ⟨hC, hE⟩ := contDiff_of_chain (fun x => ∫ p, g p x ∂ν) N D h0 h1 h2
197  refine ⟨hC, fun j hj x => ?_⟩
198  rw [hE j hj]
199
200end ParamInt

203section SteinAux

205variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
206  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
207
208/-- OU scalings `e^{-u}` and `√(1-e^{-2u})`. -/
209def ouA (u : ℝ) : ℝ := Real.exp (-u)
210def ouS (u : ℝ) : ℝ := Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u))
211
212lemma ouA_pos (u : ℝ) : 0 < ouA u := Real.exp_pos _
213@[fun_prop] lemma continuous_ouA : Continuous ouA := by unfold ouA; fun_prop
214@[fun_prop] lemma continuous_ouS : Continuous ouS := by unfold ouS; fun_prop
215lemma ouA_sq (u : ℝ) : ouA u ^ 2 = Real.exp (-2 * u) := by
216  unfold ouA; rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; push_cast; ring
217lemma ouA_le_one {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) : ouA u ≤ 1 := by
218  unfold ouA; rw [Real.exp_le_one_iff]; linarith
219lemma ouS_nonneg (u : ℝ) : 0 ≤ ouS u := Real.sqrt_nonneg _
220lemma ouS_sq {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) : ouS u ^ 2 = 1 - ouA u ^ 2 := by
221  unfold ouS; rw [Real.sq_sqrt, ouA_sq]
222  rw [sub_nonneg, Real.exp_le_one_iff]; linarith
223lemma ouA_sq_add_ouS_sq {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) : ouA u ^ 2 + ouS u ^ 2 = 1 := by
224  rw [ouS_sq hu]; ring
225lemma ouS_le_one {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) : ouS u ≤ 1 := by
226  have := ouA_sq_add_ouS_sq hu
227  nlinarith [sq_nonneg (ouA u), ouS_nonneg u]
228lemma one_sub_ouS_le {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 ≤ u) : 1 - ouS u ≤ ouA u ^ 2 := by
229  have h1 := ouS_le_one hu
230  have h2 := ouA_sq_add_ouS_sq hu
231  nlinarith [ouS_nonneg u]
232
233
234/-- the integrand of the single-integral representation of the Stein solution -/
235def steinG (Φ : B → ℝ) (p : ℝ × B) (x : B) : ℝ := Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) - Φ p.2
236
237lemma iteratedFDeriv_affine (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 5 Φ) (c : ℝ) (w : B) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ 5) (x : B) :
238    iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (fun x => Φ (c • x + w)) x = c ^ j • iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ (c • x + w) := by
239  have h1 : (fun x => Φ (c • x + w)) = (fun z => Φ (z + w)) ∘ (c • ContinuousLinearMap.id ℝ B) := by
240    ext x; simp
241  have hcd : ContDiff ℝ 5 (fun z => Φ (z + w)) := hΦ.comp (contDiff_id.add contDiff_const)
242  rw [h1, ContinuousLinearMap.iteratedFDeriv_comp_right _ hcd x (by exact_mod_cast hj),
243    iteratedFDeriv_comp_add_right]
244  ext v
245  simp only [ContinuousMultilinearMap.compContinuousLinearMap_apply, ContinuousLinearMap.smul_apply,
246    ContinuousLinearMap.id_apply, ContinuousMultilinearMap.smul_apply]
247  rw [ContinuousMultilinearMap.map_smul_univ]
248  simp
249
250lemma iteratedFDeriv_steinG (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 5 Φ) (p : ℝ × B) (j : ℕ) (hj1 : 1 ≤ j)
251    (hj5 : j ≤ 5) (x : B) :
252    iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x =
253      (ouA p.1) ^ j • iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) := by
254  have h1 : steinG Φ p = (fun x => Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2)) - fun _ => Φ p.2 := by
255    ext x; rfl
256  have hcd : ContDiff ℝ 5 (fun x => Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2)) :=
257    hΦ.comp ((contDiff_const.smul contDiff_id).add contDiff_const)
258  rw [h1, iteratedFDeriv_sub_apply (hcd.contDiffAt.of_le (by exact_mod_cast hj5))
259    (contDiff_const.contDiffAt), iteratedFDeriv_const_of_ne (by omega), iteratedFDeriv_affine Φ hΦ _ _ j hj5]
260  simp
261
262lemma contDiff_steinG (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 5 Φ) (p : ℝ × B) : ContDiff ℝ 5 (steinG Φ p) := by
263  unfold steinG
264  exact (hΦ.comp ((contDiff_const.smul contDiff_id).add contDiff_const)).sub contDiff_const
265
266
267/-- sup norm of `j`-th derivative -/
268def cbSup (Φ : B → ℝ) (j : ℕ) : ℝ := ⨆ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖
269
270lemma norm_iter_le_cbSup {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) {j : ℕ} (hj : j ≤ 5) (y : B) :
271    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖ ≤ cbSup Φ j :=
272  le_ciSup (f := fun y => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖) (hΦ.2 j hj) y
273
274lemma cbSup_nonneg {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) {j : ℕ} (hj : j ≤ 5) : 0 ≤ cbSup Φ j :=
275  (norm_nonneg _).trans (norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ hj 0)
276
277lemma abs_le_cbSup {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (y : B) : |Φ y| ≤ cbSup Φ 0 := by
278  have := norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ (j := 0) (by norm_num) y
279  simpa using this
280
281lemma lip_cb {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (y z : B) : |Φ z - Φ y| ≤ cbSup Φ 1 * ‖z - y‖ := by
282  have hd : Differentiable ℝ Φ := hΦ.1.differentiable (by norm_num)
283  have hL : LipschitzWith (Real.toNNReal (cbSup Φ 1)) Φ := by
284    refine lipschitzWith_of_nnnorm_fderiv_le hd fun x => ?_
285    rw [← NNReal.coe_le_coe, coe_nnnorm, Real.coe_toNNReal _ (cbSup_nonneg hΦ (by norm_num))]
286    have := norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ (j := 1) (by norm_num) x
287    simpa using this
288  have := hL.dist_le_mul z y
289  rw [Real.dist_eq, dist_eq_norm, Real.coe_toNNReal _ (cbSup_nonneg hΦ (by norm_num))] at this
290  exact this

292section Gaussian
293variable {γ : Measure B} [IsProbabilityMeasure γ]
294
295/-- the product measure on `(0,∞) × B` -/
296abbrev steinNu (γ : Measure B) : Measure (ℝ × B) := (volume.restrict (Set.Ioi (0:ℝ))).prod γ
297
298lemma ae_steinNu_pos : ∀ᵐ p ∂(steinNu γ), 0 < p.1 := by
299  have h : ∀ᵐ u ∂(volume.restrict (Set.Ioi (0:ℝ))), 0 < u := ae_restrict_mem measurableSet_Ioi
300  exact Measure.quasiMeasurePreserving_fst.ae h
301
302lemma integrable_exp_neg_mul (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c) :
303    Integrable (fun u : ℝ => Real.exp (-c * u)) (volume.restrict (Set.Ioi (0:ℝ))) :=
304  exp_neg_integrableOn_Ioi 0 hc
305
306lemma ouA_pow (j : ℕ) (u : ℝ) : ouA u ^ j = Real.exp (-(j : ℝ) * u) := by
307  unfold ouA; rw [← Real.exp_nat_mul]; congr 1; ring
308
309variable {Φ : B → ℝ}
310
311lemma steinG_integrable (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) (x : B) :
312    Integrable (fun p => steinG Φ p x) (steinNu γ) := by
313  set μ0 : Measure ℝ := volume.restrict (Set.Ioi (0:ℝ)) with hμ0
314  have hA1 : Integrable (fun u : ℝ => ouA u) μ0 := by
315    have := integrable_exp_neg_mul 1 one_pos
316    simpa [ouA] using this
317  have hA2 : Integrable (fun u : ℝ => ouA u ^ 2) μ0 := by
318    have := integrable_exp_neg_mul 2 two_pos
319    simpa [ouA_pow] using this
320  have hbd : Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => cbSup Φ 1 * (ouA p.1 * ‖x‖ + ouA p.1 ^ 2 * ‖p.2‖)) (steinNu γ) := by
321    refine Integrable.const_mul (Integrable.add ?_ ?_) _
322    · exact ((hA1.mul_const ‖x‖).comp_fst γ)
323    · exact hA2.mul_prod hγ1
324  refine hbd.mono' ?_ ?_
325  · have hc : Continuous (fun p : ℝ × B => steinG Φ p x) := by
326      unfold steinG
327      have hΦc := hΦ.1.continuous
328      fun_prop
329    exact hc.aestronglyMeasurable
330  · filter_upwards [ae_steinNu_pos (γ := γ)] with p hp
331    unfold steinG
332    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]
333    refine (lip_cb hΦ p.2 _).trans ?_
334    refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left ?_ (cbSup_nonneg hΦ (by norm_num))
335    have h1 : ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2 - p.2 = ouA p.1 • x - (1 - ouS p.1) • p.2 := by
336      module
337    rw [h1]
338    refine (norm_sub_le _ _).trans ?_
339    rw [norm_smul, norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (ouA_pos _),
340      abs_of_nonneg (sub_nonneg.2 (ouS_le_one hp.le))]
341    gcongr
342    exact one_sub_ouS_le hp.le
343
344
345lemma steinG_meas (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ 5) (x : B) :
346    AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x) (steinNu γ) := by
347  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos j with h0 | h0
348  · subst h0
349    have hc : Continuous (fun p : ℝ × B => steinG Φ p x) := by
350      unfold steinG
351      have hΦc := hΦ.1.continuous
352      fun_prop
353    have : (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 0 (steinG Φ p) x) =
354        fun p => (continuousMultilinearCurryFin0 ℝ B ℝ).symm (steinG Φ p x) := rfl
355    rw [this]
356    exact ((continuousMultilinearCurryFin0 ℝ B ℝ).symm.continuous.comp hc).aestronglyMeasurable
357  · have hc : Continuous (fun p : ℝ × B =>
358        (ouA p.1) ^ j • iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2)) := by
359      have hΦc := hΦ.1.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (m := j) (by exact_mod_cast hj)
360      fun_prop
361    have : (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x) =
362        fun p : ℝ × B => (ouA p.1) ^ j • iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) := by
363      ext1 p; exact iteratedFDeriv_steinG Φ hΦ.1 p j h0 hj x
364    rw [this]
365    exact hc.aestronglyMeasurable
366
367lemma steinG_bound (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (j : ℕ) (hj1 : 1 ≤ j) (hj : j ≤ 5) (p : ℝ × B) (x : B) :
368    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x‖ ≤ cbSup Φ j * Real.exp (-(j : ℝ) * p.1) := by
369  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinG Φ hΦ.1 p j hj1 hj, norm_smul, norm_pow, Real.norm_eq_abs,
370    abs_of_pos (ouA_pos _), ouA_pow, mul_comm]
371  gcongr
372  exact norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ hj _
373
374lemma steinG_bound_int (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (j : ℕ) (hj1 : 1 ≤ j) :
375    Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => cbSup Φ j * Real.exp (-(j : ℝ) * p.1)) (steinNu γ) := by
376  have h := (integrable_exp_neg_mul (j : ℝ) (by exact_mod_cast hj1)).const_mul (cbSup Φ j)
377  exact h.comp_fst γ
378
379lemma stein_contDiff_core (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) :
380    ContDiff ℝ 5 (fun x => ∫ p, steinG Φ p x ∂(steinNu γ)) ∧
381      ∀ j ≤ 5, ∀ x, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (fun x => ∫ p, steinG Φ p x ∂(steinNu γ)) x =
382        ∫ p, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x ∂(steinNu γ) := by
383  refine contDiff_integral_dominated (steinNu γ) (steinG Φ) 5 (by norm_num)
384    (contDiff_steinG Φ hΦ.1) (steinG_integrable hΦ hγ1) (fun j hj x => steinG_meas hΦ j hj x)
385    (fun j p => cbSup Φ j * Real.exp (-(j : ℝ) * p.1))
386    (fun j hj1 _ => steinG_bound_int hΦ j hj1) (fun j hj1 hj5 p x => steinG_bound hΦ j hj1 hj5 p x)
387
388
389lemma integrable_cont_bdd' (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (a s : ℝ) (x : B) :
390    Integrable (fun y : B => Φ (a • x + s • y)) γ := by
391  refine Integrable.of_bound (C := cbSup Φ 0) ?_ ?_
392  · exact (hΦ.1.continuous.comp (by fun_prop : Continuous fun y : B => a • x + s • y)).aestronglyMeasurable
393  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
394      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_le_cbSup hΦ _
395
396lemma integrable_Phi (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) : Integrable Φ γ := by
397  refine Integrable.of_bound (C := cbSup Φ 0) hΦ.1.continuous.aestronglyMeasurable ?_
398  exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
399      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_le_cbSup hΦ _
400
401/-- the Stein solution as a single integral against `ν` (γ-form) -/
402lemma stein_inner (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) (u : ℝ) :
403    ∫ y, steinG Φ (u, y) x ∂γ =
404      ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ - ∫ y, Φ y ∂γ := by
405  unfold steinG
406  simp only
407  exact integral_sub (integrable_cont_bdd' hΦ _ _ _) (integrable_Phi hΦ)
408
409
410/-- Stein solution in terms of the Gaussian law `γ` -/
411def steinSolG (γ : Measure B) (Φ : B → ℝ) (x : B) : ℝ :=
412  -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0 : ℝ), (∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ - ∫ y, Φ y ∂γ)
413
414lemma steinSolG_eq_nu (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) (x : B) :
415    steinSolG γ Φ x = -∫ p, steinG Φ p x ∂(steinNu γ) := by
416  unfold steinSolG
417  congr 1
418  rw [integral_prod _ (steinG_integrable hΦ hγ1 x)]
419  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => ?_)
420  exact (stein_inner hΦ x u).symm
421
422lemma contDiff_steinSolG (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) :
423    ContDiff ℝ 5 (steinSolG γ Φ) := by
424  have h := (stein_contDiff_core (γ := γ) hΦ hγ1).1.neg
425  have e : steinSolG γ Φ = fun x => -∫ p, steinG Φ p x ∂(steinNu γ) := by
426    ext x; exact steinSolG_eq_nu hΦ hγ1 x
427  rw [e]; exact h
428
429lemma iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ)
430    (j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ 5) (x : B) :
431    iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinSolG γ Φ) x = -∫ p, iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x ∂(steinNu γ) := by
432  have e : steinSolG γ Φ = -(fun x => ∫ p, steinG Φ p x ∂(steinNu γ)) := by
433    ext x; exact steinSolG_eq_nu hΦ hγ1 x
434  have hcd := (stein_contDiff_core (γ := γ) hΦ hγ1).1
435  rw [e, iteratedFDeriv_neg_apply, (stein_contDiff_core (γ := γ) hΦ hγ1).2 j hj x]
436
437lemma norm_iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG_le (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ)
438    (j : ℕ) (hj1 : 1 ≤ j) (hj : j ≤ 5) (x : B) :
439    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinSolG γ Φ) x‖ ≤ (j : ℝ)⁻¹ * cbSup Φ j := by
440  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG hΦ hγ1 j hj x, norm_neg]
441  refine (norm_integral_le_of_norm_le (steinG_bound_int hΦ j hj1)
442    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => steinG_bound hΦ j hj1 hj p x)).trans (le_of_eq ?_)
443  rw [integral_prod _ (steinG_bound_int hΦ j hj1)]
444  simp only [integral_const, probReal_univ, smul_eq_mul, one_mul]
445  rw [integral_const_mul]
446  have hj0 : (0:ℝ) < j := by exact_mod_cast hj1
447  have := integral_exp_mul_Ioi (a := -(j:ℝ)) (by linarith) 0
448  simp only [mul_zero, Real.exp_zero] at this
449  rw [this]
450  field_simp
451
452end Gaussian
453

455section PLevel
456variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
457variable {Φ : B → ℝ}
458
459lemma steinSolution_eq_steinSolG (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) :
460    steinSolution P G Φ = steinSolG (P.map G) Φ := by
461  funext x
462  unfold steinSolution steinSolG
463  congr 1
464  refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioi fun u _ => ?_
465  have h1 : ∫ ω, Φ (Real.exp (-u) • x + Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) • G ω) ∂P =
466      ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂(P.map G) := by
467    rw [integral_map hG]
468    · rfl
469    · exact (hΦ.1.continuous.comp (by fun_prop : Continuous fun y : B => ouA u • x + ouS u • y)).aestronglyMeasurable
470  have h2 : ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P = ∫ y, Φ y ∂(P.map G) := by
471    rw [integral_map hG hΦ.1.continuous.aestronglyMeasurable]
472  rw [h1, h2]
473
474lemma gaussian_norm_integrable (G : Ω → B) (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G)) :
475    Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) (P.map G) := by
476  haveI := hGauss.1
477  exact (IsGaussian.integrable_id (μ := P.map G)).norm
478
479theorem stein_bounds' (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P) (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G))
480    (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) :
481    (∀ x, IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ =>
482        ∫ ω, Φ (Real.exp (-u) • x + Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) • G ω) ∂P -
483          ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P) (Set.Ioi 0)) ∧
484    ContDiff ℝ 5 (steinSolution P G Φ) ∧
485    ∀ j, 1 ≤ j → j ≤ 5 → ∀ x,
486      ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinSolution P G Φ) x‖ ≤
487        (j : ℝ)⁻¹ * ⨆ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖ := by
488  haveI := hGauss.1
489  have hγ1 := gaussian_norm_integrable P G hGauss
490  refine ⟨fun x => ?_, ?_, fun j hj1 hj5 x => ?_⟩
491  · have h : IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ => ∫ y, steinG Φ (u, y) x ∂(P.map G)) (Set.Ioi 0) :=
492      (steinG_integrable (γ := P.map G) hΦ hγ1 x).integral_prod_left
493    refine h.congr_fun (fun u _ => ?_) measurableSet_Ioi
494    show ∫ y, steinG Φ (u, y) x ∂(P.map G) = _
495    rw [stein_inner hΦ x u]
496    have h1 : ∫ ω, Φ (Real.exp (-u) • x + Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) • G ω) ∂P =
497        ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂(P.map G) := by
498      rw [integral_map hG]
499      · rfl
500      · exact (hΦ.1.continuous.comp (by fun_prop : Continuous fun y : B => ouA u • x + ouS u • y)).aestronglyMeasurable
501    have h2 : ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P = ∫ y, Φ y ∂(P.map G) := by
502      rw [integral_map hG hΦ.1.continuous.aestronglyMeasurable]
503    rw [h1, h2]
504  · rw [steinSolution_eq_steinSolG P G hG hΦ]
505    exact contDiff_steinSolG hΦ hγ1
506  · rw [steinSolution_eq_steinSolG P G hG hΦ]
507    exact norm_iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG_le hΦ hγ1 j hj1 hj5 x
508
509end PLevel
510
511end SteinAux

513section GaussRot
514variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
515  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
516variable {γ : Measure B} [IsProbabilityMeasure γ]
517
518/-- centered Gaussian: the mean vanishes -/
519lemma gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) : γ[id] = 0 := by
520  have := hγ.1
521  have h : ∫ x, x ∂γ = 0 := by
522    refine SeparatingDual.eq_zero_of_forall_dual_eq_zero (R := ℝ) fun L => ?_
523    have h1 := IsGaussian.integral_dual (μ := γ) L
524    rw [← h1]; exact hγ.2 L
525  simpa using h
526
527/-- rotation invariance in the form needed for the Ornstein–Uhlenbeck semigroup -/
528lemma gaussian_comb_map (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) {a s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s)
529    (has : a ^ 2 + s ^ 2 = 1) :
530    (γ.prod γ).map (fun q : B × B => a • q.1 + s • q.2) = γ := by
531  have := hγ.1
532  obtain ⟨θ, hc, hsn⟩ : ∃ θ : ℝ, Real.cos θ = a ∧ Real.sin θ = s := by
533    refine ⟨Real.arccos a, Real.cos_arccos (by nlinarith) (by nlinarith), ?_⟩
534    rw [Real.sin_arccos]
535    have : 1 - a ^ 2 = s ^ 2 := by linarith
536    rw [this, Real.sqrt_sq hs]
537  have hrot := IsGaussian.map_rotation_eq_self (μ := γ) (gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hγ) θ
538  have e : (fun q : B × B => a • q.1 + s • q.2) = Prod.fst ∘ (ContinuousLinearMap.rotation θ) := by
539    ext q <;> simp [ContinuousLinearMap.rotation_apply, hc, hsn]
540  rw [e, ← Measure.map_map measurable_fst (ContinuousLinearMap.rotation θ).continuous.measurable, hrot]
541  simp
542
543
544
545/-- Gaussian rotation: the OU kernel preserves the Gaussian law, in integrated form. -/
546lemma gaussian_comb_integral (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) {a s : ℝ} (hs : 0 ≤ s)
547    (has : a ^ 2 + s ^ 2 = 1) {h : B → ℝ} (hc : Continuous h) {M : ℝ} (hM : ∀ z, |h z| ≤ M) :
548    ∫ y, ∫ x, h (a • x + s • y) ∂γ ∂γ = ∫ z, h z ∂γ := by
549  have hint : Integrable (fun q : B × B => h (a • q.1 + s • q.2)) (γ.prod γ) := by
550    refine Integrable.of_bound (C := M) ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => by
551      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact hM _)
552    exact (hc.comp (by fun_prop)).aestronglyMeasurable
553  rw [← integral_prod_symm (fun q : B × B => h (a • q.1 + s • q.2)) hint]
554  have hm : Measurable fun q : B × B => a • q.1 + s • q.2 := by fun_prop
555  have := integral_map (μ := γ.prod γ) hm.aemeasurable (f := h) hc.aestronglyMeasurable
556  rw [gaussian_comb_map hγ hs has] at this
557  exact this.symm
558
559
560variable {Φ : B → ℝ}
561
562lemma iter3_steinSolG_apply (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) (x : B)
563    (v : Fin 3 → B) :
564    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolG γ Φ) x v =
565      -∫ p, ouA p.1 ^ 3 * iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) v ∂(steinNu γ) := by
566  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG hΦ hγ1 3 (by norm_num) x, neg_apply]
567  congr 1
568  have hint : Integrable (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinG Φ p) x) (steinNu γ) :=
569    (steinG_bound_int hΦ 3 (by norm_num)).mono' (steinG_meas hΦ 3 (by norm_num) x)
570      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => steinG_bound hΦ 3 (by norm_num) (by norm_num) p x)
571  have := (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin 3 => B) ℝ v).integral_comp_comm hint
572  simp only [ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply_apply] at this
573  rw [← this]
574  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
575  show (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinG Φ p) x) v = _
576  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinG Φ hΦ.1 p 3 (by norm_num) (by norm_num) x]
577  simp
578
579lemma third_core (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ)
580    (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) (v : Fin 3 → B) :
581    ∫ x, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolG γ Φ) x v ∂γ =
582      -(1 / 3) * ∫ z, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ z v ∂γ := by
583  set h : B → ℝ := fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ z v with hh
584  have hc : Continuous h :=
585    (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin 3 => B) ℝ v).continuous.comp
586      (hΦ.1.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (m := 3) (by norm_num))
587  set M : ℝ := cbSup Φ 3 * ∏ i, ‖v i‖ with hM
588  have hbd : ∀ z, |h z| ≤ M := fun z => by
589    have := (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ z).le_opNorm v
590    rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
591    refine this.trans ?_
592    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ (by norm_num) z)
593      (Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => norm_nonneg _)
594  have hM0 : 0 ≤ M := (abs_nonneg _).trans (hbd 0)
595  let Fn : B → ℝ × B → ℝ := fun x p => ouA p.1 ^ 3 * h (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2)
596  have hbdint : Integrable (fun z : B × (ℝ × B) => M * Real.exp (-(3:ℝ) * z.2.1)) (γ.prod (steinNu γ)) := by
597    have h1 := ((integrable_exp_neg_mul 3 (by norm_num)).const_mul M).comp_fst γ
598    exact Integrable.comp_snd (f := fun p : ℝ × B => M * Real.exp (-(3:ℝ) * p.1)) h1 γ
599  have hint2 : Integrable (Function.uncurry Fn) (γ.prod (steinNu γ)) := by
600    refine hbdint.mono' ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
601    · have : Continuous (Function.uncurry Fn) := by
602        have := hc
603        simp only [Function.uncurry, Fn]
604        fun_prop
605      exact this.aestronglyMeasurable
606    · simp only [Function.uncurry, Fn]
607      rw [norm_mul, norm_pow, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (ouA_pos _), ouA_pow, Real.norm_eq_abs,
608        mul_comm]
609      exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hbd _) (Real.exp_pos _).le
610  calc ∫ x, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolG γ Φ) x v ∂γ
611      = ∫ x, -∫ p, Fn x p ∂(steinNu γ) ∂γ :=
612        integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => iter3_steinSolG_apply hΦ hγ1 x v)
613    _ = -∫ x, ∫ p, Fn x p ∂(steinNu γ) ∂γ := integral_neg _
614    _ = -∫ p, ∫ x, Fn x p ∂γ ∂(steinNu γ) := by rw [integral_integral_swap hint2]
615    _ = -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0:ℝ), ∫ y, ∫ x, Fn x (u, y) ∂γ ∂γ := by
616        congr 1
617        exact integral_prod (fun p : ℝ × B => ∫ x, Fn x p ∂γ) hint2.integral_prod_right
618    _ = -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0:ℝ), ouA u ^ 3 * ∫ z, h z ∂γ := by
619        congr 1
620        refine setIntegral_congr_fun measurableSet_Ioi fun u hu => ?_
621        have hu0 : 0 ≤ u := le_of_lt hu
622        simp only [Fn]
623        simp_rw [integral_const_mul]
624        congr 1
625        exact gaussian_comb_integral hγ (ouS_nonneg u) (ouA_sq_add_ouS_sq hu0) hc hbd
626    _ = -(1 / 3) * ∫ z, h z ∂γ := by
627        congr 1
628        rw [integral_mul_const]
629        have h3 : ∫ u in Set.Ioi (0:ℝ), ouA u ^ 3 = 1 / 3 := by
630          simp_rw [ouA_pow]
631          have := integral_exp_mul_Ioi (a := -(3:ℕ)) (by norm_num) 0
632          simp only [mul_zero, Real.exp_zero] at this
633          rw [this]; norm_num
634        rw [h3]; ring
635
636
637section PLevel3
638variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
639
640lemma stein_third' (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P) (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G))
641    (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (v : Fin 3 → B) :
642    ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolution P G Φ) (G ω) v ∂P =
643      -(1 / 3) * ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω) v ∂P := by
644  have := hGauss.1
645  have hγ1 := gaussian_norm_integrable P G hGauss
646  have hf := contDiff_steinSolG (γ := P.map G) hΦ hγ1
647  rw [steinSolution_eq_steinSolG P G hG hΦ]
648  have c1 : Continuous fun x => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolG (P.map G) Φ) x v :=
649    (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin 3 => B) ℝ v).continuous.comp
650      (hf.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (m := 3) (by norm_num))
651  have c2 : Continuous fun x => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ x v :=
652    (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin 3 => B) ℝ v).continuous.comp
653      (hΦ.1.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (m := 3) (by norm_num))
654  have e1 := integral_map hG c1.aestronglyMeasurable
655  have e2 := integral_map hG c2.aestronglyMeasurable
656  exact e1.symm.trans ((third_core hGauss hΦ hγ1 v).trans (by rw [e2]))
657
658end PLevel3
659
660end GaussRot

665/-! ### Gaussian integration by parts and the Stein equation (Lemma 7.2, `F:eq:Stein-eq`) -/

667section GaussIBP
668open ProbabilityTheory
669variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
670  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
671variable {γ : Measure B} [IsProbabilityMeasure γ]
672
673lemma gaussian_sqnorm_integrable (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) :
674    Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖ ^ 2) γ := by
675  haveI := hγ.1
676  have := (IsGaussian.memLp_id γ 2 (by norm_num)).integrable_norm_pow (by norm_num)
677  simpa using this
678
679lemma integral_fst_prod {g : B → ℝ} (hg : Integrable g γ) :
680    ∫ q : B × B, g q.1 ∂(γ.prod γ) = ∫ w, g w ∂γ := by
681  rw [integral_prod _ (hg.comp_fst γ)]
682  simp
683
684
685lemma gaussian_ibp (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Φ)
686    {M1 M2 : ℝ} (h1 : ∀ w, ‖fderiv ℝ Φ w‖ ≤ M1) (h2 : ∀ w, ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) w‖ ≤ M2)
687    (c : B) (s : ℝ) :
688    ∫ w, fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • w) w ∂γ =
689      s * ∫ w, ∫ z, fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • w) z z ∂γ ∂γ := by
690  have := hγ.1
691  have hM1 : 0 ≤ M1 := (norm_nonneg (fderiv ℝ Φ 0)).trans (h1 0)
692  have hM2 : 0 ≤ M2 := (norm_nonneg (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) 0)).trans (h2 0)
693  have hd1 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
694  have hd2 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ)) := by
695    have : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
696    exact this.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
697  have hdiff1 : Differentiable ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) := by
698    have : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
699    exact this.differentiable (by norm_num)
700  set μ := γ.prod γ with hμ
701  have hint1 : Integrable (fun w : B => ‖w‖) γ := (IsGaussian.integrable_id (μ := γ)).norm
702  have hint2 := gaussian_sqnorm_integrable hγ
703  -- the rotated integrand
704  let pt : ℝ → B × B → B := fun θ q => c + s • (Real.cos θ • q.1 + Real.sin θ • q.2)
705  let vec : ℝ → B × B → B := fun θ q => -(Real.sin θ) • q.1 + Real.cos θ • q.2
706  let F : ℝ → B × B → ℝ := fun θ q => fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q) (vec θ q)
707  let F' : ℝ → B × B → ℝ := fun θ q =>
708    fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q) (s • vec θ q) (vec θ q) +
709      fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q) (-(Real.cos θ • q.1 + Real.sin θ • q.2))
710  have hFcont : ∀ θ, Continuous (F θ) := fun θ => by
711    simp only [F, pt, vec]
712    exact hd1.comp (by fun_prop) |>.clm_apply (by fun_prop)
713  -- invariance
714  have hrotmeas : ∀ θ : ℝ, Measurable (ContinuousLinearMap.rotation θ : B × B → B × B) :=
715    fun θ => (ContinuousLinearMap.rotation θ).continuous.measurable
716  have hJ : ∀ θ, ∫ q, F θ q ∂μ = ∫ q, F 0 q ∂μ := by
717    intro θ
718    have hrot := IsGaussian.map_rotation_eq_self (μ := γ) (gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hγ) θ
719    have e : ∀ q : B × B, F θ q = F 0 (ContinuousLinearMap.rotation θ q) := by
720      intro q
721      simp [F, pt, vec, ContinuousLinearMap.rotation_apply]
722    have h := integral_map (μ := μ) (hrotmeas θ).aemeasurable (f := F 0)
723      (hFcont 0).aestronglyMeasurable
724    rw [hrot] at h
725    rw [h]
726    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall e)
727  -- pointwise derivative
728  have hpt : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), HasDerivAt (fun θ => pt θ q) (s • vec θ q) θ := by
729    intro θ q
730    have h1 := ((Real.hasDerivAt_cos θ).smul_const q.1).add ((Real.hasDerivAt_sin θ).smul_const q.2)
731    have h2 := (h1.const_smul s).const_add c
732    convert h2 using 1
733  have hvec : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), HasDerivAt (fun θ => vec θ q)
734      (-(Real.cos θ • q.1 + Real.sin θ • q.2)) θ := by
735    intro θ q
736    have h1 := ((Real.hasDerivAt_sin θ).neg.smul_const q.1).add ((Real.hasDerivAt_cos θ).smul_const q.2)
737    convert h1 using 1
738    module
739  have hderivF : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), HasDerivAt (fun θ => F θ q) (F' θ q) θ := by
740    intro θ q
741    have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun θ => fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q))
742        (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q) (s • vec θ q)) θ :=
743      (hdiff1 (pt θ q)).hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt θ (hpt θ q)
744    exact h1.clm_apply (hvec θ q)
745  have hn1 : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), ‖vec θ q‖ ≤ ‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖ := by
746    intro θ q
747    refine (norm_add_le _ _).trans ?_
748    simp only [norm_smul, norm_neg, Real.norm_eq_abs]
749    have a1 := Real.abs_sin_le_one θ
750    have a2 := Real.abs_cos_le_one θ
751    nlinarith [norm_nonneg q.1, norm_nonneg q.2]
752  have hn2 : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), ‖Real.cos θ • q.1 + Real.sin θ • q.2‖ ≤ ‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖ := by
753    intro θ q
754    refine (norm_add_le _ _).trans ?_
755    simp only [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs]
756    have a1 := Real.abs_sin_le_one θ
757    have a2 := Real.abs_cos_le_one θ
758    nlinarith [norm_nonneg q.1, norm_nonneg q.2]
759  set b : B × B → ℝ := fun q => M2 * |s| * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) ^ 2 + M1 * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) with hb
760  have hn1int : Integrable (fun q : B × B => ‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) μ :=
761    (hint1.comp_fst γ).add (hint1.comp_snd γ)
762  have hn2int : Integrable (fun q : B × B => (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) ^ 2) μ := by
763    have := ((hint2.comp_fst γ).add ((hint1.mul_prod hint1).const_mul 2)).add (hint2.comp_snd γ)
764    refine this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => ?_)
765    simp only [Pi.add_apply]
766    ring
767  have hbint : Integrable b μ := (hn2int.const_mul (M2 * |s|)).add (hn1int.const_mul M1)
768  have hFbound : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), ‖F θ q‖ ≤ M1 * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) := by
769    intro θ q
770    refine ((fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q)).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
771    exact mul_le_mul (h1 _) (hn1 θ q) (norm_nonneg _) hM1
772  have hF'bound : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (q : B × B), ‖F' θ q‖ ≤ b q := by
773    intro θ q
774    refine (norm_add_le _ _).trans ?_
775    have e1 : ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q) (s • vec θ q) (vec θ q)‖ ≤
776        M2 * |s| * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) ^ 2 := by
777      refine ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q) (s • vec θ q)).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
778      have : ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q) (s • vec θ q)‖ ≤ M2 * (|s| * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖)) := by
779        refine ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (pt θ q)).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
780        rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs]
781        exact mul_le_mul (h2 _) (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hn1 θ q) (abs_nonneg _)) (by positivity) hM2
782      calc _ ≤ M2 * (|s| * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖)) * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) :=
783            mul_le_mul this (hn1 θ q) (norm_nonneg _) (by positivity)
784        _ = _ := by ring
785    have e2 : ‖fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q) (-(Real.cos θ • q.1 + Real.sin θ • q.2))‖ ≤
786        M1 * (‖q.1‖ + ‖q.2‖) := by
787      refine ((fderiv ℝ Φ (pt θ q)).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
788      rw [norm_neg]
789      exact mul_le_mul (h1 _) (hn2 θ q) (norm_nonneg _) hM1
790    exact add_le_add e1 e2
791  have hF'cont : ∀ θ, Continuous (F' θ) := fun θ => by
792    simp only [F', pt, vec]
793    refine Continuous.add ?_ ?_
794    · exact (hd2.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop) |>.clm_apply (by fun_prop)
795    · exact (hd1.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop)
796  have hD := hasDerivAt_integral_of_dominated_loc_of_deriv_le (μ := μ) (F := F) (F' := F')
797    (x₀ := 0) (bound := b) (Metric.ball_mem_nhds _ one_pos)
798    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun θ => (hFcont θ).aestronglyMeasurable)
799    (((hn1int.const_mul M1)).mono' (hFcont 0).aestronglyMeasurable
800      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => hFbound 0 q))
801    (hF'cont 0).aestronglyMeasurable
802    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q θ _ => hF'bound θ q) hbint
803    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q θ _ => hderivF θ q)
804  have hconst : HasDerivAt (fun θ => ∫ q, F θ q ∂μ) 0 0 := by
805    have : (fun θ => ∫ q, F θ q ∂μ) = fun _ => ∫ q, F 0 q ∂μ := funext hJ
806    rw [this]; exact hasDerivAt_const _ _
807  have hzero : ∫ q, F' 0 q ∂μ = 0 := hD.2.unique hconst
808  -- evaluate at θ = 0
809  have hF'0 : ∀ q : B × B, F' 0 q = s * fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2 q.2 -
810      fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • q.1) q.1 := by
811    intro q
812    simp [F', pt, vec]
813    ring
814  have hAcont : Continuous fun q : B × B => fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2 q.2 :=
815    ((hd2.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop)
816  have hBcont : Continuous fun q : B × B => fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • q.1) q.1 :=
817    (hd1.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop)
818  have hAint : Integrable (fun q : B × B => fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2 q.2) μ := by
819    refine (hint2.comp_snd γ |>.const_mul M2).mono' hAcont.aestronglyMeasurable
820      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => ?_)
821    refine ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
822    have : ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2‖ ≤ M2 * ‖q.2‖ :=
823      ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1)).le_opNorm _).trans
824        (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (h2 _) (norm_nonneg _))
825    calc _ ≤ M2 * ‖q.2‖ * ‖q.2‖ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this (norm_nonneg _)
826      _ = _ := by ring
827  have hBint : Integrable (fun q : B × B => fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • q.1) q.1) μ := by
828    refine (hint1.comp_fst γ |>.const_mul M1).mono' hBcont.aestronglyMeasurable
829      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => ?_)
830    exact ((fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • q.1)).le_opNorm _).trans
831      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (h1 _) (norm_nonneg _))
832  have hBint' : Integrable (fun w : B => fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • w) w) γ := by
833    refine (hint1.const_mul M1).mono' ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun w => ?_)
834    · exact ((hd1.comp (by fun_prop : Continuous fun w : B => c + s • w)).clm_apply
835        continuous_id).aestronglyMeasurable
836    · exact ((fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • w)).le_opNorm _).trans
837        (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (h1 _) (norm_nonneg _))
838  have hz : s * ∫ q, fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (c + s • q.1) q.2 q.2 ∂μ -
839      ∫ q, fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • q.1) q.1 ∂μ = 0 := by
840    rw [← integral_const_mul, ← integral_sub (hAint.const_mul s) hBint]
841    rw [← hzero]
842    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun q => (hF'0 q).symm)
843  rw [integral_prod _ hAint] at hz
844  have hB := integral_fst_prod (γ := γ) (g := fun w => fderiv ℝ Φ (c + s • w) w) hBint'
845  linarith

848lemma ouS_pos {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) : 0 < ouS u := by
849  unfold ouS
850  refine Real.sqrt_pos.2 ?_
851  have : Real.exp (-2 * u) < 1 := by
852    have := Real.exp_lt_exp.2 (show -2 * u < 0 by linarith)
853    simpa using this
854  linarith
855
856lemma ouS_mono {u v : ℝ} (h : u ≤ v) : ouS u ≤ ouS v := by
857  unfold ouS
858  refine Real.sqrt_le_sqrt ?_
859  have : Real.exp (-2 * v) ≤ Real.exp (-2 * u) := Real.exp_le_exp.2 (by linarith)
860  linarith
861
862lemma hasDerivAt_ouA (u : ℝ) : HasDerivAt ouA (-ouA u) u := by
863  have := (hasDerivAt_id u).neg.exp
864  convert this using 1
865  · funext x; simp [ouA]
866  · simp [ouA]
867
868lemma hasDerivAt_ouS {u : ℝ} (hu : 0 < u) : HasDerivAt ouS (ouA u ^ 2 / ouS u) u := by
869  have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun u : ℝ => 1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) (2 * Real.exp (-2 * u)) u := by
870    have := (((hasDerivAt_id u).const_mul (-2)).exp).const_sub 1
871    convert this using 1
872    · rfl
873    · simp only [id]
874      ring_nf
875  have hne : 1 - Real.exp (-2 * u) ≠ 0 := by
876    have : Real.exp (-2 * u) < 1 := by
877      have := Real.exp_lt_exp.2 (show -2 * u < 0 by linarith)
878      simpa using this
879    linarith
880  have h2 := h1.sqrt hne
881  have hs : ouS u ≠ 0 := (ouS_pos hu).ne'
882  have e : ouS = fun y => √(1 - Real.exp (-2 * y)) := rfl
883  rw [e]
884  convert h2 using 1
885  rw [ouA_sq]
886  simp only
887  field_simp

889lemma norm_fderiv_le_cbSup {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (w : B) : ‖fderiv ℝ Φ w‖ ≤ cbSup Φ 1 := by
890  have := norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ (j := 1) (by norm_num) w
891  simpa using this
892
893lemma norm_fderiv2_le_cbSup {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (w : B) :
894    ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) w‖ ≤ cbSup Φ 2 := by
895  have := norm_iter_le_cbSup hΦ (j := 2) (by norm_num) w
896  rw [← norm_iteratedFDeriv_fderiv (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := Φ) (n := 1)] at this
897  simpa using this

902lemma hasDerivAt_stein_T (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) {u₀ : ℝ} (hu₀ : 0 < u₀) :
903    HasDerivAt (fun u => ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ)
904      (ouA u₀ ^ 2 * ∫ y', ∫ y, fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA u₀ • x + ouS u₀ • y') y y ∂γ ∂γ -
905        ouA u₀ * ∫ y', fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA u₀ • x + ouS u₀ • y') x ∂γ) u₀ := by
906  haveI := hγ.1
907  have hint1 : Integrable (fun w : B => ‖w‖) γ := (IsGaussian.integrable_id (μ := γ)).norm
908  have hcd : ContDiff ℝ 5 Φ := hΦ.1
909  have hd1 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hcd.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
910  have hdiffΦ : Differentiable ℝ Φ := hcd.differentiable (by norm_num)
911  set σ : ℝ := ouS (u₀ / 2) with hσ
912  have hσpos : 0 < σ := ouS_pos (by linarith)
913  let F : ℝ → B → ℝ := fun u y => Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y)
914  let F' : ℝ → B → ℝ := fun u y =>
915    fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ((-ouA u) • x + (ouA u ^ 2 / ouS u) • y)
916  have hFcont : ∀ u, Continuous (F u) := fun u => by
917    simp only [F]; exact hΦ.1.continuous.comp (by fun_prop)
918  have hF'cont : ∀ u, Continuous (F' u) := fun u => by
919    simp only [F']
920    exact (hd1.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply (by fun_prop)
921  set b : B → ℝ := fun y => cbSup Φ 1 * (‖x‖ + σ⁻¹ * ‖y‖) with hb
922  have hbint : Integrable b γ := ((integrable_const ‖x‖).add (hint1.const_mul σ⁻¹)).const_mul _
923  have hD := hasDerivAt_integral_of_dominated_loc_of_deriv_le (μ := γ) (F := F) (F' := F')
924    (x₀ := u₀) (bound := b) (Metric.ball_mem_nhds u₀ (half_pos hu₀))
925    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => (hFcont u).aestronglyMeasurable)
926    (integrable_cont_bdd' hΦ _ _ x)
927    (hF'cont u₀).aestronglyMeasurable
928    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y u hu => by
929      have hu' : u₀ / 2 < u := by
930        have := (Metric.mem_ball.1 hu)
931        rw [Real.dist_eq, abs_lt] at this
932        linarith [this.1]
933      have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
934      have hsu : σ ≤ ouS u := ouS_mono hu'.le
935      have hspos : 0 < ouS u := lt_of_lt_of_le hσpos hsu
936      have ha1 := ouA_le_one hu0.le
937      have ha0 := ouA_pos u
938      refine ((fderiv ℝ Φ _).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
939      refine mul_le_mul (norm_fderiv_le_cbSup hΦ _) ?_ (norm_nonneg _) (cbSup_nonneg hΦ (by norm_num))
940      refine (norm_add_le _ _).trans ?_
941      rw [norm_smul, norm_smul, norm_neg, Real.norm_eq_abs, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos ha0,
942        abs_of_pos (div_pos (pow_pos ha0 2) hspos)]
943      refine add_le_add (by nlinarith [norm_nonneg x]) ?_
944      refine mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right ?_ (norm_nonneg _)
945      rw [div_le_iff₀ hspos]
946      calc ouA u ^ 2 ≤ 1 := pow_le_one₀ ha0.le ha1
947        _ = σ⁻¹ * σ := by field_simp
948        _ ≤ σ⁻¹ * ouS u := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsu (inv_nonneg.2 hσpos.le))
949    hbint
950    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y u hu => by
951      have hu' : u₀ / 2 < u := by
952        have := (Metric.mem_ball.1 hu)
953        rw [Real.dist_eq, abs_lt] at this
954        linarith [this.1]
955      have hu0 : 0 < u := by linarith
956      have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun u => ouA u • x + ouS u • y)
957          ((-ouA u) • x + (ouA u ^ 2 / ouS u) • y) u :=
958        ((hasDerivAt_ouA u).smul_const x).add ((hasDerivAt_ouS hu0).smul_const y)
959      exact (hdiffΦ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y)).hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt u h1)
960  have hmain := hD.2
961  convert hmain using 1
962  set a := ouA u₀ with ha
963  set s := ouS u₀ with hs
964  have hspos : 0 < s := ouS_pos hu₀
965  have hibp := gaussian_ibp hγ (hcd.of_le (by norm_num : (2 : WithTop ℕ∞) ≤ 5))
966    (fun w => norm_fderiv_le_cbSup hΦ w) (fun w => norm_fderiv2_le_cbSup hΦ w) (a • x) s
967  have hcpt : Continuous fun y : B => a • x + s • y := by fun_prop
968  have hI1 : Integrable (fun y : B => fderiv ℝ Φ (a • x + s • y) x) γ := by
969    refine Integrable.of_bound (C := cbSup Φ 1 * ‖x‖) (hd1.comp hcpt |>.clm_apply continuous_const).aestronglyMeasurable
970      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
971    exact ((fderiv ℝ Φ _).le_opNorm _).trans
972      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_fderiv_le_cbSup hΦ _) (norm_nonneg _))
973  have hI3 : Integrable (fun y : B => fderiv ℝ Φ (a • x + s • y) y) γ := by
974    refine ((hint1.const_mul (cbSup Φ 1))).mono' (hd1.comp hcpt |>.clm_apply continuous_id).aestronglyMeasurable
975      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
976    exact ((fderiv ℝ Φ _).le_opNorm _).trans
977      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_fderiv_le_cbSup hΦ _) (norm_nonneg _))
978  have e : ∀ y : B, F' u₀ y = (-a) * fderiv ℝ Φ (a • x + s • y) x +
979      (a ^ 2 / s) * fderiv ℝ Φ (a • x + s • y) y := by
980    intro y
981    simp only [F']
982    rw [map_add, map_smul, map_smul, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul]
983  simp_rw [e]
984  rw [integral_add (hI1.const_mul _) (hI3.const_mul _), integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, hibp]
985  field_simp
986  ring

989lemma iter_steinSolG_apply (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ) (x : B)
990    (j : ℕ) (hj1 : 1 ≤ j) (hj : j ≤ 5) (v : Fin j → B) :
991    iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinSolG γ Φ) x v =
992      -∫ p, ouA p.1 ^ j * iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) v ∂(steinNu γ) := by
993  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinSolG hΦ hγ1 j hj x, neg_apply]
994  congr 1
995  have hint : Integrable (fun p => iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x) (steinNu γ) :=
996    (steinG_bound_int hΦ j hj1).mono' (steinG_meas hΦ j hj x)
997      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => steinG_bound hΦ j hj1 hj p x)
998  have := (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin j => B) ℝ v).integral_comp_comm hint
999  simp only [ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply_apply] at this
1000  rw [← this]
1001  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
1002  show (iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinG Φ p) x) v = _
1003  rw [iteratedFDeriv_steinG Φ hΦ.1 p j hj1 hj x]
1004  simp

1006lemma stein_D1_int (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1007    Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => ouA p.1 * fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) x) (steinNu γ) := by
1008  have hb : Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => (cbSup Φ 1 * ‖x‖) * Real.exp (-(1 : ℝ) * p.1)) (steinNu γ) :=
1009    ((integrable_exp_neg_mul 1 one_pos).const_mul _).comp_fst γ
1010  refine hb.mono' ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
1011  · have hd1 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.1.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
1012    exact (continuous_ouA.comp continuous_fst).mul
1013      ((hd1.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply continuous_const) |>.aestronglyMeasurable
1014  · rw [norm_mul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (ouA_pos _)]
1015    have h1 : ‖fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) x‖ ≤ cbSup Φ 1 * ‖x‖ :=
1016      ((fderiv ℝ Φ _).le_opNorm _).trans
1017        (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_fderiv_le_cbSup hΦ _) (norm_nonneg _))
1018    have : ouA p.1 = Real.exp (-(1 : ℝ) * p.1) := by simp [ouA]
1019    calc ouA p.1 * ‖fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) x‖
1020        ≤ ouA p.1 * (cbSup Φ 1 * ‖x‖) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (ouA_pos _).le
1021      _ = _ := by rw [this]; ring
1022
1023
1024lemma stein_D2_int (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1025    Integrable (fun z : B × (ℝ × B) => ouA z.2.1 ^ 2 *
1026      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA z.2.1 • x + ouS z.2.1 • z.2.2) z.1 z.1) (γ.prod (steinNu γ)) := by
1027  have hint2 := gaussian_sqnorm_integrable hγ
1028  have hg : Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => cbSup Φ 2 * Real.exp (-(2 : ℝ) * p.1)) (steinNu γ) :=
1029    ((integrable_exp_neg_mul 2 two_pos).const_mul _).comp_fst γ
1030  have hb := hint2.mul_prod hg
1031  refine hb.mono' ?_ (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
1032  · have hd1 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.1.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
1033    have hd2 : Continuous (fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ)) := by
1034      have : ContDiff ℝ 1 (fderiv ℝ Φ) := hΦ.1.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
1035      exact this.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)
1036    exact ((continuous_ouA.comp (continuous_fst.comp continuous_snd)).pow 2).mul
1037      (((hd2.comp (by fun_prop)).clm_apply continuous_fst).clm_apply continuous_fst)
1038      |>.aestronglyMeasurable
1039  · rw [norm_mul, Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_pos (pow_pos (ouA_pos _) 2)]
1040    have h1 : ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA z.2.1 • x + ouS z.2.1 • z.2.2) z.1 z.1‖ ≤
1041        cbSup Φ 2 * ‖z.1‖ ^ 2 := by
1042      refine ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) _ z.1).le_opNorm _).trans ?_
1043      have : ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA z.2.1 • x + ouS z.2.1 • z.2.2) z.1‖ ≤
1044          cbSup Φ 2 * ‖z.1‖ :=
1045        ((fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) _).le_opNorm _).trans
1046          (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_fderiv2_le_cbSup hΦ _) (norm_nonneg _))
1047      calc _ ≤ cbSup Φ 2 * ‖z.1‖ * ‖z.1‖ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this (norm_nonneg _)
1048        _ = _ := by ring
1049    have e : ouA z.2.1 ^ 2 = Real.exp (-(2 : ℝ) * z.2.1) := by rw [ouA_sq]
1050    calc ouA z.2.1 ^ 2 * ‖fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA z.2.1 • x + ouS z.2.1 • z.2.2) z.1 z.1‖
1051        ≤ ouA z.2.1 ^ 2 * (cbSup Φ 2 * ‖z.1‖ ^ 2) :=
1052          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left h1 (pow_pos (ouA_pos _) 2).le
1053      _ = _ := by rw [e]; ring
1054
1055
1056lemma stein_T_cont (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1057    Continuous fun u : ℝ => ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ := by
1058  refine continuous_of_dominated (bound := fun _ => cbSup Φ 0) (fun u => ?_) (fun u => ?_)
1059    (integrable_const _) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1060  · exact (integrable_cont_bdd' hΦ _ _ x).aestronglyMeasurable
1061  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
1062      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_le_cbSup hΦ _
1063  · exact hΦ.1.continuous.comp (by fun_prop)
1064
1065lemma stein_T_zero (x : B) : ∫ y, Φ (ouA 0 • x + ouS 0 • y) ∂γ = Φ x := by
1066  have h1 : ouA 0 = 1 := by simp [ouA]
1067  have h2 : ouS 0 = 0 := by simp [ouS]
1068  simp [h1, h2]
1069
1070lemma stein_T_tendsto (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1071    Tendsto (fun u : ℝ => ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ) atTop (𝓝 (∫ y, Φ y ∂γ)) := by
1072  refine tendsto_integral_filter_of_dominated_convergence (fun _ => cbSup Φ 0) ?_ ?_
1073    (integrable_const _) ?_
1074  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => (integrable_cont_bdd' hΦ _ _ x).aestronglyMeasurable
1075  · exact Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => by
1076      rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]; exact abs_le_cbSup hΦ _
1077  · refine Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_
1078    have hA : Tendsto ouA atTop (𝓝 0) := by
1079      unfold ouA
1080      exact Real.tendsto_exp_neg_atTop_nhds_zero
1081    have hS : Tendsto ouS atTop (𝓝 1) := by
1082      unfold ouS
1083      have : Tendsto (fun u : ℝ => Real.exp (-2 * u)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
1084        exact Real.tendsto_exp_atBot.comp (tendsto_id.const_mul_atTop_of_neg (by norm_num : (-2:ℝ) < 0))
1085      have h := (this.const_sub 1).sqrt
1086      simpa using h
1087    have h := (hΦ.1.continuous.tendsto ((0 : ℝ) • x + (1 : ℝ) • y)).comp
1088      ((hA.smul_const x).add (hS.smul_const y))
1089    simpa [Function.comp_def] using h
1090
1091
1092lemma stein_core (hγ : IsCenteredGaussian γ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1093    ∫ y, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (steinSolG γ Φ) x ![y, y] ∂γ - fderiv ℝ (steinSolG γ Φ) x x =
1094      Φ x - ∫ y, Φ y ∂γ := by
1095  haveI := hγ.1
1096  have hγ1 : Integrable (fun y : B => ‖y‖) γ := (IsGaussian.integrable_id (μ := γ)).norm
1097  set f := steinSolG γ Φ with hf
1098  set μ0 : Measure ℝ := volume.restrict (Set.Ioi (0:ℝ)) with hμ0
1099  -- first derivative
1100  have hDx : fderiv ℝ f x x =
1101      -∫ p, ouA p.1 * fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) x ∂(steinNu γ) := by
1102    have := iter_steinSolG_apply hΦ hγ1 x 1 le_rfl (by norm_num) ![x]
1103    simpa using this
1104  have hD2 : ∀ y : B, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 f x ![y, y] =
1105      -∫ p, ouA p.1 ^ 2 * fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) y y ∂(steinNu γ) := by
1106    intro y
1107    have := iter_steinSolG_apply hΦ hγ1 x 2 (by norm_num) (by norm_num) ![y, y]
1108    simpa [iteratedFDeriv_two_apply] using this
1109  have hint2 := stein_D2_int hγ hΦ x
1110  have hint1 := stein_D1_int (γ := γ) hΦ x
1111  -- the second-derivative term
1112  have hP2 : ∫ y, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 f x ![y, y] ∂γ =
1113      -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0:ℝ), ouA u ^ 2 * ∫ y', ∫ y,
1114        fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') y y ∂γ ∂γ := by
1115    simp_rw [hD2]
1116    rw [integral_neg, integral_integral_swap hint2]
1117    congr 1
1118    have hI : Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => ∫ y, ouA p.1 ^ 2 *
1119        fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) y y ∂γ) (steinNu γ) :=
1120      hint2.integral_prod_right
1121    rw [integral_prod _ hI]
1122    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => ?_)
1123    simp only
1124    rw [← integral_const_mul]
1125    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y' => ?_)
1126    simp only
1127    rw [integral_const_mul]
1128  have hP1 : fderiv ℝ f x x =
1129      -∫ u in Set.Ioi (0:ℝ), ouA u * ∫ y', fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') x ∂γ := by
1130    rw [hDx]
1131    congr 1
1132    rw [integral_prod _ hint1]
1133    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => ?_)
1134    simp only
1135    rw [integral_const_mul]
1136  -- integrability in u
1137  have hIU2 : Integrable (fun u : ℝ => ouA u ^ 2 * ∫ y', ∫ y,
1138      fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') y y ∂γ ∂γ) μ0 := by
1139    have h1 : Integrable (fun p : ℝ × B => ∫ y, ouA p.1 ^ 2 *
1140        fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA p.1 • x + ouS p.1 • p.2) y y ∂γ) (steinNu γ) :=
1141      hint2.integral_prod_right
1142    have h2 := h1.integral_prod_left
1143    refine h2.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => ?_)
1144    simp only
1145    rw [← integral_const_mul]
1146    refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y' => ?_)
1147    simp only
1148    rw [integral_const_mul]
1149  have hIU1 : Integrable (fun u : ℝ => ouA u * ∫ y', fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') x ∂γ) μ0 := by
1150    have h2 := hint1.integral_prod_left
1151    refine h2.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun u => ?_)
1152    simp only
1153    rw [integral_const_mul]
1154  -- FTC
1155  have hFTC := integral_Ioi_of_hasDerivAt_of_tendsto (a := (0:ℝ))
1156    (f := fun u : ℝ => ∫ y, Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y) ∂γ)
1157    (f' := fun u => ouA u ^ 2 * ∫ y', ∫ y,
1158        fderiv ℝ (fderiv ℝ Φ) (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') y y ∂γ ∂γ -
1159      ouA u * ∫ y', fderiv ℝ Φ (ouA u • x + ouS u • y') x ∂γ)
1160    (stein_T_cont hΦ x).continuousWithinAt
1161    (fun u hu => hasDerivAt_stein_T hγ hΦ x hu)
1162    (hIU2.sub hIU1) (stein_T_tendsto hΦ x)
1163  rw [stein_T_zero, integral_sub hIU2 hIU1] at hFTC
1164  rw [hP2, hP1]
1165  linarith
1166
1167end GaussIBP
1168
1169section Stein
1170
1171variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
1172  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
1173variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
1174
1175/-- Lemma 7.2 `M:F:lem:Stein` (lines 7774-7789), finiteness and derivative bounds
1176`F:eq:Stein-bounds`: for a centered (Radon) Gaussian `G` on a separable Banach space and
1177`Φ ∈ C_b^5(B)`, the Stein integral converges for each `x`, `f` is `C^5` with
1178`‖D^j f‖_∞ ≤ j⁻¹ ‖D^jΦ‖_∞` for `1 ≤ j ≤ 5`. -/
1179theorem stein_bounds (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P) (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G))
1180    (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) :
1181    (∀ x, IntegrableOn (fun u : ℝ =>
1182        ∫ ω, Φ (Real.exp (-u) • x + Real.sqrt (1 - Real.exp (-2 * u)) • G ω) ∂P -
1183          ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P) (Set.Ioi 0)) ∧
1184    ContDiff ℝ 5 (steinSolution P G Φ) ∧
1185    ∀ j, 1 ≤ j → j ≤ 5 → ∀ x,
1186      ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j (steinSolution P G Φ) x‖ ≤
1187        (j : ℝ)⁻¹ * ⨆ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖ :=
1188  stein_bounds' P G hG hGauss Φ hΦ
1189
1190/-- Lemma 7.2, the Stein equation `F:eq:Stein-eq`:
1191`⟨D²f(x), C_G⟩ - Df(x)[x] = Φ(x) - E[Φ(G)]`. -/
1192theorem stein_equation (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P) (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G))
1193    (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (x : B) :
1194    covarianceContraction P G (steinSolution P G Φ) x - fderiv ℝ (steinSolution P G Φ) x x =
1195      Φ x - ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P := by
1196  haveI := hGauss.1
1197  haveI : IsProbabilityMeasure (P.map G) := (Measure.isProbabilityMeasure_map_iff hG).2 inferInstance
1198  have hf := steinSolution_eq_steinSolG P G hG hΦ
1199  have hcore := stein_core (γ := P.map G) hGauss hΦ x
1200  have hfd : ContDiff ℝ 5 (steinSolG (P.map G) Φ) :=
1201    contDiff_steinSolG hΦ (gaussian_norm_integrable P G hGauss)
1202  have c : Continuous fun y : B => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (steinSolG (P.map G) Φ) x ![y, y] :=
1203    (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (steinSolG (P.map G) Φ) x).cont.comp (by fun_prop)
1204  have h1 := integral_map hG c.aestronglyMeasurable
1205  have h2 : ∫ ω, Φ (G ω) ∂P = ∫ y, Φ y ∂(P.map G) := by
1206    rw [integral_map hG hΦ.1.continuous.aestronglyMeasurable]
1207  rw [hf]
1208  unfold covarianceContraction
1209  rw [h1] at hcore
1210  rw [h2]
1211  exact hcore
1212
1213/-- Lemma 7.2, third-derivative identity `F:eq:Stein-third`:
1214`E[D³f(G)] = -⅓ E[D³Φ(G)]` (as continuous trilinear maps). -/
1215theorem stein_third_derivative (G : Ω → B) (hG : AEMeasurable G P)
1216    (hGauss : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G)) (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 5 Φ) (v : Fin 3 → B) :
1217    ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 (steinSolution P G Φ) (G ω) v ∂P =
1218      -(1 / 3) * ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω) v ∂P :=
1219  stein_third' P G hG hGauss hΦ v
1220
1221end Stein
1222
1223/-! ### Gaussian quadratic chaos (Proposition 7.18, first display; work package CHAOS-01) -/
1224
1225section Chaos
1226
1227variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
1228
1229/-- i.i.d. standard Gaussian coordinates `G_i`. -/
1230def IsStdGaussianSeq (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) : Prop :=
1231  ProbabilityTheory.iIndepFun G P ∧ ∀ i, law P (G i) = ProbabilityTheory.gaussianReal 0 1
1232
1233/-- Symmetric Hilbert–Schmidt matrix: `A_ij = A_ji`, `∑ A_ij² < ∞`. -/
1234def IsSymmetricHS (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) : Prop :=
1235  (∀ i j, A i j = A j i) ∧ Summable fun p : ℕ × ℕ => A p.1 p.2 ^ 2
1236
1237/-- Hilbert–Schmidt norm `‖A‖_HS`. -/
1238def hsNorm (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) : ℝ := Real.sqrt (∑' p : ℕ × ℕ, A p.1 p.2 ^ 2)
1239
1240/-- The finite sections `Q_n(A) = ½ ∑_{i,j<n} A_ij (G_i G_j - δ_ij)`. -/
1241def chaosSection (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (ω : Ω) : ℝ :=
1242  (1 / 2) * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n,
1243    A i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0)
1244
1245/-- `Q_n → Q` in `L²(P)`. -/
1246def TendstoL2 (Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (Q : Ω → ℝ) : Prop :=
1247  AEStronglyMeasurable Q P ∧
1248    Tendsto (fun n => ∫⁻ ω, ‖Qn n ω - Q ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0)
1249
1250open Classical in
1251/-- `Q(A)`, the centered second Gaussian chaos of `A`: the `L²` limit of the sections
1252(junk `0` if no limit exists; `chaos_tendsto` shows it exists for symmetric HS `A`). -/
1253def chaos (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) : Ω → ℝ :=
1254  if h : ∃ Q : Ω → ℝ, TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A G) Q then h.choose else 0
1255
1256end Chaos
1257
1258section ChaosAux
1259open ProbabilityTheory Matrix
1260
1261lemma frob_eig (N : ℕ) (a : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) (ha : a.IsHermitian) :
1262    ∑ k, ha.eigenvalues k ^ 2 = ∑ i, ∑ j, a i j ^ 2 := by
1263  have h1 : ∑ i, ∑ j, a i j ^ 2 = (a * a).trace := by
1264    have : a * a = aᴴ * a := by rw [ha.eq]
1265    rw [this, Matrix.trace]
1266    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, sq]
1267    rw [Finset.sum_comm]
1268  rw [h1]
1269  have hs := ha.spectral_theorem
1270  set U : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ := (ha.eigenvectorUnitary : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ)
1271  set D : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ := Matrix.diagonal (RCLike.ofReal ∘ ha.eigenvalues)
1272  have hU : star U * U = 1 := Unitary.coe_star_mul_self ha.eigenvectorUnitary
1273  have e : a = U * D * star U := by
1274    conv_lhs => rw [hs]
1275    rfl
1276  have : a * a = U * (D * D) * star U := by
1277    calc a * a = (U * D * star U) * (U * D * star U) := by rw [← e]
1278      _ = U * D * (star U * U) * D * star U := by simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
1279      _ = U * (D * D) * star U := by rw [hU]; simp [Matrix.mul_assoc]
1280  rw [this, Matrix.trace_mul_comm, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, hU, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.trace]
1281  simp [D, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal, sq]
1282
1283
1284lemma quad_eig (N : ℕ) (a : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) (ha : a.IsHermitian) :
1285    ∃ (b : OrthonormalBasis (Fin N) ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N))) (l : Fin N → ℝ),
1286      (∀ x : Fin N → ℝ, x ⬝ᵥ (a *ᵥ x) =
1287        ∑ k, l k * (inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x)) ^ 2) ∧
1288      (∑ k, l k = a.trace) ∧ (∑ k, l k ^ 2 = ∑ i, ∑ j, a i j ^ 2) := by
1289  refine ⟨ha.eigenvectorBasis, ha.eigenvalues, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
1290  · intro x
1291    set b := ha.eigenvectorBasis
1292    have hx : x = ∑ k, (inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x)) • ⇑(b k) := by
1293      have := b.sum_repr' (WithLp.toLp 2 x)
1294      have h2 := congrArg WithLp.ofLp this
1295      simpa using h2.symm
1296    have key : ∀ k, x ⬝ᵥ ⇑(b k) = inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x) := by
1297      intro k
1298      rw [EuclideanSpace.inner_eq_star_dotProduct]
1299      simp [dotProduct_comm]
1300    have h1 : a *ᵥ x = ∑ k, (inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x) * ha.eigenvalues k) • ⇑(b k) := by
1301      conv_lhs => rw [hx]
1302      simp only [Matrix.mulVec_sum, Matrix.mulVec_smul]
1303      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
1304      have := ha.mulVec_eigenvectorBasis k
1305      simp only [WithLp.ofLp] at this ⊢
1306      rw [this, smul_smul]
1307    rw [h1]
1308    simp only [dotProduct_sum, dotProduct_smul, key, smul_eq_mul]
1309    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => by ring
1310  · exact_mod_cast ha.trace_eq_sum_eigenvalues.symm
1311  · exact frob_eig N a ha
1312
1313
1314
