1316lemma map_pi_coord_eq (N : ℕ) (b : OrthonormalBasis (Fin N) ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N))) :
1317    (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)).map
1318      (fun x : Fin N → ℝ => fun k => inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x)) =
1319    Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1320  set γ := Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)
1321  set φ : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N) → (Fin N → ℝ) := fun v k => inner ℝ (b k) v with hφ
1322  have hφm : Measurable φ := by
1323    refine Measurable.of_eval fun k => ?_
1324    exact (continuous_const.inner continuous_id).measurable
1325  have h1 := map_pi_eq_stdGaussian (ι := Fin N)
1326  have h2 := stdGaussian_eq_map_pi_orthonormalBasis (E := EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin N)) b
1327  have e1 : (fun x : Fin N → ℝ => fun k => inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x)) = φ ∘ (WithLp.toLp 2) := rfl
1328  rw [e1, ← Measure.map_map hφm (by fun_prop), h1, h2, Measure.map_map hφm (by fun_prop)]
1329  have : φ ∘ (fun x : Fin N → ℝ => ∑ i, x i • b i) = id := by
1330    ext x k
1331    simp [hφ, inner_sum, inner_smul_right, b.inner_eq_ite]
1332  rw [this, Measure.map_id]
1333
1334
1335lemma pi_integral_eval (N : ℕ) (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : Continuous f) (k : Fin N) :
1336    ∫ y, f (y k) ∂(Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) =
1337      ∫ x, f x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1338  have h := (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) k).map_eq
1339  conv_rhs => rw [← h]
1340  exact (integral_map (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable hf.aestronglyMeasurable).symm
1341
1342lemma pi_integral_cross (N : ℕ) (f g : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : Continuous f) (hg : Continuous g) (k l : Fin N) (hkl : k ≠ l) :
1343    ∫ y, f (y k) * g (y l) ∂(Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) =
1344      (∫ x, f x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1)) * ∫ x, g x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1345  have hind := iIndepFun_pi (μ := fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) (X := fun _ => id)
1346    (fun _ => aemeasurable_id)
1347  have h2 := (hind.indepFun hkl)
1348  have := h2.integral_fun_comp_mul_comp (f := f) (g := g)
1349    (measurable_pi_apply k).aemeasurable (measurable_pi_apply l).aemeasurable (by simpa using hf.aestronglyMeasurable) (by simpa using hg.aestronglyMeasurable)
1350  rw [← pi_integral_eval N f hf k, ← pi_integral_eval N g hg l]
1351  exact this
1352
1353
1354lemma gauss_integrable_pow (n : ℕ) : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ n) (gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1355  have := (memLp_id_gaussianReal (μ := 0) (v := 1) n).integrable_norm_pow'
1356  rw [← integrable_norm_iff (by fun_prop)]
1357  simpa [norm_pow] using this
1358
1359lemma gauss_sq : ∫ x : ℝ, x ^ 2 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 1 := by
1360  have h := variance_id_gaussianReal (μ := 0) (v := 1)
1361  rw [variance_eq_integral (by fun_prop)] at h
1362  simpa using h
1363
1364lemma gauss_one : ∫ x : ℝ, x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 0 := by simp
1365
1366lemma gauss_sqm1 : ∫ x : ℝ, (x ^ 2 - 1) ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 0 := by
1367  rw [integral_sub (gauss_integrable_pow 2) (integrable_const _)]
1368  simp [gauss_sq]
1369
1370
1371/-- `v = E (g² - 1)²`. -/
1372def vGauss : ℝ := ∫ x : ℝ, (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1)
1373
1374lemma gauss_integrable_sqm1_sq : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2) (gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1375  have h : (fun x : ℝ => (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2) = fun x => x ^ 4 - 2 * x ^ 2 + 1 := by ext x; ring
1376  rw [h]
1377  exact ((gauss_integrable_pow 4).sub ((gauss_integrable_pow 2).const_mul 2)).add (integrable_const _)
1378
1379lemma pi_integrable_eval (N : ℕ) (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : Continuous f)
1380    (hfi : Integrable f (gaussianReal 0 1)) (k : Fin N) :
1381    Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => f (y k)) (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) := by
1382  have h := (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) k)
1383  exact h.integrable_comp_of_integrable hfi
1384
1385lemma pi_sqm1_orth (N : ℕ) (k m : Fin N) :
1386    ∫ y, (y k ^ 2 - 1) * (y m ^ 2 - 1) ∂(Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) =
1387      if k = m then vGauss else 0 := by
1388  by_cases h : k = m
1389  · subst h
1390    simp only [ite_true, vGauss]
1391    rw [← pi_integral_eval N (fun x => (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2) (by fun_prop) k]
1392    congr 1; ext y; ring
1393  · simp only [h, ite_false]
1394    rw [pi_integral_cross N (fun x => x ^ 2 - 1) (fun x => x ^ 2 - 1) (by fun_prop) (by fun_prop) k m h,
1395      gauss_sqm1]
1396    simp
1397
1398lemma pi_sqm1_integrable (N : ℕ) (k m : Fin N) :
1399    Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => (y k ^ 2 - 1) * (y m ^ 2 - 1))
1400      (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) := by
1401  have hi : ∀ k : Fin N, Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => (y k ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2)
1402      (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) := fun k =>
1403    pi_integrable_eval N (fun x => (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2) (by fun_prop) gauss_integrable_sqm1_sq k
1404  have hmeas : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => (y k ^ 2 - 1) * (y m ^ 2 - 1))
1405      (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) := by fun_prop
1406  refine Integrable.mono' (((hi k).add (hi m)).const_mul (1/2)) hmeas (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun y => ?_)
1407  simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, Pi.add_apply]
1408  rw [abs_mul]
1409  nlinarith [sq_nonneg (|y k ^ 2 - 1| - |y m ^ 2 - 1|), sq_abs (y k ^ 2 - 1), sq_abs (y m ^ 2 - 1),
1410    abs_nonneg (y k ^ 2 - 1), abs_nonneg (y m ^ 2 - 1)]
1411
1412
1413lemma quad_L2 (N : ℕ) (a : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ) (ha : a.IsHermitian) :
1414    ∫ z, (z ⬝ᵥ (a *ᵥ z) - a.trace) ^ 2 ∂(Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)) =
1415      vGauss * ∑ i, ∑ j, a i j ^ 2 := by
1416  obtain ⟨b, l, h1, h2, h3⟩ := quad_eig N a ha
1417  set γ := Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) with hγ
1418  set φ : (Fin N → ℝ) → (Fin N → ℝ) := fun x k => inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x) with hφ
1419  have hφm : Measurable φ := by
1420    refine Measurable.of_eval fun k => ?_
1421    exact (continuous_const.inner (by fun_prop : Continuous fun x : Fin N → ℝ => WithLp.toLp 2 x)).measurable
1422  set H : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ := fun y => (∑ k, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 2 with hH
1423  have hint : ∀ z : Fin N → ℝ, (z ⬝ᵥ (a *ᵥ z) - a.trace) ^ 2 = H (φ z) := by
1424    intro z
1425    simp only [hH, hφ]
1426    rw [h1 z, ← h2]
1427    congr 1
1428    rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
1429    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => by ring
1430  simp_rw [hint]
1431  have hHm : AEStronglyMeasurable H γ := by
1432    have : Continuous H := by simp only [hH]; fun_prop
1433    exact this.aestronglyMeasurable
1434  have hmap := map_pi_coord_eq N b
1435  have : ∫ z, H (φ z) ∂γ = ∫ y, H y ∂(γ.map φ) := (integral_map hφm.aemeasurable (by
1436    rw [show γ.map φ = γ from hmap]; exact hHm)).symm
1437  rw [this, show γ.map φ = γ from hmap]
1438  have hexp : ∀ y : Fin N → ℝ, H y = ∑ k, ∑ m, (l k * l m) * ((y k ^ 2 - 1) * (y m ^ 2 - 1)) := by
1439    intro y
1440    simp only [hH, sq, Finset.sum_mul_sum]
1441    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun m _ => by ring
1442  simp_rw [hexp]
1443  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun k _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun m _ => (pi_sqm1_integrable N k m).const_mul _)]
1444  have : ∀ k ∈ (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin N)), ∫ y, ∑ m, (l k * l m) * ((y k ^ 2 - 1) * (y m ^ 2 - 1)) ∂γ
1445      = l k ^ 2 * vGauss := by
1446    intro k _
1447    rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun m _ => (pi_sqm1_integrable N k m).const_mul _)]
1448    simp_rw [integral_const_mul, pi_sqm1_orth]
1449    simp [sq, mul_comm, mul_assoc, mul_left_comm]
1450  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl this, ← h3, Finset.mul_sum]
1451  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => by ring
1452
1453
1454variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
1455
1456lemma IsStdGaussianSeq.aemeasurable {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (i : ℕ) :
1457    AEMeasurable (G i) P := by
1458  by_contra h
1459  have := hG.2 i
1460  rw [law, Measure.map_of_not_aemeasurable_of_ne_zero h (IsProbabilityMeasure.ne_zero P)] at this
1461  have h2 : NullSingletonClass (gaussianReal 0 1) := nullSingletonClass_gaussianReal one_ne_zero
1462  have h1 : (gaussianReal 0 1) {Classical.ofNonempty} = 1 := by rw [← this]; simp
1463  rw [measure_singleton] at h1
1464  simp at h1
1465
1466lemma law_vec {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (N : ℕ) :
1467    P.map (fun ω (i : Fin N) => G i ω) = Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1468  have hind : iIndepFun (fun i : Fin N => G i) P := hG.1.precomp Fin.val_injective
1469  have := (iIndepFun_iff_map_fun_eq_pi_map (fun i : Fin N => hG.aemeasurable i)).1 hind
1470  rw [this]
1471  congr 1
1472  ext i : 1
1473  exact hG.2 i
1474
1475
1476/-- The finite quadratic form `∑_{i,j<N} c_ij (G_i G_j - δ_ij)`. -/
1477def quadSum (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (ω : Ω) : ℝ :=
1478  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, c i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0)
1479
1480lemma quadSum_eq (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (ω : Ω) :
1481    quadSum c G N ω =
1482      (fun i : Fin N => G i ω) ⬝ᵥ ((Matrix.of fun i j : Fin N => c i j) *ᵥ (fun i : Fin N => G i ω))
1483        - (Matrix.of fun i j : Fin N => c i j).trace := by
1484  unfold quadSum
1485  rw [Finset.sum_range]
1486  simp only [Finset.sum_range, Matrix.trace, Matrix.diag, Matrix.of_apply, dotProduct, Matrix.mulVec]
1487  simp only [mul_sub, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero]
1488  have : ∀ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (if (i : ℕ) = (j : ℕ) then c i j else 0) = c i i := by
1489    intro i
1490    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
1491    · simp
1492    · intro j _ hj; rw [if_neg (fun h => hj (Fin.ext h.symm))]
1493    · simp
1494  simp only [this]
1495  congr 1
1496  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
1497  rw [Finset.mul_sum]
1498  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => by ring
1499
1500lemma quadSum_L2 {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
1501    (hc : ∀ i j, c i j = c j i) (N : ℕ) :
1502    ∫ ω, quadSum c G N ω ^ 2 ∂P =
1503      vGauss * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, c i j ^ 2 := by
1504  set a : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ := Matrix.of fun i j : Fin N => c i j with ha
1505  have hH : a.IsHermitian := by
1506    ext i j
1507    simp [ha, Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, hc]
1508  have hZ : AEMeasurable (fun ω (i : Fin N) => G i ω) P :=
1509    AEMeasurable.of_eval fun i => hG.aemeasurable i
1510  have hF : Continuous (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => (z ⬝ᵥ (a *ᵥ z) - a.trace) ^ 2) := by
1511    unfold dotProduct Matrix.mulVec; fun_prop
1512  have h1 : ∫ ω, quadSum c G N ω ^ 2 ∂P =
1513      ∫ z, (z ⬝ᵥ (a *ᵥ z) - a.trace) ^ 2 ∂(P.map (fun ω (i : Fin N) => G i ω)) := by
1514    rw [integral_map hZ hF.aestronglyMeasurable]
1515    simp_rw [quadSum_eq c G N]
1516    rfl
1517  rw [h1, law_vec hG N, quad_L2 N a hH, Finset.sum_range]
1518  congr 1
1519  simp only [ha, Matrix.of_apply, Finset.sum_range]
1520
1521
1522lemma Lp2_norm_sq (g : Lp ℝ 2 P) : ‖g‖ ^ 2 = ∫ ω, (g ω) ^ 2 ∂P := by
1523  have h := real_inner_self_eq_norm_sq g
1524  rw [← h, L2.inner_def]
1525  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
1526  simp [sq]
1527
1528lemma gauss_pow_memLp {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (i : ℕ) (n : ℕ) :
1529    Integrable (fun ω => G i ω ^ n) P := by
1530  have h := gauss_integrable_pow n
1531  rw [← hG.2 i] at h
1532  exact (integrable_map_measure (continuous_pow n).aestronglyMeasurable (hG.aemeasurable i)).1 h
1533
1534lemma memLp_term {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (i j : ℕ) :
1535    MemLp (fun ω => G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) 2 P := by
1536  have hm : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => G i ω * G j ω) P :=
1537    ((hG.aemeasurable i).mul (hG.aemeasurable j)).aestronglyMeasurable
1538  have h1 : MemLp (fun ω => G i ω * G j ω) 2 P := by
1539    rw [memLp_two_iff_integrable_sq hm]
1540    refine Integrable.mono' (((gauss_pow_memLp hG i 4).add (gauss_pow_memLp hG j 4)).const_mul (1/2))
1541      (hm.pow 2) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
1542    simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, Pi.add_apply]
1543    rw [abs_of_nonneg (sq_nonneg _)]
1544    nlinarith [sq_nonneg (G i ω ^ 2 - G j ω ^ 2)]
1545  exact h1.sub (memLp_const _)
1546
1547lemma memLp_quadSum {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (N : ℕ) :
1548    MemLp (quadSum c G N) 2 P := by
1549  unfold quadSum
1550  have : (fun ω => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N,
1551      c i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0)) =
1552      ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N,
1553        fun ω => c i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) := by
1554    ext ω; simp
1555  rw [this]
1556  refine memLp_finsetSum' _ fun i _ => memLp_finsetSum' _ fun j _ => ?_
1557  exact (memLp_term hG i j).const_mul _
1558
1559
1560lemma sum_range_sq_ite (f : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (m n : ℕ) (hmn : m ≤ n) :
1561    ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n, (if i < m ∧ j < m then f i j else 0) =
1562      ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range m, f i j := by
1563  have h1 : ∀ i, i < m → ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n, (if i < m ∧ j < m then f i j else 0) =
1564      ∑ j ∈ Finset.range m, f i j := by
1565    intro i hi
1566    rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_mono hmn) (fun j _ hj => by
1567      simp only [Finset.mem_range, not_lt] at hj; simp [not_lt.2 hj])]
1568    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
1569    simp only [Finset.mem_range] at hj
1570    simp [hi, hj]
1571  rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_mono hmn) (fun i _ hi => by
1572    simp only [Finset.mem_range, not_lt] at hi
1573    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun j _ => by simp [not_lt.2 hi])]
1574  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => h1 i (Finset.mem_range.1 hi)
1575
1576/-- partial Hilbert–Schmidt sums over squares -/
1577def hsPartial (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (n : ℕ) : ℝ :=
1578  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n, A i j ^ 2
1579
1580lemma section_sub (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (m n : ℕ) (hmn : m ≤ n) (ω : Ω) :
1581    chaosSection A G n ω - chaosSection A G m ω =
1582      (1 / 2) * quadSum (fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then 0 else A i j) G n ω := by
1583  unfold chaosSection quadSum
1584  have h := sum_range_sq_ite (fun i j => A i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0)) m n hmn
1585  have e : ∀ i j : ℕ, (if i < m ∧ j < m then (0:ℝ) else A i j) * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0)
1586      = A i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) -
1587        (if i < m ∧ j < m then A i j * (G i ω * G j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) else 0) := by
1588    intro i j; split_ifs <;> simp
1589  simp_rw [e, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, h]
1590  ring
1591
1592lemma section_L2_diff {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
1593    (hA : ∀ i j, A i j = A j i) (m n : ℕ) (hmn : m ≤ n) :
1594    ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - chaosSection A G m ω) ^ 2 ∂P =
1595      vGauss / 4 * (hsPartial A n - hsPartial A m) := by
1596  have hc : ∀ i j, (fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then (0:ℝ) else A i j) i j =
1597      (fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then (0:ℝ) else A i j) j i := by
1598    intro i j; simp only [hA i j, and_comm]
1599  have h := quadSum_L2 hG (fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then 0 else A i j) hc n
1600  simp_rw [section_sub A G m n hmn, mul_pow]
1601  rw [integral_const_mul, h]
1602  have e : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n,
1603      ((fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then (0:ℝ) else A i j) i j) ^ 2 = hsPartial A n - hsPartial A m := by
1604    have h2 := sum_range_sq_ite (fun i j => A i j ^ 2) m n hmn
1605    have e2 : ∀ i j : ℕ, ((fun i j => if i < m ∧ j < m then (0:ℝ) else A i j) i j) ^ 2 =
1606        A i j ^ 2 - (if i < m ∧ j < m then A i j ^ 2 else 0) := by
1607      intro i j; simp only; split_ifs <;> simp
1608    simp_rw [e2, Finset.sum_sub_distrib, h2]
1609    rfl
1610  rw [e]
1611  ring
1612
1613
1614lemma hsPartial_tendsto (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (hs : Summable fun p : ℕ × ℕ => A p.1 p.2 ^ 2) :
1615    Tendsto (hsPartial A) atTop (𝓝 (∑' p : ℕ × ℕ, A p.1 p.2 ^ 2)) := by
1616  have h := hs.hasSum
1617  have hsq : Tendsto (fun n : ℕ => Finset.range n ×ˢ Finset.range n) atTop atTop := by
1618    rw [Filter.tendsto_atTop_atTop]
1619    intro s
1620    refine ⟨s.sup Prod.fst + s.sup Prod.snd + 1, fun n hn => ?_⟩
1621    intro p hp
1622    simp only [Finset.mem_product, Finset.mem_range]
1623    have h1 : p.1 ≤ s.sup Prod.fst := Finset.le_sup (f := Prod.fst) hp
1624    have h2 : p.2 ≤ s.sup Prod.snd := Finset.le_sup (f := Prod.snd) hp
1625    constructor <;> omega
1626  have := h.comp hsq
1627  convert this using 1
1628  ext n
1629  simp [hsPartial, Function.comp, Finset.sum_product]
1630
1631lemma section_L2_le {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
1632    (hA : ∀ i j, A i j = A j i) (m n : ℕ) :
1633    ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - chaosSection A G m ω) ^ 2 ∂P ≤
1634      vGauss / 4 * |hsPartial A n - hsPartial A m| := by
1635  have hv : 0 ≤ vGauss := integral_nonneg fun x => sq_nonneg _
1636  rcases le_total m n with h | h
1637  · rw [section_L2_diff hG A hA m n h]
1638    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_abs_self _) (by positivity)
1639  · have e : ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - chaosSection A G m ω) ^ 2 ∂P =
1640        ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G m ω - chaosSection A G n ω) ^ 2 ∂P := by
1641      congr 1; ext ω; ring
1642    rw [e, section_L2_diff hG A hA n m h, abs_sub_comm]
1643    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (le_abs_self _) (by positivity)
1644
1645lemma exists_chaos_limit {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
1646    (hA : IsSymmetricHS A) :
1647    ∃ Q : Ω → ℝ, MemLp Q 2 P ∧
1648      Tendsto (fun n => ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
1649  have hmem : ∀ n, MemLp (chaosSection A G n) 2 P := fun n => (memLp_quadSum hG A n).const_mul (1/2)
1650  set q : ℕ → Lp ℝ 2 P := fun n => (hmem n).toLp (chaosSection A G n) with hq
1651  have hv : 0 ≤ vGauss := integral_nonneg fun x => sq_nonneg _
1652  have hdist : ∀ m n, dist (q n) (q m) ^ 2 = ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - chaosSection A G m ω) ^ 2 ∂P := by
1653    intro m n
1654    rw [dist_eq_norm, Lp2_norm_sq]
1655    refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1656    have h1 := Lp.coeFn_sub (q n) (q m)
1657    have h2 := (hmem n).coeFn_toLp
1658    have h3 := (hmem m).coeFn_toLp
1659    filter_upwards [h1, h2, h3] with ω h1 h2 h3
1660    simp only [hq] at h1 h2 h3 ⊢
1661    rw [h1]; simp only [Pi.sub_apply]; rw [h2, h3]
1662  have hcau : CauchySeq q := by
1663    have hconv := (hsPartial_tendsto A hA.2).cauchySeq
1664    rw [Metric.cauchySeq_iff] at hconv ⊢
1665    intro ε hε
1666    obtain ⟨N, hN⟩ := hconv (ε ^ 2 / (vGauss / 4 + 1)) (by positivity)
1667    refine ⟨N, fun m hm n hn => ?_⟩
1668    have hb : dist (q m) (q n) ^ 2 < ε ^ 2 := by
1669      rw [hdist n m]
1670      have := hN m hm n hn
1671      rw [Real.dist_eq] at this
1672      calc _ ≤ vGauss / 4 * |hsPartial A m - hsPartial A n| := section_L2_le hG A hA.1 n m
1673        _ ≤ vGauss / 4 * (ε ^ 2 / (vGauss / 4 + 1)) :=
1674            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left this.le (by positivity)
1675        _ < ε ^ 2 := by
1676            rw [mul_div_assoc', div_lt_iff₀ (by positivity)]
1677            nlinarith [sq_nonneg ε, hε]
1678    exact lt_of_pow_lt_pow_left₀ 2 hε.le hb
1679  obtain ⟨qlim, hqlim⟩ := cauchySeq_tendsto_of_complete hcau
1680  refine ⟨qlim, Lp.memLp qlim, ?_⟩
1681  have hd : Tendsto (fun n => dist (q n) qlim ^ 2) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
1682    have := (tendsto_iff_dist_tendsto_zero.1 hqlim).pow 2
1683    simpa using this
1684  have hE : ∀ n, dist (q n) qlim ^ 2 = ∫ ω, (chaosSection A G n ω - qlim ω) ^ 2 ∂P := by
1685    intro n
1686    rw [dist_eq_norm, Lp2_norm_sq]
1687    refine integral_congr_ae ?_
1688    have h1 := Lp.coeFn_sub (q n) qlim
1689    have h2 := (hmem n).coeFn_toLp
1690    filter_upwards [h1, h2] with ω h1 h2
1691    simp only [hq] at h1 h2 ⊢
1692    rw [h1]; simp only [Pi.sub_apply]; rw [h2]
1693  simpa [hE] using hd
1694
1695/-- Bochner `L²` convergence of a `MemLp 2` limit gives the lintegral form. -/
1696lemma tendstoL2_of_memLp {Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} {Q : Ω → ℝ} (hQ : MemLp Q 2 P)
1697    (hmem : ∀ n, MemLp (Qn n) 2 P)
1698    (h : Tendsto (fun n => ∫ ω, (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0)) : TendstoL2 P Qn Q := by
1699  refine ⟨hQ.aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
1700  have e : ∀ n, ∫⁻ ω, ‖Qn n ω - Q ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P = ENNReal.ofReal (∫ ω, (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P) := by
1701    intro n
1702    have hi : Integrable (fun ω => (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2) P := ((hmem n).sub hQ).integrable_sq
1703    rw [ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal hi (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => sq_nonneg _)]
1704    refine lintegral_congr fun ω => ?_
1705    rw [← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _), Real.norm_eq_abs, sq_abs]
1706  simp_rw [e]
1707  simpa using ENNReal.tendsto_ofReal h
1708
1709end ChaosAux

1711section ChaosAux2
1712open ProbabilityTheory Matrix
1713variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P]

1715lemma lintegral_enorm_sq_eq {f : Ω → ℝ} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f P) :
1716    ∫⁻ ω, ‖f ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P = eLpNorm f 2 P ^ 2 := by
1717  rw [eLpNorm_eq_lintegral_rpow_enorm_toReal two_ne_zero ENNReal.ofNat_ne_top hf]
1718  simp only [ENNReal.toReal_ofNat]
1719  rw [← ENNReal.rpow_natCast, ← ENNReal.rpow_mul]
1720  norm_num
1721  
1722lemma TendstoL2.tendsto_eLpNorm {Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} {Q : Ω → ℝ}
1723    (hmeas : ∀ n, AEStronglyMeasurable (Qn n) P) (h : TendstoL2 P Qn Q) :
1724    Tendsto (fun n => eLpNorm (Qn n - Q) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
1725  have h2 := h.2
1726  have e : ∀ n, ∫⁻ ω, ‖Qn n ω - Q ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P = eLpNorm (Qn n - Q) 2 P ^ 2 :=
1727    fun n => lintegral_enorm_sq_eq ((hmeas n).sub h.1)
1728  simp_rw [e] at h2
1729  have hc : Continuous fun x : ℝ≥0∞ => x ^ (1 / 2 : ℝ) := ENNReal.continuous_rpow_const
1730  have := (hc.tendsto 0).comp h2
1731  simp only [Function.comp_def] at this
1732  rw [ENNReal.zero_rpow_of_pos (by norm_num)] at this
1733  refine this.congr fun n => ?_
1734  rw [← ENNReal.rpow_natCast, ← ENNReal.rpow_mul]
1735  norm_num
1736
1737lemma TendstoL2.ae_unique {Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} {Q Q' : Ω → ℝ}
1738    (hmeas : ∀ n, AEStronglyMeasurable (Qn n) P)
1739    (h : TendstoL2 P Qn Q) (h' : TendstoL2 P Qn Q') : Q =ᵐ[P] Q' := by
1740  have h1 := h.tendsto_eLpNorm hmeas
1741  have h2 := h'.tendsto_eLpNorm hmeas
1742  have hle : ∀ n, eLpNorm (Q - Q') 2 P ≤ eLpNorm (Qn n - Q) 2 P + eLpNorm (Qn n - Q') 2 P := by
1743    intro n
1744    have e : Q - Q' = -(Qn n - Q) + (Qn n - Q') := by abel
1745    rw [e]
1746    refine (eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)).trans ?_
1747    rw [eLpNorm_neg]
1748  have hz : eLpNorm (Q - Q') 2 P = 0 := by
1749    have ht := h1.add h2
1750    rw [add_zero] at ht
1751    exact le_antisymm (ge_of_tendsto' ht hle) bot_le
1752  have := (eLpNorm_eq_zero_iff (by norm_num : (2:ℝ≥0∞) ≠ 0)).1 hz
1753  filter_upwards [this] with ω hω
1754  simpa [sub_eq_zero] using hω
1755
1756
1757lemma sum_fin_dite {n m : ℕ} (hnm : n ≤ m) (f : Fin n → ℝ) :
1758    ∑ j : Fin m, (if hj : (j : ℕ) < n then f ⟨j, hj⟩ else 0) = ∑ j : Fin n, f j := by
1759  set F : ℕ → ℝ := fun j => if hj : j < n then f ⟨j, hj⟩ else 0 with hF
1760  have h1 : ∑ j : Fin m, (if hj : (j : ℕ) < n then f ⟨j, hj⟩ else 0) = ∑ j : Fin m, F j := rfl
1761  have h2 : ∑ j : Fin n, f j = ∑ j : Fin n, F j := by
1762    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
1763    simp [hF, j.2]
1764  rw [h1, h2, Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range F m, Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range F n]
1765  refine (Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_mono hnm) fun j _ hj => ?_).symm
1766  simp only [Finset.mem_range, not_lt] at hj
1767  simp [hF, not_lt.2 hj]
1768
1769/-- the block-orthogonal matrix `diag(U, I)` on `Fin m` -/
1770def blockU (n m : ℕ) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℝ :=
1771  Matrix.of fun i j => if hi : (i : ℕ) < n then (if hj : (j : ℕ) < n then U ⟨i, hi⟩ ⟨j, hj⟩ else 0)
1772    else (if i = j then 1 else 0)
1773
1774lemma blockU_row_ge {n m : ℕ} (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) {i : Fin m} (hi : ¬ (i : ℕ) < n)
1775    (k : Fin m) : blockU n m U i k = if i = k then 1 else 0 := by
1776  simp [blockU, hi]
1777
1778lemma blockU_col_ge {n m : ℕ} (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) {i : Fin m} (hi : ¬ (i : ℕ) < n)
1779    (k : Fin m) : blockU n m U k i = if k = i then 1 else 0 := by
1780  by_cases hk : (k : ℕ) < n
1781  · have : k ≠ i := fun h => hi (h ▸ hk)
1782    simp [blockU, hk, hi, this]
1783  · simp [blockU, hk]
1784
1785lemma blockU_row_lt {n m : ℕ} (hnm : n ≤ m) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) {i : Fin m}
1786    (hi : (i : ℕ) < n) (v : Fin m → ℝ) :
1787    ∑ k, blockU n m U i k * v k = ∑ k : Fin n, U ⟨i, hi⟩ k * v (Fin.castLE hnm k) := by
1788  rw [← sum_fin_dite hnm]
1789  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
1790  by_cases hk : (k : ℕ) < n
1791  · simp [blockU, hi, hk]
1792  · simp [blockU, hi, hk]
1793
1794lemma blockU_row_lt' {n m : ℕ} (hnm : n ≤ m) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) {i : Fin m}
1795    (hi : (i : ℕ) < n) (v : Fin m → ℝ) :
1796    ∑ k, v k * blockU n m U i k = ∑ k : Fin n, v (Fin.castLE hnm k) * U ⟨i, hi⟩ k := by
1797  simp_rw [mul_comm (v _)]
1798  exact blockU_row_lt hnm U hi v
1799
1800lemma blockU_col_lt {n m : ℕ} (hnm : n ≤ m) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ) {i : Fin m}
1801    (hi : (i : ℕ) < n) (v : Fin m → ℝ) :
1802    ∑ k, blockU n m U k i * v k = ∑ k : Fin n, U k ⟨i, hi⟩ * v (Fin.castLE hnm k) := by
1803  rw [← sum_fin_dite hnm]
1804  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
1805  by_cases hk : (k : ℕ) < n
1806  · simp [blockU, hi, hk]
1807  · have : k ≠ i := fun h => hk (h ▸ hi)
1808    simp [blockU, hk, this]
1809
1810lemma blockU_orth {n m : ℕ} (hnm : n ≤ m) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ)
1811    (hU : U ∈ Matrix.orthogonalGroup (Fin n) ℝ) :
1812    (blockU n m U)ᵀ * blockU n m U = 1 := by
1813  have hU' : Uᵀ * U = 1 := (Matrix.mem_orthogonalGroup_iff' (Fin n) ℝ).1 hU
1814  ext i j
1815  rw [Matrix.mul_apply]
1816  simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply]
1817  by_cases hi : (i : ℕ) < n
1818  · rw [blockU_col_lt hnm U hi]
1819    by_cases hj : (j : ℕ) < n
1820    · have := congrFun (congrFun hU' ⟨i, hi⟩) ⟨j, hj⟩
1821      rw [Matrix.mul_apply] at this
1822      simp only [Matrix.transpose_apply] at this
1823      rw [Matrix.one_apply]
1824      have e : ∀ k : Fin n, blockU n m U (Fin.castLE hnm k) j = U k ⟨j, hj⟩ := by
1825        intro k; simp [blockU, k.2, hj]
1826      simp_rw [e, this, Matrix.one_apply]
1827      simp [Fin.ext_iff]
1828    · have e : ∀ k : Fin n, blockU n m U (Fin.castLE hnm k) j = 0 := by
1829        intro k; simp [blockU, k.2, hj]
1830      simp_rw [e, mul_zero, Finset.sum_const_zero]
1831      rw [Matrix.one_apply, if_neg]
1832      intro h; exact hj (h ▸ hi)
1833  · simp_rw [blockU_col_ge U hi]
1834    simp only [ite_mul, one_mul, zero_mul, Finset.sum_ite_eq', Finset.mem_univ, if_true]
1835    by_cases hj : (j : ℕ) < n
1836    · have hij : i ≠ j := fun h => hi (by rw [h]; exact hj)
1837      rw [Matrix.one_apply, if_neg hij]
1838      simp [blockU, hi, hij]
1839    · rw [blockU_row_ge U hi, Matrix.one_apply]
1840
1841
1842lemma section_rot (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ)
1843    (hU : U ∈ Matrix.orthogonalGroup (Fin n) ℝ) (G' : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (A' : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
1844    (hG' : ∀ i ω, G' i ω = if h : i < n then ∑ j : Fin n, U ⟨i, h⟩ j * G j ω else G i ω)
1845    (hA' : ∀ i j, A' i j = if hi : i < n then (if hj : j < n then
1846        ∑ k : Fin n, ∑ l : Fin n, U ⟨i, hi⟩ k * A k l * U ⟨j, hj⟩ l else
1847        ∑ k : Fin n, U ⟨i, hi⟩ k * A k j)
1848      else (if hj : j < n then ∑ l : Fin n, A i l * U ⟨j, hj⟩ l else A i j))
1849    (m : ℕ) (hnm : n ≤ m) (ω : Ω) :
1850    chaosSection A' G' m ω = chaosSection A G m ω := by
1851  have e1 : ∀ (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (H : ℕ → Ω → ℝ), chaosSection c H m ω = 1 / 2 * quadSum c H m ω :=
1852    fun _ _ => rfl
1853  rw [e1, e1, quadSum_eq, quadSum_eq]
1854  set V := blockU n m U with hV
1855  have hVV := blockU_orth hnm U hU
1856  set g : Fin m → ℝ := fun i => G i ω with hg
1857  set a : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) ℝ := Matrix.of fun i j : Fin m => A i j with ha
1858  have hg' : (fun i : Fin m => G' i ω) = V *ᵥ g := by
1859    ext i
1860    simp only [Matrix.mulVec, dotProduct]
1861    rw [hG']
1862    by_cases hi : (i : ℕ) < n
1863    · rw [dif_pos hi, hV, blockU_row_lt hnm U hi]
1864      simp [hg]
1865    · rw [dif_neg hi, hV]
1866      simp_rw [blockU_row_ge U hi]
1867      simp [hg]
1868  have ha' : (Matrix.of fun i j : Fin m => A' i j) = V * a * Vᵀ := by
1869    ext i j
1870    simp only [Matrix.of_apply, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply]
1871    rw [hA']
1872    by_cases hj : (j : ℕ) < n
1873    · rw [hV, blockU_row_lt' hnm U hj (fun l => ∑ k, blockU n m U i k * a k l)]
1874      by_cases hi : (i : ℕ) < n
1875      · rw [dif_pos hi, dif_pos hj]
1876        simp_rw [blockU_row_lt hnm U hi, Finset.sum_mul]
1877        rw [Finset.sum_comm]
1878        simp [ha]
1879      · rw [dif_neg hi, dif_pos hj]
1880        simp_rw [blockU_row_ge U hi]
1881        simp [ha]
1882    · rw [hV]
1883      simp_rw [blockU_row_ge U hj]
1884      simp only [mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero, Finset.sum_ite_eq, Finset.mem_univ, if_true]
1885      by_cases hi : (i : ℕ) < n
1886      · rw [dif_pos hi, dif_neg hj, blockU_row_lt hnm U hi]
1887        simp [ha]
1888      · rw [dif_neg hi, dif_neg hj]
1889        simp_rw [blockU_row_ge U hi]
1890        simp [ha]
1891  rw [hg', ha']
1892  have h1 : (V * a * Vᵀ) *ᵥ (V *ᵥ g) = V *ᵥ (a *ᵥ g) := by
1893    rw [mulVec_mulVec, Matrix.mul_assoc (V * a), hVV, Matrix.mul_one, ← mulVec_mulVec]
1894  have h2 : ∀ w : Fin m → ℝ, (V *ᵥ g) ⬝ᵥ (V *ᵥ w) = g ⬝ᵥ w := by
1895    intro w
1896    rw [dotProduct_mulVec, ← mulVec_transpose, mulVec_mulVec, hVV, one_mulVec]
1897  have h3 : trace (V * a * Vᵀ) = trace a := by
1898    rw [trace_mul_comm, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, hVV, Matrix.one_mul]
1899  rw [h1, h2, h3]
1900

1902lemma int_pow_gq (k : ℕ) : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ k * Real.exp (-(1/2) * x ^ 2)) := by
1903  have := integrable_rpow_mul_exp_neg_mul_sq (b := 1/2) (by norm_num) (s := (k : ℝ))
1904    (by have := Nat.cast_nonneg (α := ℝ) k; linarith)
1905  simpa [Real.rpow_natCast] using this

1907lemma gauss_moment_eq (k : ℕ) : ∫ x, x ^ k ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) =
1908    (Real.sqrt (2 * Real.pi))⁻¹ * ∫ x : ℝ, x ^ k * Real.exp (-(1/2) * x ^ 2) := by
1909  rw [integral_gaussianReal_eq_integral_smul one_ne_zero, ← integral_const_mul]
1910  refine integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
1911  simp only [gaussianPDFReal, smul_eq_mul, NNReal.coe_one, mul_one, sub_zero]
1912  have : -x ^ 2 / 2 = -(1/2) * x ^ 2 := by ring
1913  rw [this]; ring
1914
1915lemma gauss_moment_succ_succ (n : ℕ) :
1916    ∫ x, x ^ (n + 2) ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = (n + 1) * ∫ x, x ^ n ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) := by
1917  rw [gauss_moment_eq, gauss_moment_eq]
1918  set q : ℝ → ℝ := fun x => Real.exp (-(1/2) * x ^ 2) with hq
1919  have hv : ∀ x, HasDerivAt (fun x => -q x) (x * q x) x := by
1920    intro x
1921    have h := ((hasDerivAt_pow 2 x).const_mul (-(1/2) : ℝ)).exp.neg
1922    convert h using 1
1923    simp only [hq]; push_cast; ring
1924  have hu : ∀ x, HasDerivAt (fun x : ℝ => x ^ (n + 1)) (((n : ℝ) + 1) * x ^ n) x := by
1925    intro x
1926    have := hasDerivAt_pow (n + 1) x
1927    convert this using 1
1928    push_cast; ring
1929  have key := integral_mul_deriv_eq_deriv_mul_of_integrable (u := fun x : ℝ => x ^ (n + 1))
1930    (v := fun x => -q x) (u' := fun x => ((n : ℝ) + 1) * x ^ n) (v' := fun x => x * q x)
1931    (fun x _ => hu x) (fun x _ => hv x)
1932    ((int_pow_gq (n + 2)).congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only [Pi.mul_apply, hq]; ring))
1933    (((int_pow_gq n).const_mul (-((n : ℝ) + 1))).congr
1934      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only [Pi.mul_apply, hq]; ring))
1935    (((int_pow_gq (n + 1)).neg).congr
1936      (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only [Pi.mul_apply, Pi.neg_apply, hq]; ring))
1937  have e1 : ∫ x, x ^ (n + 2) * q x = ∫ x, x ^ (n + 1) * (x * q x) :=
1938    integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by ring)
1939  have e2 : ∫ x, ((n : ℝ) + 1) * x ^ n * -q x = -(((n : ℝ) + 1) * ∫ x, x ^ n * q x) := by
1940    rw [← integral_const_mul, ← integral_neg]
1941    exact integral_congr_ae (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by ring)
1942  rw [e1, key, e2]
1943  ring
1944
1945lemma gauss_m4 : ∫ x, x ^ 4 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 3 := by
1946  rw [gauss_moment_succ_succ 2, gauss_sq]; norm_num
1947lemma gauss_m6 : ∫ x, x ^ 6 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 15 := by
1948  rw [gauss_moment_succ_succ 4, gauss_m4]; norm_num
1949lemma gauss_m8 : ∫ x, x ^ 8 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 105 := by
1950  rw [gauss_moment_succ_succ 6, gauss_m6]; norm_num
1951
1952lemma vGauss_eq : vGauss = 2 := by
1953  unfold vGauss
1954  have e : ∀ x : ℝ, (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 2 = x ^ 4 - 2 * x ^ 2 + 1 := fun x => by ring
1955  simp_rw [e]
1956  have i4 := gauss_integrable_pow 4
1957  have i2 := gauss_integrable_pow 2
1958  have j1 : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ 4 - 2 * x ^ 2) (gaussianReal 0 1) := i4.sub (i2.const_mul 2)
1959  rw [integral_add j1 (integrable_const _), integral_sub i4 (i2.const_mul 2),
1960    integral_const_mul, gauss_m4, gauss_sq]
1961  simp; norm_num
1962
1963lemma gauss_sqm1_four : ∫ x, (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 4 ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = 60 := by
1964  have e : ∀ x : ℝ, (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 4 = x ^ 8 - 4 * x ^ 6 + 6 * x ^ 4 - 4 * x ^ 2 + 1 :=
1965    fun x => by ring
1966  simp_rw [e]
1967  have i8 := gauss_integrable_pow 8
1968  have i6 := gauss_integrable_pow 6
1969  have i4 := gauss_integrable_pow 4
1970  have i2 := gauss_integrable_pow 2
1971  have j1 : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ 8 - 4 * x ^ 6) (gaussianReal 0 1) := i8.sub (i6.const_mul 4)
1972  have j2 : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ 8 - 4 * x ^ 6 + 6 * x ^ 4) (gaussianReal 0 1) :=
1973    j1.add (i4.const_mul 6)
1974  have j3 : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => x ^ 8 - 4 * x ^ 6 + 6 * x ^ 4 - 4 * x ^ 2) (gaussianReal 0 1) :=
1975    j2.sub (i2.const_mul 4)
1976  rw [integral_add j3 (integrable_const _), integral_sub j2 (i2.const_mul 4),
1977    integral_add j1 (i4.const_mul 6), integral_sub i8 (i6.const_mul 4),
1978    integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, gauss_m8, gauss_m6, gauss_m4, gauss_sq]
1979  simp; norm_num
1980

1982lemma abs_pow_le_one_add_pow_four (x : ℝ) {k : ℕ} (hk : k ≤ 4) : |x| ^ k ≤ 1 + x ^ 4 := by
1983  have h4 : x ^ 4 = |x| ^ 4 := by
1984    rw [pow_abs]; exact (abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)).symm
1985  rw [h4]
1986  rcases le_total |x| 1 with h | h
1987  · have : |x| ^ k ≤ 1 := pow_le_one₀ (abs_nonneg x) h
1988    have : 0 ≤ |x| ^ 4 := by positivity
1989    linarith
1990  · have : |x| ^ k ≤ |x| ^ 4 := pow_le_pow_right₀ h hk
1991    linarith

1993section Indep4
1994variable {Ω' : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω'] {μ : Measure Ω'} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
1995
1996lemma integrable_pow_of_four {X : Ω' → ℝ} (hX : AEStronglyMeasurable X μ)
1997    (h4 : Integrable (fun ω => X ω ^ 4) μ) {k : ℕ} (hk : k ≤ 4) :
1998    Integrable (fun ω => X ω ^ k) μ := by
1999  refine ((integrable_const 1).add h4).mono' (hX.pow k) (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
2000  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_pow]
2001  exact abs_pow_le_one_add_pow_four _ hk
2002
2003lemma indep_moments {X Y : Ω' → ℝ} (hXY : IndepFun X Y μ) (hXm : Measurable X) (hYm : Measurable Y)
2004    (hX4 : Integrable (fun ω => X ω ^ 4) μ) (hY4 : Integrable (fun ω => Y ω ^ 4) μ)
2005    (hX0 : ∫ ω, X ω ∂μ = 0) (hY0 : ∫ ω, Y ω ∂μ = 0) :
2006    Integrable (fun ω => (X ω + Y ω) ^ 4) μ ∧
2007    ∫ ω, (X ω + Y ω) ^ 2 ∂μ = ∫ ω, X ω ^ 2 ∂μ + ∫ ω, Y ω ^ 2 ∂μ ∧
2008    ∫ ω, (X ω + Y ω) ^ 4 ∂μ =
2009      ∫ ω, X ω ^ 4 ∂μ + 6 * (∫ ω, X ω ^ 2 ∂μ) * (∫ ω, Y ω ^ 2 ∂μ) + ∫ ω, Y ω ^ 4 ∂μ := by
2010  have iX : ∀ k ≤ 4, Integrable (fun ω => X ω ^ k) μ := fun k hk =>
2011    integrable_pow_of_four hXm.aestronglyMeasurable hX4 hk
2012  have iY : ∀ k ≤ 4, Integrable (fun ω => Y ω ^ k) μ := fun k hk =>
2013    integrable_pow_of_four hYm.aestronglyMeasurable hY4 hk
2014  have ind : ∀ a b : ℕ, IndepFun (fun ω => X ω ^ a) (fun ω => Y ω ^ b) μ := fun a b =>
2015    hXY.comp (measurable_id.pow_const a) (measurable_id.pow_const b)
2016  have iXY : ∀ a b : ℕ, a ≤ 4 → b ≤ 4 → Integrable (fun ω => X ω ^ a * Y ω ^ b) μ :=
2017    fun a b ha hb => (ind a b).integrable_mul (iX a ha) (iY b hb)
2018  have eXY : ∀ a b : ℕ, ∫ ω, X ω ^ a * Y ω ^ b ∂μ = (∫ ω, X ω ^ a ∂μ) * ∫ ω, Y ω ^ b ∂μ :=
2019    fun a b => (ind a b).integral_fun_mul_eq_mul_integral ((hXm.pow_const a).aestronglyMeasurable)
2020      ((hYm.pow_const b).aestronglyMeasurable)
2021  have hX1 : ∫ ω, X ω ^ 1 ∂μ = 0 := by simpa using hX0
2022  have hY1 : ∫ ω, Y ω ^ 1 ∂μ = 0 := by simpa using hY0
2023  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
2024  · have := (((((iX 4 le_rfl).add ((iXY 3 1 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 4)).add
2025      ((iXY 2 2 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 6)).add
2026      ((iXY 1 3 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 4)).add (iY 4 le_rfl))
2027    refine this.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
2028    simp only [Pi.add_apply]; ring
2029  · have e : ∀ ω, (X ω + Y ω) ^ 2 = X ω ^ 2 + (2 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 1) + Y ω ^ 2) :=
2030      fun ω => by ring
2031    simp_rw [e]
2032    have a1 : Integrable (fun ω => 2 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 1)) μ :=
2033      (iXY 1 1 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 2
2034    have a2 : Integrable (fun ω => 2 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 1) + Y ω ^ 2) μ := a1.add (iY 2 (by norm_num))
2035    rw [integral_add (iX 2 (by norm_num)) a2, integral_add a1 (iY 2 (by norm_num)),
2036      integral_const_mul, eXY, hX1]
2037    ring
2038  · have e : ∀ ω, (X ω + Y ω) ^ 4 = X ω ^ 4 + (4 * (X ω ^ 3 * Y ω ^ 1) +
2039        (6 * (X ω ^ 2 * Y ω ^ 2) + (4 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 3) + Y ω ^ 4))) := fun ω => by ring
2040    simp_rw [e]
2041    have i31 : Integrable (fun ω => 4 * (X ω ^ 3 * Y ω ^ 1)) μ :=
2042      (iXY 3 1 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 4
2043    have i22 : Integrable (fun ω => 6 * (X ω ^ 2 * Y ω ^ 2)) μ :=
2044      (iXY 2 2 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 6
2045    have i13 : Integrable (fun ω => 4 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 3)) μ :=
2046      (iXY 1 3 (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).const_mul 4
2047    have i4 := iY 4 le_rfl
2048    have j1 : Integrable (fun ω => 4 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 3) + Y ω ^ 4) μ := i13.add i4
2049    have j2 : Integrable (fun ω => 6 * (X ω ^ 2 * Y ω ^ 2) + (4 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 3) + Y ω ^ 4)) μ :=
2050      i22.add j1
2051    have j3 : Integrable (fun ω => 4 * (X ω ^ 3 * Y ω ^ 1) +
2052        (6 * (X ω ^ 2 * Y ω ^ 2) + (4 * (X ω ^ 1 * Y ω ^ 3) + Y ω ^ 4))) μ := i31.add j2
2053    rw [integral_add (iX 4 le_rfl) j3, integral_add i31 j2, integral_add i22 j1,
2054      integral_add i13 i4, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul, integral_const_mul,
2055      eXY, eXY, eXY, hX1, hY1]
2056    ring
2057
2058end Indep4

2061lemma gauss_integrable_sqm1_four : Integrable (fun x : ℝ => (x ^ 2 - 1) ^ 4) (gaussianReal 0 1) := by
2062  have h := ((((gauss_integrable_pow 8).sub ((gauss_integrable_pow 6).const_mul 4)).add
2063    ((gauss_integrable_pow 4).const_mul 6)).sub ((gauss_integrable_pow 2).const_mul 4)).add
2064    (integrable_const (1 : ℝ))
2065  refine h.congr (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2066  simp only [Pi.add_apply, Pi.sub_apply]; ring

2068lemma pi_fourth_aux (N : ℕ) (l : Fin N → ℝ) (s : Finset (Fin N)) :
2069    let γN := Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1)
2070    Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => (∑ k ∈ s, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4) γN ∧
2071    ∫ y, ∑ k ∈ s, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1) ∂γN = 0 ∧
2072    ∫ y, (∑ k ∈ s, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 2 ∂γN = 2 * ∑ k ∈ s, l k ^ 2 ∧
2073    ∫ y, (∑ k ∈ s, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4 ∂γN ≤ 60 * (∑ k ∈ s, l k ^ 2) ^ 2 := by
2074  intro γN
2075  set f : Fin N → (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ := fun j y => l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1) with hf
2076  have hfm : ∀ j, Measurable (f j) := fun j => by simp only [hf]; fun_prop
2077  have hind : iIndepFun f γN :=
2078    iIndepFun_pi (μ := fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) (X := fun j (x : ℝ) => l j * (x ^ 2 - 1))
2079      (fun j => by fun_prop)
2080  induction s using Finset.induction_on with
2081  | empty => simp
2082  | insert k s hk ih =>
2083    obtain ⟨ih4, ih0, ih2, ih4le⟩ := ih
2084    have hS : Measurable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => ∑ j ∈ s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1)) := by fun_prop
2085    have hT : Measurable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) := by fun_prop
2086    have hI : IndepFun (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => ∑ j ∈ s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1))
2087        (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) γN := by
2088      have := hind.indepFun_finsetSum_of_notMem hfm hk
2089      have e : (∑ j ∈ s, f j) = fun y : Fin N → ℝ => ∑ j ∈ s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1) := by
2090        ext y; simp [hf, Finset.sum_apply]
2091      rw [e] at this
2092      exact this
2093    have hT4 : Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => (l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4) γN := by
2094      have := pi_integrable_eval N (fun x => (l k * (x ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4) (by fun_prop)
2095        ((gauss_integrable_sqm1_four.const_mul (l k ^ 4)).congr
2096          (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp only; ring)) k
2097      exact this
2098    have hT0 : ∫ y, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1) ∂γN = 0 := by
2099      rw [pi_integral_eval N (fun x => l k * (x ^ 2 - 1)) (by fun_prop) k, integral_const_mul,
2100        gauss_sqm1, mul_zero]
2101    have hT2 : ∫ y, (l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 2 ∂γN = 2 * l k ^ 2 := by
2102      rw [pi_integral_eval N (fun x => (l k * (x ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 2) (by fun_prop) k]
2103      simp_rw [mul_pow]
2104      rw [integral_const_mul]
2105      have := vGauss_eq
2106      unfold vGauss at this
2107      rw [this]; ring
2108    have hT4' : ∫ y, (l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4 ∂γN = 60 * l k ^ 4 := by
2109      rw [pi_integral_eval N (fun x => (l k * (x ^ 2 - 1)) ^ 4) (by fun_prop) k]
2110      simp_rw [mul_pow]
2111      rw [integral_const_mul, gauss_sqm1_four]; ring
2112    obtain ⟨m4, m2, m4e⟩ := indep_moments hI hS hT ih4 hT4 ih0 hT0
2113    have e : ∀ y : Fin N → ℝ, ∑ j ∈ insert k s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1) =
2114        (∑ j ∈ s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1)) + l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1) := by
2115      intro y; rw [Finset.sum_insert hk, add_comm]
2116    simp_rw [e, Finset.sum_insert hk]
2117    have hSi : Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => ∑ j ∈ s, l j * (y j ^ 2 - 1)) γN := by
2118      simpa using integrable_pow_of_four hS.aestronglyMeasurable ih4 (k := 1) (by norm_num)
2119    have hTi : Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ => l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1)) γN := by
2120      simpa using integrable_pow_of_four hT.aestronglyMeasurable hT4 (k := 1) (by norm_num)
2121    refine ⟨m4, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
2122    · rw [integral_add hSi hTi, ih0, hT0]; simp
2123    · rw [m2, ih2, hT2]; ring
2124    · rw [m4e, ih2, hT2, hT4']
2125      have hs0 : 0 ≤ ∑ j ∈ s, l j ^ 2 := Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => sq_nonneg _
2126      nlinarith [sq_nonneg (l k), pow_two_nonneg (l k ^ 2), mul_nonneg hs0 (sq_nonneg (l k))]

2129lemma quadSum_transfer {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
2130    (hc : ∀ i j, c i j = c j i) (N : ℕ) :
2131    ∃ l : Fin N → ℝ, ∑ k, l k ^ 2 = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, c i j ^ 2 ∧
2132      ∀ F : ℝ → ℝ, Continuous F →
2133        (∫ ω, F (quadSum c G N ω) ∂P = ∫ y, F (∑ k, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1))
2134            ∂(Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1))) ∧
2135        (Integrable (fun ω => F (quadSum c G N ω)) P ↔ Integrable (fun y : Fin N → ℝ =>
2136            F (∑ k, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1))) (Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1))) := by
2137  set a : Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) ℝ := Matrix.of fun i j : Fin N => c i j with ha
2138  have hH : a.IsHermitian := by
2139    ext i j
2140    simp [ha, Matrix.conjTranspose_apply, hc]
2141  obtain ⟨b, l, h1, h2, h3⟩ := quad_eig N a hH
2142  set γ := Measure.pi (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) with hγ
2143  set φ : (Fin N → ℝ) → (Fin N → ℝ) := fun x k => inner ℝ (b k) (WithLp.toLp 2 x) with hφ
2144  have hφm : Measurable φ := by
2145    refine Measurable.of_eval fun k => ?_
2146    exact (continuous_const.inner (by fun_prop : Continuous fun x : Fin N → ℝ => WithLp.toLp 2 x)).measurable
2147  have hmp : MeasurePreserving φ γ γ := ⟨hφm, map_pi_coord_eq N b⟩
2148  set Z : Ω → (Fin N → ℝ) := fun ω i => G i ω with hZdef
2149  have hZ : AEMeasurable Z P := AEMeasurable.of_eval fun i => hG.aemeasurable i
2150  have hlaw : P.map Z = γ := law_vec hG N
2151  set D : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ := fun y => ∑ k, l k * (y k ^ 2 - 1) with hD
2152  have hDc : Continuous D := by simp only [hD]; fun_prop
2153  have hφc : Continuous φ := by
2154    refine continuous_pi fun k => ?_
2155    exact continuous_const.inner (by fun_prop : Continuous fun x : Fin N → ℝ => WithLp.toLp 2 x)
2156  have hpt : ∀ ω, quadSum c G N ω = D (φ (Z ω)) := by
2157    intro ω
2158    rw [quadSum_eq]
2159    simp only [hD, hφ, hZdef]
2160    rw [h1, ← h2, ← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
2161    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => by ring
2162  have h3' : ∑ k, l k ^ 2 = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range N, c i j ^ 2 := by
2163    rw [h3, Finset.sum_range]
2164    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
2165    rw [Finset.sum_range]
2166    simp [ha]
2167  refine ⟨l, h3', fun F hF => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
2168  · simp_rw [hpt]
2169    have hc1 : Continuous fun z => F (D (φ z)) := hF.comp (hDc.comp hφc)
2170    have e0 : (∫ ω, F (D (φ (Z ω))) ∂P) = ∫ z, F (D (φ z)) ∂(P.map Z) :=
2171      (integral_map hZ (f := fun z => F (D (φ z))) (by rw [hlaw]; exact hc1.aestronglyMeasurable)).symm
2172    rw [e0, hlaw]
2173    have := integral_map hmp.measurable.aemeasurable (μ := γ) (f := fun y => F (D y))
2174      (by rw [hmp.map_eq]; exact (hF.comp hDc).aestronglyMeasurable)
2175    rw [hmp.map_eq] at this
2176    exact this.symm
2177  · simp_rw [hpt]
2178    have hc1 : Continuous fun z => F (D (φ z)) := hF.comp (hDc.comp hφc)
2179    have e1 := integrable_map_measure (μ := P) (f := Z) (g := fun z => F (D (φ z)))
2180      (by rw [hlaw]; exact hc1.aestronglyMeasurable) hZ
2181    rw [hlaw] at e1
2182    rw [← Function.comp_def (fun z => F (D (φ z))) Z, ← e1]
2183    exact hmp.integrable_comp (g := fun y => F (D y)) (hF.comp hDc).aestronglyMeasurable

2186lemma quadSum_mean {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)
2187    (hc : ∀ i j, c i j = c j i) (N : ℕ) : ∫ ω, quadSum c G N ω ∂P = 0 := by

2194lemma quadSum_four {G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ)

2206lemma sq_integral_le_integral_sq {f : Ω → ℝ} (hf : MemLp f 2 P) :
2207    (∫ ω, f ω ∂P) ^ 2 ≤ ∫ ω, f ω ^ 2 ∂P := by

2213lemma L2_limit_moments {Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} {Q : Ω → ℝ} (hQ : MemLp Q 2 P)
2214    (hmem : ∀ n, MemLp (Qn n) 2 P)
2215    (h : Tendsto (fun n => ∫ ω, (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
2216    Tendsto (fun n => ∫ ω, Qn n ω ∂P) atTop (𝓝 (∫ ω, Q ω ∂P)) ∧
2217    Tendsto (fun n => ∫ ω, Qn n ω ^ 2 ∂P) atTop (𝓝 (∫ ω, Q ω ^ 2 ∂P)) := by
2218  have hs : Tendsto (fun n => Real.sqrt (∫ ω, (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
2219    have := (Real.continuous_sqrt.tendsto 0).comp h
2220    simp only [Function.comp_def, Real.sqrt_zero] at this
2221    exact this
2222  constructor
2223  · rw [tendsto_iff_dist_tendsto_zero]
2224    refine squeeze_zero (fun n => dist_nonneg) (fun n => ?_) hs
2225    rw [Real.dist_eq, ← integral_sub ((hmem n).integrable one_le_two) (hQ.integrable one_le_two)]
2226    exact Real.abs_le_sqrt (sq_integral_le_integral_sq ((hmem n).sub hQ))
2227  · set q : ℕ → Lp ℝ 2 P := fun n => (hmem n).toLp (Qn n) with hq
2228    set q' : Lp ℝ 2 P := hQ.toLp Q with hq'
2229    have hn : ∀ n, ‖q n‖ ^ 2 = ∫ ω, Qn n ω ^ 2 ∂P := by
2230      intro n
2231      rw [Lp2_norm_sq]
2232      refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2233      filter_upwards [(hmem n).coeFn_toLp] with ω hω
2234      simp only [hq]; rw [hω]
2235    have hn' : ‖q'‖ ^ 2 = ∫ ω, Q ω ^ 2 ∂P := by
2236      rw [Lp2_norm_sq]
2237      refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2238      filter_upwards [hQ.coeFn_toLp] with ω hω
2239      simp only [hq']; rw [hω]
2240    have hd : ∀ n, ‖q n - q'‖ = Real.sqrt (∫ ω, (Qn n ω - Q ω) ^ 2 ∂P) := by
2241      intro n
2242      rw [← Real.sqrt_sq (norm_nonneg _), Lp2_norm_sq]
2243      congr 1
2244      refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2245      filter_upwards [Lp.coeFn_sub (q n) q', (hmem n).coeFn_toLp, hQ.coeFn_toLp] with ω h1 h2 h3
2246      simp only [hq, hq'] at h1 h2 h3 ⊢
2247      rw [h1]; simp only [Pi.sub_apply]; rw [h2, h3]
2248    have hconv : Tendsto q atTop (𝓝 q') := by
2249      rw [tendsto_iff_norm_sub_tendsto_zero]
2250      simp_rw [hd]; exact hs
2251    have := ((continuous_norm.tendsto q').comp hconv).pow 2
2252    simp only [Function.comp_def] at this
2253    simp_rw [hn, hn'] at this
2254    exact this

2256lemma fatou_four {Qn : ℕ → Ω → ℝ} {Q : Ω → ℝ} (hmeas : ∀ n, AEStronglyMeasurable (Qn n) P)
2257    (hT : TendstoL2 P Qn Q) {C : ℝ}
2258    (hC : ∀ n, Integrable (fun ω => Qn n ω ^ 4) P ∧ ∫ ω, Qn n ω ^ 4 ∂P ≤ C) :
2259    ∫ ω, Q ω ^ 4 ∂P ≤ C := by
2260  have hin := tendstoInMeasure_of_tendsto_eLpNorm (p := 2) two_ne_zero (hT.tendsto_eLpNorm hmeas)
2261  obtain ⟨ns, -, hae⟩ := hin.exists_seq_tendsto_ae
2262  have hC0 : 0 ≤ C := (integral_nonneg fun ω => by positivity).trans (hC 0).2
2263  have hc : Continuous fun x : ℝ => ENNReal.ofReal (x ^ 4) :=
2264    ENNReal.continuous_ofReal.comp (continuous_pow 4)
2265  have key : ∫⁻ ω, ENNReal.ofReal (Q ω ^ 4) ∂P ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
2266    calc ∫⁻ ω, ENNReal.ofReal (Q ω ^ 4) ∂P
2267        = ∫⁻ ω, liminf (fun i => ENNReal.ofReal (Qn (ns i) ω ^ 4)) atTop ∂P := by
2268          refine lintegral_congr_ae ?_
2269          filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω
2270          exact (((hc.tendsto _).comp hω).liminf_eq).symm
2271      _ ≤ liminf (fun i => ∫⁻ ω, ENNReal.ofReal (Qn (ns i) ω ^ 4) ∂P) atTop :=
2272          lintegral_liminf_le' fun i => ((hmeas _).aemeasurable.pow_const 4).ennreal_ofReal
2273      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C := by
2274          refine liminf_le_of_frequently_le' (Frequently.of_forall fun i => ?_)
2275          rw [← ofReal_integral_eq_lintegral_ofReal (hC _).1
2276            (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by positivity)]
2277          exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hC _).2
2278  rw [integral_eq_lintegral_of_nonneg_ae (f := fun ω => Q ω ^ 4)
2279    (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun ω => by positivity)
2280    (hT.1.pow 4 : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Q ω ^ 4) P)]
2281  exact ENNReal.toReal_le_of_le_ofReal hC0 key
2282
2283end ChaosAux2
2284
2285section Chaos
2286
2287variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
2288
2289/-- Proposition 7.18, display `E:eq:chaos-isometry` (lines 9784-9788), existence part:
2290the sections converge in `L²`; the limit is basis-independent (see `chaos_basis_independent`). -/
2291theorem chaos_tendsto (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (hA : IsSymmetricHS A) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
2292    (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) :
2293    TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A G) (chaos P A G) := by
2294  obtain ⟨Q, hQmem, hQ⟩ := exists_chaos_limit hG A hA
2295  have hmem : ∀ n, MemLp (chaosSection A G n) 2 P := fun n => (memLp_quadSum hG A n).const_mul (1/2)
2296  have h : ∃ Q : Ω → ℝ, TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A G) Q := ⟨Q, tendstoL2_of_memLp hQmem hmem hQ⟩
2297  unfold chaos
2298  rw [dif_pos h]
2299  exact h.choose_spec
2300
2301/-- Proposition 7.18, `E:eq:chaos-isometry`: `E[Q(A)] = 0`, `Var Q(A) = ½ ‖A‖²_HS`,
2302`E[Q(A)⁴] ≤ 15 Var(Q(A))²`. -/
2303theorem chaos_moments (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (hA : IsSymmetricHS A) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
2304    (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) :
2305    ∫ ω, chaos P A G ω ∂P = 0 ∧
2306    ProbabilityTheory.variance (chaos P A G) P = (1 / 2) * hsNorm A ^ 2 ∧
2307    ∫ ω, chaos P A G ω ^ 4 ∂P ≤ 15 * (ProbabilityTheory.variance (chaos P A G) P) ^ 2 := by
2308  obtain ⟨Q', hQmem, hQ⟩ := exists_chaos_limit hG A hA
2309  have hmem : ∀ n, MemLp (chaosSection A G n) 2 P := fun n =>
2310    (memLp_quadSum hG A n).const_mul (1/2)
2311  have hmeas : ∀ n, AEStronglyMeasurable (chaosSection A G n) P := fun n =>
2312    (hmem n).aestronglyMeasurable
2313  have hT' : TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A G) Q' := tendstoL2_of_memLp hQmem hmem hQ
2314  have hT := chaos_tendsto P A hA G hG
2315  have hae : chaos P A G =ᵐ[P] Q' := TendstoL2.ae_unique hmeas hT hT'
2316  have hsec : ∀ n ω, chaosSection A G n ω = 1 / 2 * quadSum A G n ω := fun _ _ => rfl
2317  set S := ∑' p : ℕ × ℕ, A p.1 p.2 ^ 2 with hS
2318  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := tsum_nonneg fun _ => sq_nonneg _
2319  have hpS : ∀ n, 0 ≤ hsPartial A n ∧ hsPartial A n ≤ S := by
2320    intro n
2321    have e : hsPartial A n = ∑ p ∈ Finset.range n ×ˢ Finset.range n, A p.1 p.2 ^ 2 := by
2322      rw [Finset.sum_product]; rfl
2323    refine ⟨Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => sq_nonneg _, ?_⟩
2324    rw [e]
2325    exact hA.2.sum_le_tsum _ (fun _ _ => sq_nonneg _)
2326  -- moments of the sections
2327  have hm0 : ∀ n, ∫ ω, chaosSection A G n ω ∂P = 0 := by
2328    intro n
2329    simp_rw [hsec]
2330    rw [integral_const_mul, quadSum_mean hG A hA.1, mul_zero]
2331  have hm2 : ∀ n, ∫ ω, chaosSection A G n ω ^ 2 ∂P = 1 / 2 * hsPartial A n := by
2332    intro n
2333    simp_rw [hsec, mul_pow]
2334    rw [integral_const_mul, quadSum_L2 hG A hA.1 n, vGauss_eq]
2335    unfold hsPartial; ring
2336  obtain ⟨hl1, hl2⟩ := L2_limit_moments hQmem hmem hQ
2337  have hQ0 : ∫ ω, Q' ω ∂P = 0 :=
2338    tendsto_nhds_unique hl1 (by simp_rw [hm0]; exact tendsto_const_nhds)
2339  have hQ2 : ∫ ω, Q' ω ^ 2 ∂P = 1 / 2 * S := by
2340    refine tendsto_nhds_unique hl2 ?_
2341    simp_rw [hm2]
2342    exact (hsPartial_tendsto A hA.2).const_mul _
2343  have hc0 : ∫ ω, chaos P A G ω ∂P = 0 := by rw [integral_congr_ae hae, hQ0]
2344  have hvar : ProbabilityTheory.variance (chaos P A G) P = 1 / 2 * S := by
2345    rw [ProbabilityTheory.variance_of_integral_eq_zero hT.1.aemeasurable hc0, ← hQ2]
2346    refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2347    filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω
2348    rw [hω]
2349  have hhs : hsNorm A ^ 2 = S := Real.sq_sqrt hS0
2350  refine ⟨hc0, by rw [hvar, hhs], ?_⟩
2351  rw [hvar]
2352  refine fatou_four hmeas hT fun n => ?_
2353  obtain ⟨hi4, hle4⟩ := quadSum_four hG A hA.1 n
2354  have e4 : ∀ ω, chaosSection A G n ω ^ 4 = 1 / 16 * quadSum A G n ω ^ 4 := fun ω => by
2355    rw [hsec]; ring
2356  simp_rw [e4]
2357  refine ⟨hi4.const_mul _, ?_⟩
2358  rw [integral_const_mul]
2359  have h1 := hpS n
2360  have hp : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n, A i j ^ 2) = hsPartial A n := rfl
2361  rw [hp] at hle4
2362  have : hsPartial A n ^ 2 ≤ S ^ 2 := pow_le_pow_left₀ h1.1 h1.2 2
2363  nlinarith
2364
2365/-- Proposition 7.18, basis independence: for an orthogonal change of coordinates `U`
2366(`U Uᵀ = I` on `ℓ²`), `Q(UᵀAU)` computed from `G' = U G` has the same law as `Q(A)` from `G`.
2367Stated with `U` an orthogonal matrix on finitely many coordinates extended by the identity. -/
2368theorem chaos_basis_independent (A : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ) (hA : IsSymmetricHS A) (G : ℕ → Ω → ℝ)
2369    (hG : IsStdGaussianSeq P G) (n : ℕ) (U : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) ℝ)
2370    (hU : U ∈ Matrix.orthogonalGroup (Fin n) ℝ) :
2371    let G' : ℕ → Ω → ℝ := fun i ω =>
2372      if h : i < n then ∑ j : Fin n, U ⟨i, h⟩ j * G j ω else G i ω
2373    let A' : ℕ → ℕ → ℝ := fun i j =>
2374      if hi : i < n then (if hj : j < n then
2375        ∑ k : Fin n, ∑ l : Fin n, U ⟨i, hi⟩ k * A k l * U ⟨j, hj⟩ l else
2376        ∑ k : Fin n, U ⟨i, hi⟩ k * A k j)
2377      else (if hj : j < n then ∑ l : Fin n, A i l * U ⟨j, hj⟩ l else A i j)
2378    chaos P A' G' =ᵐ[P] chaos P A G := by
2379  intro G' A'
2380  have hsec : ∀ m, n ≤ m → chaosSection A' G' m = chaosSection A G m := fun m hm =>
2381    funext fun ω => section_rot A G n U hU G' A' (fun _ _ => rfl) (fun _ _ => rfl) m hm ω
2382  have hev : ∀ (Q : Ω → ℝ), (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖chaosSection A' G' m ω - Q ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P) =ᶠ[atTop]
2383      (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖chaosSection A G m ω - Q ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P) := fun Q =>
2384    Filter.eventually_atTop.2 ⟨n, fun m hm => by simp only [hsec m hm]⟩
2385  have hT := chaos_tendsto P A hA G hG
2386  have hT' : TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A' G') (chaos P A G) :=
2387    ⟨hT.1, hT.2.congr' (hev _).symm⟩
2388  have hex : ∃ Q, TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A' G') Q := ⟨_, hT'⟩
2389  have hC : TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A' G') (chaos P A' G') := by
2390    unfold chaos; rw [dif_pos hex]; exact hex.choose_spec
2391  have hC2 : TendstoL2 P (chaosSection A G) (chaos P A' G') := ⟨hC.1, hC.2.congr' (hev _)⟩
2392  have hmeas : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (chaosSection A G m) P := fun m =>
2393    ((memLp_quadSum hG A m).const_mul (1/2)).aestronglyMeasurable
2394  exact TendstoL2.ae_unique hmeas hC2 hT
2395
2396end Chaos

2398/-! ### Lemma 7.3: Banach-valued Gaussian contractions (work package EDGE-01) -/
2399
2400section Contractions
2401
2402variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
2403  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
2404variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] (P : Measure Ω) [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
2405
2406/-- A random element `D : Ω → B` of the centered second chaos of the Gaussian space spanned by
2407i.i.d. standard Gaussian coordinates `ξ`: an `L^q(B)` limit of centered quadratic polynomials in
2408finitely many coordinates (source lines 7815-7818). -/
2409def InSecondChaos (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (q : ℝ) (D : Ω → B) : Prop :=
2410  AEStronglyMeasurable D P ∧ ∃ (n : ℕ → ℕ) (c : ℕ → ℕ → ℕ → B),
2411    Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖(∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n m), ∑ j ∈ Finset.range (n m),
2412      (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) • c m i j) - D ω‖ₑ ^ q ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0)
2413
2414/-- A random element of the first chaos: `L^q(B)` limit of linear polynomials. -/
2415def InFirstChaos (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (q : ℝ) (J : Ω → B) : Prop :=
2416  AEStronglyMeasurable J P ∧ ∃ (n : ℕ → ℕ) (c : ℕ → ℕ → B),
2417    Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖(∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n m), ξ i ω • c m i) - J ω‖ₑ ^ q ∂P)
2418      atTop (𝓝 0)
2419
2420/-! #### Auxiliary lemmas for Lemma 7.3 (proof of `gaussian_second_contraction`)
2421
2422Route: Stein-type integration by parts for the finite-dimensional standard Gaussian measure
2423(one-dimensional Stein from Mathlib's integration by parts, Fubini over `Fin (n+1)`), the
2424resulting exact identities for finite polynomials `linP`, `quadP`, and then `L^p` limits:
2425Hölder, a quantitative Fernique moment-4 bound, and Fatou. (The manuscript's route via
2426`gaussian_ibp` is replaced by this direct finite-dimensional argument.) -/
2427

2433lemma hasDerivAt_gaussianPDFReal01 (x : ℝ) :
2434    HasDerivAt (gaussianPDFReal 0 1) (-(x * gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x)) x := by
2435  have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun x : ℝ => -(x - 0) ^ 2 / (2 * ((1 : ℝ≥0) : ℝ))) (-x) x := by
2436    have := ((hasDerivAt_id x).sub_const 0).pow 2
2437    have := this.neg.div_const (2 * ((1 : ℝ≥0) : ℝ))
2438    convert this using 1
2439    · ext y; simp
2440    · simp; ring
2441  have h2 := (h1.exp).const_mul ((√(2 * Real.pi * ((1 : ℝ≥0) : ℝ)))⁻¹)
2442  convert h2 using 1
2443  · ext y; simp [gaussianPDFReal_def]
2444  · simp [gaussianPDFReal_def]; ring

2446lemma stein_real {h h' : ℝ → ℝ} (hd : ∀ x, HasDerivAt h (h' x) x)
2447    (h1 : Integrable h (gaussianReal 0 1)) (h2 : Integrable (fun x => x * h x) (gaussianReal 0 1))
2448    (h3 : Integrable h' (gaussianReal 0 1)) :
2449    ∫ x, x * h x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) = ∫ x, h' x ∂(gaussianReal 0 1) := by
2450  have hv : (1 : ℝ≥0) ≠ 0 := one_ne_zero
2451  have hden : gaussianReal 0 1 = volume.withDensity (gaussianPDF 0 1) :=
2452    gaussianReal_of_var_ne_zero 0 hv
2453  have tr : ∀ g : ℝ → ℝ, Integrable g (gaussianReal 0 1) ↔
2454      Integrable (fun x => gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x * g x) volume := by
2455    intro g
2456    rw [hden, integrable_withDensity_iff_integrable_smul' (measurable_gaussianPDF 0 1)
2457      (ae_of_all _ fun _ => gaussianPDF_lt_top)]
2458    simp [toReal_gaussianPDF]
2459  rw [integral_gaussianReal_eq_integral_smul hv, integral_gaussianReal_eq_integral_smul hv]
2460  simp only [smul_eq_mul]
2461  have h1' := (tr _).1 h1
2462  have h2' := (tr _).1 h2
2463  have h3' := (tr _).1 h3
2464  have key := integral_mul_deriv_eq_deriv_mul_of_integrable
2465    (u := h) (u' := h') (v := fun x => -gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x)
2466    (v' := fun x => x * gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x) (fun x _ => hd x)
2467    (fun x _ => by simpa using (hasDerivAt_gaussianPDFReal01 x).fun_neg)
2468    (by
2469      refine h2'.congr (Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2470      simp only [Pi.mul_apply, Pi.neg_apply]; ring)
2471    (by
2472      refine (h3'.neg).congr (Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2473      simp only [Pi.mul_apply, Pi.neg_apply]; ring)
2474    (by
2475      refine (h1'.neg).congr (Eventually.of_forall fun x => ?_)
2476      simp only [Pi.mul_apply, Pi.neg_apply]; ring)
2477  have e1 : ∫ x, gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x * (x * h x) = ∫ x, h x * (x * gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x) := by
2478    congr 1; ext x; ring
2479  have e2 : ∫ x, h' x * -gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x = - ∫ x, gaussianPDFReal 0 1 x * h' x := by
2480    rw [← integral_neg]; congr 1; ext x; ring
2481  rw [e1, key, e2]; ring

2484/-- The standard Gaussian product measure on `ℝ^N`. -/
2485abbrev gpi (N : ℕ) : Measure (Fin N → ℝ) := Measure.pi fun _ => gaussianReal 0 1
2486
2487lemma stein_pi {N : ℕ} (i : Fin N) {F F' : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2488    (hd : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => F (Function.update z i x)) (F' z) (z i))
2489    (h1 : Integrable F (gpi N)) (h2 : Integrable (fun z => z i * F z) (gpi N))
2490    (h3 : Integrable F' (gpi N)) :
2491    ∫ z, z i * F z ∂(gpi N) = ∫ z, F' z ∂(gpi N) := by
2492  obtain ⟨n, rfl⟩ : ∃ n, N = n + 1 := ⟨N - 1, by have := i.pos; omega⟩
2493  have hmp := measurePreserving_piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => gaussianReal 0 1) i
2494  set e := MeasurableEquiv.piFinSuccAbove (fun _ : Fin (n+1) => ℝ) i with he
2495  have hsymm := hmp.symm e
2496  have tr : ∀ g : (Fin (n+1) → ℝ) → ℝ, ∫ z, g z ∂(gpi (n+1)) =
2497      ∫ p, g (e.symm p) ∂((gaussianReal 0 1).prod (Measure.pi fun j : Fin n => gaussianReal 0 1)) := by
2498    intro g
2499    exact (hsymm.integral_comp' g).symm
2500  have trI : ∀ g : (Fin (n+1) → ℝ) → ℝ, Integrable g (gpi (n+1)) →
2501      Integrable (fun p => g (e.symm p))
2502        ((gaussianReal 0 1).prod (Measure.pi fun j : Fin n => gaussianReal 0 1)) := by
2503    intro g hg
2504    exact (hsymm.integrable_comp_emb e.symm.measurableEmbedding).2 hg
2505  have ins : ∀ (x : ℝ) (w : Fin n → ℝ), e.symm (x, w) = Fin.insertNth i x w := by
2506    intro x w; rfl
2507  rw [tr, tr]
2508  simp only [ins]
2509  have i1 := trI _ h1
2510  have i2 := trI _ h2
2511  have i3 := trI _ h3
2512  simp only [ins] at i1 i2 i3
2513  rw [integral_prod_symm _ i2, integral_prod_symm _ i3]
2514  refine integral_congr_ae ?_
2515  filter_upwards [i1.prod_left_ae, i2.prod_left_ae, i3.prod_left_ae] with w hw1 hw2 hw3
2516  simp only [Fin.insertNth_apply_same] at hw2 ⊢
2517  refine stein_real (h := fun x => F (Fin.insertNth i x w))
2518    (h' := fun x => F' (Fin.insertNth i x w)) (fun x => ?_) hw1 hw2 hw3
2519  have := hd (Fin.insertNth i x w)
2520  simpa [Fin.update_insertNth] using this
2521

2523instance gpi_isGaussian (N : ℕ) : IsGaussian (gpi N) := by
2524  have h : HasGaussianLaw (fun (z : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) => z i) (gpi N) := by
2525    refine iIndepFun.hasGaussianLaw (X := fun (i : Fin N) (z : Fin N → ℝ) => z i) (fun i => ?_) ?_
2526    · refine ⟨(measurable_pi_apply i).aemeasurable, ?_⟩
2527      have : (gpi N).map (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => z i) = gaussianReal 0 1 :=
2528        (measurePreserving_eval (fun _ : Fin N => gaussianReal 0 1) i).map_eq
2529      rw [this]; infer_instance
2530    · exact iIndepFun_pi (fun i => aemeasurable_id)
2531  have := h.isGaussian_map
2532  simpa using this

2534lemma integrable_one_add_norm_pow (N p : ℕ) :
2535    Integrable (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) (gpi N) := by
2536  by_cases hp : p = 0
2537  · subst hp; simp
2538  have h1 := IsGaussian.memLp_id (gpi N) (p : ℝ≥0∞) (ENNReal.natCast_ne_top p)
2539  have h1' : MemLp (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => ‖z‖) (p : ℝ≥0∞) (gpi N) := h1.norm
2540  have h2 : MemLp (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => 1 + ‖z‖) (p : ℝ≥0∞) (gpi N) :=
2541    (memLp_const (1:ℝ)).add h1'
2542  have := h2.integrable_norm_pow hp
2543  refine this.congr (Eventually.of_forall fun z => ?_)
2544  simp only [Real.norm_eq_abs, abs_of_nonneg (by positivity : (0:ℝ) ≤ 1 + ‖z‖)]
2545
2546lemma integrable_of_poly {N : ℕ} {f : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f (gpi N))
2547    {C : ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hb : ∀ z, |f z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ k) : Integrable f (gpi N) :=
2548  ((integrable_one_add_norm_pow N k).const_mul C).mono' hf
2549    (Eventually.of_forall fun z => by simpa using hb z)
2550
2551
2552lemma dstein_pi {N : ℕ} (i j : Fin N) {F Fi Fij : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2553    (hFi : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => F (Function.update z i x)) (Fi z) (z i))
2554    (hFij : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => Fi (Function.update z j x)) (Fij z) (z j))
2555    (hFc : Continuous F) (hFic : Continuous Fi) (hFijc : Continuous Fij)
2556    {C : ℝ} {p : ℕ} (hb : ∀ z, |F z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p ∧ |Fi z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p ∧
2557      |Fij z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :
2558    ∫ z, (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) * F z ∂(gpi N) = ∫ z, Fij z ∂(gpi N) := by
2559  have hz : ∀ (k : Fin N) (z : Fin N → ℝ), |z k| ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := fun k z => by
2560    have := norm_le_pi_norm z k
2561    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this; linarith [norm_nonneg z]
2562  have hpow : ∀ z : Fin N → ℝ, (1 : ℝ) ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := fun z => by linarith [norm_nonneg z]
2563  have iF : Integrable F (gpi N) := integrable_of_poly hFc.aestronglyMeasurable (fun z => (hb z).1)
2564  have iFi : Integrable Fi (gpi N) :=
2565    integrable_of_poly hFic.aestronglyMeasurable (fun z => (hb z).2.1)
2566  have iFij : Integrable Fij (gpi N) :=
2567    integrable_of_poly hFijc.aestronglyMeasurable (fun z => (hb z).2.2)
2568  have hC : 0 ≤ C := by
2569    have := (hb 0).1
2570    have h0 : (0 : ℝ) ≤ (1 + ‖(0 : Fin N → ℝ)‖) ^ p := by positivity
2571    by_contra hneg
2572    push Not at hneg
2573    have : C * (1 + ‖(0 : Fin N → ℝ)‖) ^ p < 0 ∨ (1 + ‖(0 : Fin N → ℝ)‖) ^ p = 0 := by
2574      rcases h0.lt_or_eq with h | h
2575      · left; exact mul_neg_of_neg_of_pos hneg h
2576      · right; exact h.symm
2577    rcases this with h | h
2578    · linarith [abs_nonneg (F 0)]
2579    · simp at h
2580  -- integrability of z_j * F, z_i * z_j * F, z_j * Fi
2581  have iJF : Integrable (fun z => z j * F z) (gpi N) :=
2582    integrable_of_poly ((measurable_pi_apply j).aestronglyMeasurable.mul hFc.aestronglyMeasurable)
2583      (C := C) (k := p + 1) (fun z => by
2584        rw [abs_mul, pow_succ]
2585        calc |z j| * |F z| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) * (C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :=
2586              mul_le_mul (hz j z) (hb z).1 (abs_nonneg _) (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])
2587          _ = C * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ p * (1 + ‖z‖)) := by ring)
2588  have iIJF : Integrable (fun z => z i * (z j * F z)) (gpi N) :=
2589    integrable_of_poly ((measurable_pi_apply i).aestronglyMeasurable.mul iJF.aestronglyMeasurable)
2590      (C := C) (k := p + 2) (fun z => by
2591        rw [abs_mul, abs_mul, pow_succ, pow_succ]
2592        calc |z i| * (|z j| * |F z|) ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) * ((1 + ‖z‖) * (C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p)) :=
2593              mul_le_mul (hz i z) (mul_le_mul (hz j z) (hb z).1 (abs_nonneg _)
2594                (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])) (by positivity) (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])
2595          _ = C * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ p * (1 + ‖z‖) * (1 + ‖z‖)) := by ring)
2596  have iJFi : Integrable (fun z => z j * Fi z) (gpi N) :=
2597    integrable_of_poly ((measurable_pi_apply j).aestronglyMeasurable.mul hFic.aestronglyMeasurable)
2598      (C := C) (k := p + 1) (fun z => by
2599        rw [abs_mul, pow_succ]
2600        calc |z j| * |Fi z| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) * (C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :=
2601              mul_le_mul (hz j z) (hb z).2.1 (abs_nonneg _) (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])
2602          _ = C * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ p * (1 + ‖z‖)) := by ring)
2603  -- step 1
2604  have step1 : ∫ z, z i * (z j * F z) ∂(gpi N) =
2605      ∫ z, ((if j = i then 1 else 0) * F z + z j * Fi z) ∂(gpi N) := by
2606    refine stein_pi i (F := fun z => z j * F z)
2607      (F' := fun z => (if j = i then 1 else 0) * F z + z j * Fi z) (fun z => ?_) iJF iIJF ?_
2608    · have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun x => (Function.update z i x) j) (if j = i then 1 else 0) (z i) := by
2609        by_cases h : j = i
2610        · subst h
2611          convert hasDerivAt_id (z j) using 1
2612          · ext x; simp
2613          · simp
2614        · simp only [h, ite_false]
2615          have : (fun x => (Function.update z i x) j) = fun _ => z j := by
2616            ext x; simp [Function.update_of_ne h]
2617          rw [this]; exact hasDerivAt_const _ _
2618      have h2 := h1.mul (hFi z)
2619      convert h2 using 1
2620      simp
2621    · exact (iF.const_mul _).add iJFi
2622  have step2 : ∫ z, z j * Fi z ∂(gpi N) = ∫ z, Fij z ∂(gpi N) :=
2623    stein_pi j (F := Fi) (F' := Fij) hFij iFi iJFi iFij
2624  have e1 : ∫ z, (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) * F z ∂(gpi N) =
2625      ∫ z, z i * (z j * F z) ∂(gpi N) - (if i = j then 1 else 0) * ∫ z, F z ∂(gpi N) := by
2626    rw [← integral_const_mul, ← integral_sub iIJF (iF.const_mul _)]
2627    congr 1; ext z; ring
2628  rw [e1, step1, integral_add (iF.const_mul _) iJFi, integral_const_mul, step2]
2629  by_cases h : i = j
2630  · subst h; simp
2631  · have : ¬ (j = i) := fun h' => h h'.symm
2632    simp [h, this]
2633
2634
2635section FiniteStage
2636variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]
2637
2638lemma hasDerivAt_iter {Φ : B → ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ (k + 1) Φ) {N : ℕ}
2639    (T : (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) (v : Fin k → B) :
2640    HasDerivAt (fun x => iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ (T (Function.update z i x)) v)
2641      (iteratedFDeriv ℝ (k + 1) Φ (T z) (Fin.cons (T (Pi.single i 1)) v)) (z i) := by
2642  have hu : HasDerivAt (fun x => T (Function.update z i x)) (T (Pi.single i 1)) (z i) := by
2643    have h1 := hasDerivAt_update z i (z i)
2644    have h2 := T.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt (z i) h1
2645    exact h2
2646  have hD : DifferentiableAt ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) (T z) :=
2647    (hΦ.differentiable_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast Nat.lt_succ_self k)) (T z)
2648  have hu' : HasDerivAt (fun x => T (Function.update z i x)) (T (Pi.single i 1)) (z i) := hu
2649  have hz : T (Function.update z i (z i)) = T z := by rw [Function.update_eq_self]
2650  have hF := hD.hasFDerivAt.continuousMultilinear_apply_const v
2651  rw [← hz] at hF
2652  have h3 := hF.comp_hasDerivAt (z i) hu'
2653  rw [hz] at h3
2654  have := iteratedFDeriv_succ_apply_left (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := Φ) (x := T z)
2655    (Fin.cons (T (Pi.single i 1)) v : Fin (k + 1) → B)
2656  convert h3 using 1
2657  all_goals first | rfl | simpa using this
2658
2659
2660lemma iter2_swap {F : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℝ F] {Φ : B → F}
2661    (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Φ) (y a b : B) :
2662    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y ![a, b] = iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y ![b, a] := by
2663  have h := (hΦ.contDiffAt (x := y)).isSymmSndFDerivAt (by simp)
2664  simp only [iteratedFDeriv_two_apply]
2665  exact h.eq a b
2666
2667lemma iter3_swap23 {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) (y u a b : B) :
2668    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![u, a, b] = iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![u, b, a] := by
2669  have hd : ∀ v : Fin 2 → B, fderiv ℝ (fun y' => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y' v) y =
2670      (fderiv ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ) y).flipMultilinear v := fun v =>
2671    fderiv_continuousMultilinear_apply_const
2672      ((hΦ.differentiable_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)) y) v
2673  have h1 : ∀ a b : B, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![u, a, b] =
2674      fderiv ℝ (fun y' => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y' ![a, b]) y u := by
2675    intro a b
2676    rw [hd]
2677    have := iteratedFDeriv_succ_apply_left (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := Φ) (x := y) (n := 2) ![u, a, b]
2678    simpa using this
2679  rw [h1, h1]
2680  congr 2
2681  ext y'
2682  exact iter2_swap (hΦ.of_le (by norm_num)) y' a b
2683
2684lemma iter3_swap12 {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) (y u a b : B) :
2685    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![u, a, b] = iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![a, u, b] := by
2686  have h1 : ∀ u a b : B, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![u, a, b] =
2687      iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 (fun y => fderiv ℝ Φ y) y ![u, a] b := by
2688    intro u a b
2689    have := iteratedFDeriv_succ_apply_right (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := Φ) (x := y) (n := 2) ![u, a, b]
2690    have hi : Fin.init ![u, a, b] = ![u, a] := by ext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
2691    rw [hi] at this
2692    simpa using this
2693  rw [h1, h1]
2694  have hd : ContDiff ℝ 2 (fun y => fderiv ℝ Φ y) := hΦ.fderiv_right (by norm_num)
2695  have := iter2_swap hd y u a
2696  rw [this]
2697
2698
2699lemma iter3_cyc {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) (y a p q : B) :
2700    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![a, p, q] = iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![p, q, a] := by
2701  rw [iter3_swap12 hΦ y a p q, iter3_swap23 hΦ y p a q]
2702
2703lemma iter3_rev {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) (y a b c : B) :
2704    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![b, a, c] = iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y ![c, a, b] := by
2705  rw [iter3_swap23 hΦ y b a c, iter3_swap12 hΦ y b c a, iter3_swap23 hΦ y c b a]

2707section Tri
2708variable (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (c : B)
2709
2710lemma tri_add2 (x x' y : B) : M ![c, x + x', y] = M ![c, x, y] + M ![c, x', y] := by
2711  have h := M.map_update_add ![c, x, y] 1 x x'
2712  have e : ∀ z : B, Function.update (![c, x, y] : Fin 3 → B) 1 z = ![c, z, y] := by
2713    intro z; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
2714  simpa [e] using h
2715
2716lemma tri_add3 (x y y' : B) : M ![c, x, y + y'] = M ![c, x, y] + M ![c, x, y'] := by
2717  have h := M.map_update_add ![c, x, y] 2 y y'
2718  have e : ∀ z : B, Function.update (![c, x, y] : Fin 3 → B) 2 z = ![c, x, z] := by
2719    intro z; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
2720  simpa [e] using h
2721
2722lemma tri_smul2 (t : ℝ) (x y : B) : M ![c, t • x, y] = t * M ![c, x, y] := by
2723  have h := M.map_update_smul ![c, x, y] 1 t x
2724  have e : ∀ z : B, Function.update (![c, x, y] : Fin 3 → B) 1 z = ![c, z, y] := by
2725    intro z; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
2726  simpa [e] using h
2727
2728lemma tri_smul3 (t : ℝ) (x y : B) : M ![c, x, t • y] = t * M ![c, x, y] := by
2729  have h := M.map_update_smul ![c, x, y] 2 t y
2730  have e : ∀ z : B, Function.update (![c, x, y] : Fin 3 → B) 2 z = ![c, x, z] := by
2731    intro z; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
2732  simpa [e] using h
2733
2734lemma tri_expand (a w b v : B) (t s : ℝ) :
2735    M ![c, a + t • w, b + s • v] =
2736      M ![c, a, b] + s * M ![c, a, v] + t * M ![c, w, b] + t * s * M ![c, w, v] := by
2737  rw [tri_add2, tri_add3, tri_add3, tri_smul2, tri_smul2, tri_smul3, tri_smul3]
2738  ring
2739
2740end Tri

2743/-- The linear map `z ↦ ∑ z_k • w_k`. -/
2744def Tmap {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) : (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] B :=
2745  ∑ k, (ContinuousLinearMap.proj k : (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] ℝ).smulRight (w k)
2746
2747lemma Tmap_apply {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) : Tmap w z = ∑ k, z k • w k := by
2748  simp [Tmap]
2749
2750lemma Tmap_single {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) (i : Fin N) : Tmap w (Pi.single i 1) = w i := by
2751  rw [Tmap_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
2752  · simp
2753  · intro k _ hk; simp [Pi.single_apply, hk]
2754  · simp
2755
2756lemma Tmap_update {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) (x : ℝ) :
2757    Tmap w (Function.update z i x) = Tmap w z + (x - z i) • w i := by
2758  have : Function.update z i x = z + (x - z i) • Pi.single i 1 := by
2759    ext k
2760    by_cases h : k = i
2761    · subst h; simp
2762    · simp [Function.update_of_ne h, Pi.single_apply, h]
2763  rw [this, map_add, map_smul, Tmap_single]
2764
2765lemma norm_Tmap_le {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) :
2766    ‖Tmap w z‖ ≤ (∑ k, ‖w k‖) * ‖z‖ := by
2767  rw [Tmap_apply]
2768  calc ‖∑ k, z k • w k‖ ≤ ∑ k, ‖z k • w k‖ := norm_sum_le _ _
2769    _ ≤ ∑ k, ‖z‖ * ‖w k‖ := Finset.sum_le_sum fun k _ => by
2770        rw [norm_smul]
2771        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_le_pi_norm z k) (norm_nonneg _)
2772    _ = (∑ k, ‖w k‖) * ‖z‖ := by rw [← Finset.mul_sum]; ring
2773
2774section Tri2
2775variable (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (c : B)

2777lemma hasDerivAt_quad (a w : B) (x0 : ℝ) :
2778    HasDerivAt (fun x => M ![c, a + (x - x0) • w, a + (x - x0) • w])
2779      (M ![c, w, a] + M ![c, a, w]) x0 := by
2780  have e : (fun x => M ![c, a + (x - x0) • w, a + (x - x0) • w]) = fun x =>
2781      M ![c, a, a] + (x - x0) * M ![c, a, w] + (x - x0) * M ![c, w, a] +
2782        (x - x0) * (x - x0) * M ![c, w, w] := by
2783    ext x; exact tri_expand M c a w a w (x - x0) (x - x0)
2784  rw [e]
2785  have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun x : ℝ => x - x0) 1 x0 := (hasDerivAt_id x0).sub_const x0
2786  have h2 := (((hasDerivAt_const x0 (M ![c, a, a])).add (h1.mul_const (M ![c, a, w]))).add
2787    (h1.mul_const (M ![c, w, a]))).add ((h1.mul h1).mul_const (M ![c, w, w]))
2788  convert h2 using 1
2789  simp; ring
2790
2791lemma hasDerivAt_lin3 (u a w : B) (x0 : ℝ) :
2792    HasDerivAt (fun x => M ![c, u, a + (x - x0) • w]) (M ![c, u, w]) x0 := by
2793  have e : (fun x => M ![c, u, a + (x - x0) • w]) = fun x =>
2794      M ![c, u, a] + (x - x0) * M ![c, u, w] := by
2795    ext x; rw [tri_add3, tri_smul3]
2796  rw [e]
2797  have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun x : ℝ => x - x0) 1 x0 := (hasDerivAt_id x0).sub_const x0
2798  convert (hasDerivAt_const x0 (M ![c, u, a])).add (h1.mul_const (M ![c, u, w])) using 1
2799  simp
2800
2801lemma hasDerivAt_lin2 (u a w : B) (x0 : ℝ) :
2802    HasDerivAt (fun x => M ![c, a + (x - x0) • w, u]) (M ![c, w, u]) x0 := by
2803  have e : (fun x => M ![c, a + (x - x0) • w, u]) = fun x =>
2804      M ![c, a, u] + (x - x0) * M ![c, w, u] := by
2805    ext x; rw [tri_add2, tri_smul2]
2806  rw [e]
2807  have h1 : HasDerivAt (fun x : ℝ => x - x0) 1 x0 := (hasDerivAt_id x0).sub_const x0
2808  convert (hasDerivAt_const x0 (M ![c, a, u])).add (h1.mul_const (M ![c, w, u])) using 1
2809  simp
