2820section OmegaStage
2821variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]
2822variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}
2823
2824/-- The coordinate vector `(ξ_0, …, ξ_{N-1})`. -/
2825def Zvec (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (ω : Ω) : Fin N → ℝ := fun i => ξ i ω
2826
2827lemma aemeasurable_Zvec (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (N : ℕ) : AEMeasurable (Zvec ξ N) P :=
2828  AEMeasurable.of_eval fun i => hξ.aemeasurable i
2829
2830lemma map_Zvec (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (N : ℕ) : P.map (Zvec ξ N) = gpi N :=
2831  law_vec hξ N
2832
2833lemma integral_comp_Zvec (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (N : ℕ) {g : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2834    (hg : Continuous g) : ∫ ω, g (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P = ∫ z, g z ∂(gpi N) := by
2835  rw [← map_Zvec hξ N]
2836  exact (integral_map (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ N) hg.aestronglyMeasurable).symm
2837
2838lemma integrable_comp_Zvec (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (N : ℕ) {g : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2839    (hg : Continuous g) {C : ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hb : ∀ z, |g z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ k) :
2840    Integrable (fun ω => g (Zvec ξ N ω)) P := by
2841  have h1 : Integrable g (gpi N) := integrable_of_poly hg.aestronglyMeasurable hb
2842  rw [← map_Zvec hξ N] at h1
2843  exact (integrable_map_measure hg.aestronglyMeasurable (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ N)).1 h1
2844
2845lemma stein_Z (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (i : Fin N) {F F' : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2846    (hd : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => F (Function.update z i x)) (F' z) (z i))
2847    (hFc : Continuous F) (hF'c : Continuous F') {C : ℝ} {p : ℕ}
2848    (hb : ∀ z, |F z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p ∧ |F' z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :
2849    ∫ ω, ξ i ω * F (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P = ∫ ω, F' (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P := by
2850  have e1 := integral_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => z i * F z)
2851    ((continuous_apply i).mul hFc)
2852  have e2 := integral_comp_Zvec hξ N hF'c
2853  have hz : ∀ z : Fin N → ℝ, |z i| ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := fun z => by
2854    have := norm_le_pi_norm z i
2855    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this; linarith [norm_nonneg z]
2856  have iF : Integrable F (gpi N) := integrable_of_poly hFc.aestronglyMeasurable (fun z => (hb z).1)
2857  have iF' : Integrable F' (gpi N) :=
2858    integrable_of_poly hF'c.aestronglyMeasurable (fun z => (hb z).2)
2859  have iZF : Integrable (fun z => z i * F z) (gpi N) :=
2860    integrable_of_poly ((continuous_apply i).mul hFc).aestronglyMeasurable (C := C) (k := p + 1)
2861      (fun z => by
2862        rw [abs_mul, pow_succ]
2863        calc |z i| * |F z| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) * (C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :=
2864              mul_le_mul (hz z) (hb z).1 (abs_nonneg _) (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])
2865          _ = C * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ p * (1 + ‖z‖)) := by ring)
2866  have := stein_pi i hd iF iZF iF'
2867  show ∫ ω, (fun z => z i * F z) (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P = _
2868  rw [e1, e2]
2869  exact this
2870
2871lemma dstein_Z (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (i j : Fin N) {F Fi Fij : (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ}
2872    (hFi : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => F (Function.update z i x)) (Fi z) (z i))
2873    (hFij : ∀ z, HasDerivAt (fun x => Fi (Function.update z j x)) (Fij z) (z j))
2874    (hFc : Continuous F) (hFic : Continuous Fi) (hFijc : Continuous Fij)
2875    {C : ℝ} {p : ℕ} (hb : ∀ z, |F z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p ∧ |Fi z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p ∧
2876      |Fij z| ≤ C * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ p) :
2877    ∫ ω, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) * F (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P =
2878      ∫ ω, Fij (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P := by
2879  have e1 := integral_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) * F z)
2880    ((((continuous_apply i).mul (continuous_apply j)).sub continuous_const).mul hFc)
2881  have e2 := integral_comp_Zvec hξ N hFijc
2882  show ∫ ω, (fun z => (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) * F z) (Zvec ξ N ω) ∂P = _
2883  rw [e1, e2]
2884  exact dstein_pi i j hFi hFij hFc hFic hFijc hb
2885
2886end OmegaStage
2887
2888end Aux_Hlp2

2893section PhiStage
2894variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]
2895variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}
2896
2897lemma memCb_bound {Φ : B → ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hΦ : MemCb k Φ) :
2898    ∃ K : ℝ, 0 ≤ K ∧ ∀ j ≤ k, ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ x‖ ≤ K := by
2899  have h : ∀ j : Fin (k + 1), ∃ K : ℝ, ∀ x, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ x‖ ≤ K := fun j => by
2900    obtain ⟨K, hK⟩ := hΦ.2 j (by omega)
2901    exact ⟨K, fun x => hK ⟨x, rfl⟩⟩
2902  choose Kf hKf using h
2903  refine ⟨∑ j, |Kf j|, Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => abs_nonneg _, fun j hj x => ?_⟩
2904  have hj' : j < k + 1 := by omega
2905  calc ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ x‖ ≤ Kf ⟨j, hj'⟩ := hKf ⟨j, hj'⟩ x
2906    _ ≤ |Kf ⟨j, hj'⟩| := le_abs_self _
2907    _ ≤ ∑ j, |Kf j| :=
2908        Finset.single_le_sum (f := fun j => |Kf j|) (fun j _ => abs_nonneg _) (Finset.mem_univ _)
2909
2910lemma abs_iter_le {Φ : B → ℝ} {K : ℝ} {j : ℕ} {y : B} (h : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y‖ ≤ K)
2911    (v : Fin j → B) : |iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y v| ≤ K * ∏ i, ‖v i‖ := by
2912  have := (iteratedFDeriv ℝ j Φ y).le_opNorm v
2913  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
2914  exact this.trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right h (Finset.prod_nonneg fun i _ => norm_nonneg _))

2916lemma le_prod_one_add (a b c : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hc : 0 ≤ c) :
2917    a ≤ (1 + a) * (1 + b) * (1 + c) ∧ a * b ≤ (1 + a) * (1 + b) * (1 + c) ∧
2918      a * b * c ≤ (1 + a) * (1 + b) * (1 + c) := by
2919  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩ <;> nlinarith [mul_nonneg ha hb, mul_nonneg hb hc, mul_nonneg ha hc,
2920    mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg ha hb) hc]

2922lemma fin2_termA (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
2923    (w : Fin N → B) (cc : B) (i j : Fin N) :
2924    ∫ ω, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) * fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) cc ∂P =
2925      ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) ![cc, w i, w j] ∂P := by
2926  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
2927  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
2928  set T := Tmap w with hT
2929  have hcont : ∀ k ≤ 3, ∀ v : Fin k → B, Continuous fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ (T z) v := by
2930    intro k hk v
2931    have h1 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) :=
2932      hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast hk)
2933    exact (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin k => B) ℝ v).continuous.comp
2934      (h1.comp T.continuous)
2935  have key := dstein_Z hξ i j (N := N) (P := P)
2936    (F := fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ (T z) ![cc])
2937    (Fi := fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (T z) ![w i, cc])
2938    (Fij := fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T z) ![w j, w i, cc])
2939    (fun z => by
2940      have := hasDerivAt_iter (Φ := Φ) (k := 1) (hΦ3.of_le (by norm_num)) T z i ![cc]
2941      rw [Tmap_single] at this
2942      exact this)
2943    (fun z => by
2944      have := hasDerivAt_iter (Φ := Φ) (k := 2) (hΦ3.of_le (by norm_num)) T z j ![w i, cc]
2945      rw [Tmap_single] at this
2946      exact this)
2947    (hcont 1 (by norm_num) _) (hcont 2 (by norm_num) _) (hcont 3 (le_refl _) _)
2948    (C := K * (1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖)) (p := 0) (fun z => by
2949      have b1 := abs_iter_le (hK 1 (by norm_num) (T z)) ![cc]
2950      have b2 := abs_iter_le (hK 2 (by norm_num) (T z)) ![w i, cc]
2951      have b3 := abs_iter_le (hK 3 (by norm_num) (T z)) ![w j, w i, cc]
2952      simp only [Fin.prod_univ_succ, Fin.prod_univ_zero, Matrix.cons_val_zero,
2953        Matrix.cons_val_succ, mul_one, Fin.succ_zero_eq_one] at b1 b2 b3
2954      simp only [pow_zero, mul_one]
2955      obtain ⟨l1, l2, l3⟩ := le_prod_one_add ‖cc‖ ‖w i‖ ‖w j‖ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_nonneg _)
2956        (norm_nonneg _)
2957      have e1 : K * ‖cc‖ ≤ K * (1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖) := by
2958        calc K * ‖cc‖ ≤ K * ((1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖)) :=
2959              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left l1 hK0
2960          _ = _ := by ring
2961      have e2 : K * (‖w i‖ * ‖cc‖) ≤ K * (1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖) := by
2962        calc K * (‖w i‖ * ‖cc‖) = K * (‖cc‖ * ‖w i‖) := by ring
2963          _ ≤ K * ((1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖)) :=
2964              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left l2 hK0
2965          _ = _ := by ring
2966      have e3 : K * (‖w j‖ * (‖w i‖ * ‖cc‖)) ≤ K * (1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖) := by
2967        calc K * (‖w j‖ * (‖w i‖ * ‖cc‖)) = K * (‖cc‖ * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖) := by ring
2968          _ ≤ K * ((1 + ‖cc‖) * (1 + ‖w i‖) * (1 + ‖w j‖)) :=
2969              mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left l3 hK0
2970          _ = _ := by ring
2971      exact ⟨b1.trans e1, b2.trans e2, b3.trans e3⟩)
2972  simp only [iteratedFDeriv_one_apply, Matrix.cons_val_zero] at key
2973  rw [key]
2974  refine integral_congr_ae (Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_)
2975  exact iter3_rev hΦ3 _ (w i) (w j) cc

2978lemma abs_tri_le (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (a b c : B) : |M ![a, b, c]| ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖a‖ * ‖b‖ * ‖c‖) := by
2979  have := M.le_opNorm ![a, b, c]
2980  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
2981  simpa [Fin.prod_univ_succ, mul_assoc] using this

2983lemma continuous_tri (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) {X : Type*} [TopologicalSpace X] {f g h : X → B}
2984    (hf : Continuous f) (hg : Continuous g) (hh : Continuous h) :
2985    Continuous fun x => M ![f x, g x, h x] := by
2986  refine M.cont.comp (continuous_pi fun k => ?_)
2987  fin_cases k <;> simpa

2989lemma fin2_termB (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ)
2990    (cc : B) (i j : Fin N) :
2991    ∫ ω, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
2992        M ![cc, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)] ∂P =
2993      M ![cc, w i, w j] + M ![cc, w j, w i] := by
2994  set T := Tmap w with hT
2995  set A : ℝ := ∑ k, ‖w k‖ with hA
2996  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => norm_nonneg _
2997  have key := dstein_Z hξ i j (N := N) (P := P)
2998    (F := fun z => M ![cc, T z, T z])
2999    (Fi := fun z => M ![cc, w i, T z] + M ![cc, T z, w i])
3000    (Fij := fun _ => M ![cc, w i, w j] + M ![cc, w j, w i])
3001    (fun z => by
3002      have := hasDerivAt_quad M cc (T z) (w i) (z i)
3003      simp only [hT, Tmap_update]
3004      exact this)
3005    (fun z => by
3006      have h1 := hasDerivAt_lin3 M cc (w i) (T z) (w j) (z j)
3007      have h2 := hasDerivAt_lin2 M cc (w i) (T z) (w j) (z j)
3008      simp only [hT, Tmap_update]
3009      exact h1.add h2)
3010    (continuous_tri M continuous_const T.continuous T.continuous)
3011    ((continuous_tri M continuous_const continuous_const T.continuous).add
3012      (continuous_tri M continuous_const T.continuous continuous_const))
3013    continuous_const
3014    (C := ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (A ^ 2 + 2 * ‖w i‖ * A + 2 * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖)) (p := 2) (fun z => by
3015      have hz0 := norm_nonneg z
3016      have hT' : ‖T z‖ ≤ A * ‖z‖ := by rw [hT, hA]; rw [mul_comm]; exact (by
3017        have := norm_Tmap_le w z; linarith)
3018      have hTn := norm_nonneg (T z)
3019      have b1 := abs_tri_le M cc (T z) (T z)
3020      have b2 := abs_tri_le M cc (w i) (T z)
3021      have b3 := abs_tri_le M cc (T z) (w i)
3022      have b4 := abs_tri_le M cc (w i) (w j)
3023      have b5 := abs_tri_le M cc (w j) (w i)
3024      have hM := norm_nonneg M
3025      have hc := norm_nonneg cc
3026      have hwi := norm_nonneg (w i)
3027      have hwj := norm_nonneg (w j)
3028      set S : ℝ := (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 with hS
3029      have hs : ‖z‖ ≤ S := by rw [hS]; nlinarith
3030      have hs2 : ‖z‖ ^ 2 ≤ S := by rw [hS]; nlinarith
3031      have hs1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ S := by rw [hS]; nlinarith
3032      have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := by linarith
3033      have hMc : 0 ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ := mul_nonneg hM hc
3034      set q : ℝ := A ^ 2 + 2 * ‖w i‖ * A + 2 * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖ with hq
3035      have hq1 : A ^ 2 * S ≤ q * S := by
3036        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hS0
3037        rw [hq]; nlinarith [mul_nonneg hwi hA0, mul_nonneg hwi hwj]
3038      have hq2 : 2 * ‖w i‖ * A * S ≤ q * S := by
3039        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hS0
3040        rw [hq]; nlinarith [sq_nonneg A, mul_nonneg hwi hwj]
3041      have hq3 : 2 * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖ * S ≤ q * S := by
3042        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right _ hS0
3043        rw [hq]; nlinarith [sq_nonneg A, mul_nonneg hwi hA0]
3044      refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
3045      · calc |M ![cc, T z, T z]| ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖T z‖ * ‖T z‖) := b1
3046          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * (A * ‖z‖) * (A * ‖z‖)) := by gcongr
3047          _ = ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (A ^ 2 * ‖z‖ ^ 2) := by ring
3048          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (A ^ 2 * S) := by
3049              apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ hMc
3050              exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs2 (sq_nonneg A)
3051          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (q * S) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hq1 hMc
3052          _ = _ := by ring
3053      · calc |M ![cc, w i, T z] + M ![cc, T z, w i]|
3054            ≤ |M ![cc, w i, T z]| + |M ![cc, T z, w i]| := abs_add_le _ _
3055          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖w i‖ * ‖T z‖) + ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖T z‖ * ‖w i‖) := add_le_add b2 b3
3056          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖w i‖ * (A * ‖z‖)) + ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * (A * ‖z‖) * ‖w i‖) := by gcongr
3057          _ = ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (2 * ‖w i‖ * A * ‖z‖) := by ring
3058          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (2 * ‖w i‖ * A * S) := by
3059              apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ hMc
3060              exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hs (by positivity)
3061          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (q * S) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hq2 hMc
3062          _ = _ := by ring
3063      · calc |M ![cc, w i, w j] + M ![cc, w j, w i]|
3064            ≤ |M ![cc, w i, w j]| + |M ![cc, w j, w i]| := abs_add_le _ _
3065          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖) + ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖w j‖ * ‖w i‖) := add_le_add b4 b5
3066          _ = ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (2 * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖) := by ring
3067          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (2 * ‖w i‖ * ‖w j‖ * S) := by
3068              apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ hMc
3069              exact le_mul_of_one_le_right (by positivity) hs1
3070          _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * (q * S) := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hq3 hMc
3071          _ = _ := by ring)
3072  simpa using key

3081section Assemble2
3082variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]

3085/-- The finite centered quadratic polynomial `∑ (ξ_i ξ_j - δ_ij) c_ij`. -/
3086def Dfin (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) (ω : Ω) : B :=
3087  ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) • c i j
3088
3089lemma abs_zz_le {N : ℕ} (i j : Fin N) (z : Fin N → ℝ) :
3090    |z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3091  have hi : |z i| ≤ ‖z‖ := by simpa using norm_le_pi_norm z i
3092  have hj : |z j| ≤ ‖z‖ := by simpa using norm_le_pi_norm z j
3093  have h0 := norm_nonneg z
3094  have : |z i * z j| ≤ ‖z‖ ^ 2 := by
3095    rw [abs_mul, sq]; exact mul_le_mul hi hj (abs_nonneg _) h0
3096  calc |z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0| ≤ |z i * z j| + |(if i = j then (1 : ℝ) else 0)| :=
3097        abs_sub _ _
3098    _ ≤ ‖z‖ ^ 2 + 1 := by
3099        refine add_le_add this ?_
3100        split_ifs <;> simp
3101    _ ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by nlinarith
3102
3103lemma continuous_zz {N : ℕ} (i j : Fin N) :
3104    Continuous fun z : Fin N → ℝ => z i * z j - if i = j then (1 : ℝ) else 0 :=
3105  (((continuous_apply i).mul (continuous_apply j)).sub continuous_const)

3107lemma integrable_zz_quad (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B)
3108    (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (cc : B) (i j : Fin N) :
3109    Integrable (fun ω => (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3110      M ![cc, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)]) P := by
3111  set T := Tmap w
3112  set A : ℝ := ∑ k, ‖w k‖ with hA
3113  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => norm_nonneg _
3114  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3115      M ![cc, T z, T z]) ((continuous_zz i j).mul
3116        (continuous_tri M continuous_const T.continuous T.continuous))
3117    (C := ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2) (k := 4) (fun z => ?_)
3118  have hz0 := norm_nonneg z
3119  have hT' : ‖T z‖ ≤ A * ‖z‖ := by
3120    exact norm_Tmap_le w z
3121  have b1 := abs_tri_le M cc (T z) (T z)
3122  have hM := norm_nonneg M
3123  have hc := norm_nonneg cc
3124  have hTn := norm_nonneg (T z)
3125  rw [abs_mul]
3126  have e1 := abs_zz_le i j z
3127  have e2 : |M ![cc, T z, T z]| ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3128    calc |M ![cc, T z, T z]| ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * ‖T z‖ * ‖T z‖) := b1
3129      _ ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖cc‖ * (A * ‖z‖) * (A * ‖z‖)) := by gcongr
3130      _ = ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2 * ‖z‖ ^ 2 := by ring
3131      _ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3132          gcongr; nlinarith
3133  calc |z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0| * |M ![cc, T z, T z]|
3134      ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * (‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2) :=
3135        mul_le_mul e1 e2 (abs_nonneg _) (by positivity)
3136    _ = ‖M‖ * ‖cc‖ * A ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 4 := by ring

3139lemma abs_fderiv_le {Φ : B → ℝ} {K : ℝ} (hK : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y‖ ≤ K) (y cc : B) :
3140    |fderiv ℝ Φ y cc| ≤ K * ‖cc‖ := by

3144lemma integrable_termA (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3145    (w : Fin N → B) (cc : B) (i j : Fin N) :
3146    Integrable (fun ω => (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3147      fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) cc) P := by
3148  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
3149  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3150  set T := Tmap w
3151  have hc : Continuous fun z => fderiv ℝ Φ (T z) cc :=
3152    (ContinuousLinearMap.apply ℝ ℝ cc).continuous.comp
3153      ((hΦ3.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)).comp T.continuous)
3154  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3155      fderiv ℝ Φ (T z) cc) ((continuous_zz i j).mul hc) (C := K * ‖cc‖) (k := 2) (fun z => ?_)
3156  rw [abs_mul]
3157  calc |z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0| * |fderiv ℝ Φ (T z) cc|
3158      ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * (K * ‖cc‖) :=
3159        mul_le_mul (abs_zz_le i j z) (abs_fderiv_le (fun y => hK 1 (by norm_num) y) _ _)
3160          (abs_nonneg _) (by positivity)
3161    _ = K * ‖cc‖ * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by ring

3163lemma fin2_A (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3164    (w : Fin N → B) (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) :
3165    ∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) (Dfin ξ N c ω) ∂P =
3166      ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) ![c i j, w i, w j] ∂P := by
3167  have e : ∀ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) (Dfin ξ N c ω) =
3168      ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3169        fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) (c i j) := by
3170    intro ω
3171    simp only [Dfin, map_sum, map_smul, smul_eq_mul]
3172  simp_rw [e]
3173  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j _ =>
3174    integrable_termA hξ hΦ w (c i j) i j)]
3175  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3176  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun j _ => integrable_termA hξ hΦ w (c i j) i j)]
3177  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3178  exact fin2_termA hξ hΦ w (c i j) i j

3181/-- the polynomial `Dfin` as a function of the coordinate vector -/
3182def Qfun {N : ℕ} (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) : B :=
3183  ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) • c i j
3184
3185lemma Dfin_eq (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) {N : ℕ} (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) (ω : Ω) :
3186    Dfin ξ N c ω = Qfun c (Zvec ξ N ω) := rfl
3187
3188lemma continuous_Qfun {N : ℕ} (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) : Continuous (Qfun c) := by
3189  unfold Qfun
3190  refine continuous_finsetSum _ fun i _ => continuous_finsetSum _ fun j _ => ?_
3191  exact (continuous_zz i j).smul continuous_const
3192
3193lemma norm_Qfun_le {N : ℕ} (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) :
3194    ‖Qfun c z‖ ≤ (∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, ‖c i j‖) * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3195  unfold Qfun
3196  calc ‖∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) • c i j‖
3197      ≤ ∑ i : Fin N, ‖∑ j : Fin N, (z i * z j - if i = j then 1 else 0) • c i j‖ :=
3198        norm_sum_le _ _
3199    _ ≤ ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * ‖c i j‖ := by
3200        refine Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => ?_
3201        refine (norm_sum_le _ _).trans (Finset.sum_le_sum fun j _ => ?_)
3202        rw [norm_smul, Real.norm_eq_abs]
3203        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (abs_zz_le i j z) (norm_nonneg _)
3204    _ = (∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, ‖c i j‖) * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3205        rw [Finset.sum_mul]
3206        refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3207        rw [Finset.sum_mul]
3208        exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => mul_comm _ _
3209
3210lemma integrable_prodB (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3211    (w : Fin N → B) (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) :
3212    Integrable (fun p : Ω × Ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))
3213      ![Dfin ξ N c p.2, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2)]) (P.prod P) := by
3214  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
3215  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3216  set T := Tmap w with hT
3217  set A : ℝ := ∑ k, ‖w k‖ with hA
3218  set Cc : ℝ := ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, ‖c i j‖ with hCc
3219  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => norm_nonneg _
3220  have hCc0 : 0 ≤ Cc := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => norm_nonneg _
3221  have hcont3 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) :=
3222    hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
3223  let F : (Fin N → ℝ) × (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ := fun q =>
3224    iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T q.1) ![Qfun c q.2, T q.2, T q.2]
3225  have hFc : Continuous F := by
3226    refine Continuous.eval (hcont3.comp (T.continuous.comp continuous_fst)) ?_
3227    refine continuous_pi fun k => ?_
3228    fin_cases k
3229    · exact (continuous_Qfun c).comp continuous_snd
3230    · exact T.continuous.comp continuous_snd
3231    · exact T.continuous.comp continuous_snd
3232  have hmeas : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => F (Zvec ξ N p.1, Zvec ξ N p.2))
3233      (P.prod P) := by
3234    have hZ : AEStronglyMeasurable (Zvec ξ N) P := (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ N).aestronglyMeasurable
3235    exact hFc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable (hZ.comp_fst.prodMk hZ.comp_snd)
3236  have hbd : Integrable (fun y : Ω => K * (‖Qfun c (Zvec ξ N y)‖ * ‖T (Zvec ξ N y)‖ *
3237      ‖T (Zvec ξ N y)‖)) P := by
3238    refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => K * (‖Qfun c z‖ * ‖T z‖ * ‖T z‖))
3239      (continuous_const.mul ((((continuous_Qfun c).norm).mul T.continuous.norm).mul
3240        T.continuous.norm)) (C := K * Cc * A ^ 2) (k := 4) (fun z => ?_)
3241    have hz0 := norm_nonneg z
3242    have hT' : ‖T z‖ ≤ A * ‖z‖ := norm_Tmap_le w z
3243    have hq := norm_Qfun_le c z
3244    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
3245    calc K * (‖Qfun c z‖ * ‖T z‖ * ‖T z‖)
3246        ≤ K * ((Cc * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2) * (A * ‖z‖) * (A * ‖z‖)) := by gcongr
3247      _ = K * Cc * A ^ 2 * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * ‖z‖ ^ 2) := by ring
3248      _ ≤ K * Cc * A ^ 2 * ((1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2) := by
3249          gcongr; nlinarith
3250      _ = K * Cc * A ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 4 := by ring
3251  refine Integrable.mono' (hbd.comp_snd P) hmeas (Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
3252  have := abs_iter_le (hK 3 (le_refl _) (T (Zvec ξ N p.1))) ![Qfun c (Zvec ξ N p.2),
3253    T (Zvec ξ N p.2), T (Zvec ξ N p.2)]
3254  simp only [Fin.prod_univ_succ, Fin.prod_univ_zero, Matrix.cons_val_zero, Matrix.cons_val_succ,
3255    mul_one] at this
3256  simpa [F, Dfin_eq, mul_assoc] using this
3257
3258
3259lemma tri_sum0 {ι : Type*} (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → B) (a b : B) :
3260    M ![∑ i ∈ s, f i, a, b] = ∑ i ∈ s, M ![f i, a, b] := by
3261  have h : ∀ x : B, M ![x, a, b] = (M.toContinuousLinearMap ![0, a, b] 0) x := by
3262    intro x
3263    simp only [ContinuousMultilinearMap.toContinuousLinearMap_apply]
3264    congr 1
3265    ext k; fin_cases k <;> simp [Function.update]
3266  simp_rw [h]
3267  exact map_sum _ _ _
3268
3269lemma tri_smul0 (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (t : ℝ) (x a b : B) : M ![t • x, a, b] = t * M ![x, a, b] := by
3270  have h := M.map_update_smul ![x, a, b] 0 t x
3271  have e : ∀ z : B, Function.update (![x, a, b] : Fin 3 → B) 0 z = ![z, a, b] := by
3272    intro z; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
3273  simpa [e] using h
3274
3275lemma integrable_termC (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3276    (w : Fin N → B) (cc u v : B) :
3277    Integrable (fun ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) ![cc, u, v]) P := by
3278  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
3279  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3280  have hcont3 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
3281  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w z) ![cc, u, v])
3282    ?_ (C := K * (‖cc‖ * ‖u‖ * ‖v‖)) (k := 0) (fun z => ?_)
3283  · exact (ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin 3 => B) ℝ ![cc, u, v]).continuous.comp
3284      (hcont3.comp (Tmap w).continuous)
3285  · have := abs_iter_le (hK 3 (le_refl _) (Tmap w z)) ![cc, u, v]
3286    simp only [Fin.prod_univ_succ, Fin.prod_univ_zero, Matrix.cons_val_zero, Matrix.cons_val_succ,
3287      mul_one] at this
3288    simpa [mul_assoc] using this

3290lemma fin2_B (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3291    (w : Fin N → B) (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) :
3292    ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))
3293        ![Dfin ξ N c p.2, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2)] ∂(P.prod P) =
3294      2 * ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N,
3295        ∫ ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) ![c i j, w i, w j] ∂P := by
3296  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3297  rw [integral_prod _ (integrable_prodB hξ hΦ w c)]
3298  have inner : ∀ x : Ω, ∫ y, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x))
3299      ![Dfin ξ N c y, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y)] ∂P =
3300      2 * ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N,
3301        iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x)) ![c i j, w i, w j] := by
3302    intro x
3303    set M := iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x)) with hM
3304    have e : ∀ y, M ![Dfin ξ N c y, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y)] =
3305        ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ j : Fin N, (ξ i y * ξ j y - if i = j then 1 else 0) *
3306          M ![c i j, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y)] := by
3307      intro y
3308      simp only [Dfin]
3309      rw [tri_sum0]
3310      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3311      rw [tri_sum0]
3312      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3313      rw [tri_smul0]
3314    simp_rw [e]
3315    rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j _ =>
3316      integrable_zz_quad hξ w M (c i j) i j)]
3317    rw [Finset.mul_sum]
3318    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3319    rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun j _ => integrable_zz_quad hξ w M (c i j) i j), Finset.mul_sum]
3320    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
3321    rw [fin2_termB hξ w M (c i j) i j, hM, iter3_swap23 hΦ3 _ (c i j) (w j) (w i)]
3322    ring
3323  simp_rw [inner]
3324  rw [integral_const_mul]
3325  congr 1
3326  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun j _ =>
3327    integrable_termC hξ hΦ w (c i j) (w i) (w j))]
3328  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3329  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun j _ => integrable_termC hξ hΦ w (c i j) (w i) (w j))]
3330
3331lemma fin2_identity (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
3332    (w : Fin N → B) (c : Fin N → Fin N → B) :
3333    ∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) (Dfin ξ N c ω) ∂P =
3334      (1 / 2) * ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))
3335        ![Dfin ξ N c p.2, Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2), Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2)] ∂(P.prod P) := by
3336  rw [fin2_A hξ hΦ w c, fin2_B hξ hΦ w c]
3337  ring

3339end Assemble2

3347section Moment4
3348variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
3349  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
3350
3351lemma three_quarters_gt : (2 : ℝ≥0∞)⁻¹ < 3 / 4 := by
3352  rw [ENNReal.inv_lt_iff_inv_lt]
3353  have : (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞)⁻¹ = 4 / 3 := by
3354    rw [ENNReal.inv_div (by norm_num) (by norm_num)]
3355  rw [this, ENNReal.div_lt_iff (by norm_num) (by norm_num)]
3356  norm_num
3357
3358lemma three_quarters_lt : (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) < 1 := by
3359  rw [ENNReal.div_lt_iff (by norm_num) (by norm_num)]
3360  norm_num
3361
3362lemma three_ratio : ((3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) / (1 - 3 / 4)).toReal = 3 := by
3363  have h34 : (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) = ENNReal.ofReal (3 / 4) := by
3364    rw [ENNReal.ofReal_div_of_pos (by norm_num)]; simp
3365  have h14 : (1 - 3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) = ENNReal.ofReal (1 / 4) := by
3366    rw [h34, ← ENNReal.ofReal_one, ← ENNReal.ofReal_sub _ (by norm_num)]
3367    norm_num
3368  rw [h14, h34, ENNReal.toReal_div, ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by norm_num),
3369    ENNReal.toReal_ofReal (by norm_num)]
3370  norm_num
3371
3372/-- The Fernique constant for `c = 3/4`. -/
3373def fernK1 : ℝ≥0∞ :=
3374  ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (logRatio (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞))) +
3375    ∑' n : ℕ, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (-2⁻¹ * Real.log ((3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) / (1 - 3 / 4)).toReal * 2 ^ n))
3376
3377lemma fernK1_lt_top : fernK1 < ∞ := by
3378  refine ENNReal.add_lt_top.mpr ⟨ENNReal.ofReal_lt_top, ?_⟩
3379  refine Summable.tsum_ofReal_lt_top <|
3380    Real.summable_exp_nat_mul_of_ge ?_ (fun i ↦ mod_cast (Nat.lt_pow_self (by simp)).le)
3381  refine mul_neg_of_neg_of_pos (by simp) (Real.log_pos ?_)
3382  rw [three_ratio]; norm_num
3383
3384lemma logRatio_pos' : 0 < logRatio (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) := logRatio_pos three_quarters_gt three_quarters_lt
3385
3386/-- Moment-4 bound for centered Gaussian measures in terms of the second moment. -/
3387lemma gaussian_moment4_le {μ : Measure B} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ] (hμ : IsCenteredGaussian μ) :
3388    ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂μ ≤
3389      ENNReal.ofReal (32 / logRatio (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) ^ 2) * fernK1 * (∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ) ^ 2 := by
3390  set L := logRatio (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) with hL
3391  have hLpos : 0 < L := logRatio_pos'
3392  set m := ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ with hm
3393  have hmfin : m < ∞ := by
3394    have := (gaussian_sqnorm_integrable hμ)
3395    have h2 := this.lintegral_lt_top
3396    rw [hm]
3397    refine lt_of_le_of_lt (le_of_eq ?_) h2
3398    refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
3399    rw [← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _)]
3400  rcases eq_or_ne m 0 with h0 | h0
3401  · have hae : ∀ᵐ x ∂μ, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 2 = 0 := by
3402      have := (lintegral_eq_zero_iff (by fun_prop)).1 h0
3403      exact this
3404    have : ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂μ = 0 := by
3405      refine lintegral_eq_zero_iff (by fun_prop) |>.2 ?_
3406      filter_upwards [hae] with x hx
3407      have : ‖x‖ₑ = 0 := by simpa using hx
3408      simp [this]
3409    rw [this]; simp
3410  · have hmpos : 0 < m.toReal := ENNReal.toReal_pos h0 hmfin.ne
3411    set s := m.toReal with hs
3412    set a : ℝ := 2 * Real.sqrt s with ha_def
3413    have ha : 0 < a := by positivity
3414    have ha2 : a ^ 2 = 4 * s := by
3415      rw [ha_def, mul_pow, Real.sq_sqrt hmpos.le]; norm_num
3416    have hms : ENNReal.ofReal s = m := ENNReal.ofReal_toReal hmfin.ne
3417    -- Markov
3418    have hS : MeasurableSet {x : B | a < ‖x‖} := measurableSet_lt measurable_const measurable_norm
3419    have hmark : 4 * m * μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖} ≤ m := by
3420      have h1 := mul_meas_ge_le_lintegral₀ (μ := μ) (f := fun x : B => ‖x‖ₑ ^ 2)
3421        (by fun_prop) (4 * m)
3422      refine le_trans (mul_le_mul' le_rfl (measure_mono ?_)) h1
3423      intro x hx
3424      simp only [Set.mem_ofPred_eq] at hx ⊢
3425      have : (4 * m) = ENNReal.ofReal (a ^ 2) := by
3426        rw [ha2, ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by norm_num), hms]; norm_num
3427      rw [this, ← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _)]
3428      exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (pow_le_pow_left₀ ha.le hx.le 2)
3429    have hcompl : 4 * μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖} ≤ 1 := by
3430      have : (4 * μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖}) * m ≤ 1 * m := by
3431        rw [one_mul]; calc _ = 4 * m * μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖} := by ring
3432          _ ≤ m := hmark
3433      exact (ENNReal.mul_le_mul_iff_left h0 hmfin.ne).1 this
3434    have hc : (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) ≤ μ {x : B | ‖x‖ ≤ a} := by
3435      have e : {x : B | ‖x‖ ≤ a} = {x : B | a < ‖x‖}ᶜ := by
3436        ext x; simp
3437      rw [e, prob_compl_eq_one_sub hS]
3438      refine ENNReal.le_sub_of_add_le_right (measure_ne_top _ _) ?_
3439      have h4 : (4 : ℝ≥0∞) * (3 / 4 + μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖}) ≤ 4 * 1 := by
3440        rw [mul_add, mul_one]
3441        have : (4 : ℝ≥0∞) * (3 / 4) = 3 := by
3442          rw [mul_comm, ENNReal.div_mul_cancel (by norm_num) (by norm_num)]
3443        rw [this]
3444        calc (3 : ℝ≥0∞) + 4 * μ {x : B | a < ‖x‖} ≤ 3 + 1 := by gcongr
3445          _ = 4 := by norm_num
3446      exact (ENNReal.mul_le_mul_iff_right (by norm_num) (by norm_num)).1 h4
3447    have := hμ.1
3448    have h_rot := IsGaussian.map_rotation_eq_self (μ := μ) (gaussian_integral_id_eq_zero hμ) (-(Real.pi / 4))
3449    have hF := lintegral_exp_mul_sq_norm_le_of_map_rotation_eq_self (a := a) h_rot hc three_quarters_gt
3450    have hF' : ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2)) ∂μ ≤ fernK1 := hF
3451    -- pointwise bound
3452    set C : ℝ := 32 / L ^ 2 * s ^ 2 with hC
3453    have hpt : ∀ x : B, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 4 ≤
3454        ENNReal.ofReal C * ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2)) := by
3455      intro x
3456      rw [← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _), ← ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity)]
3457      refine ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal ?_
3458      set t : ℝ := L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 with ht
3459      have htpos : 0 < t := by positivity
3460      have hy := Real.quadratic_le_exp_of_nonneg (x := t * ‖x‖ ^ 2) (by positivity)
3461      have e1 : C = 2 / t ^ 2 := by
3462        rw [hC, ht, mul_pow, inv_pow, ha2]
3463        field_simp
3464        ring
3465      rw [e1, show L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2 = t * ‖x‖ ^ 2 from by rw [ht]]
3466      rw [div_mul_eq_mul_div, le_div_iff₀ (by positivity)]
3467      nlinarith [sq_nonneg (t * ‖x‖ ^ 2), mul_pos htpos htpos, norm_nonneg x, pow_nonneg (norm_nonneg x) 2]
3468    calc ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂μ
3469        ≤ ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal C * ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2)) ∂μ :=
3470          lintegral_mono hpt
3471      _ = ENNReal.ofReal C * ∫⁻ x, ENNReal.ofReal (Real.exp (L * a⁻¹ ^ 2 * ‖x‖ ^ 2)) ∂μ :=
3472          lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
3473      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal C * fernK1 := by gcongr
3474      _ = ENNReal.ofReal (32 / L ^ 2) * fernK1 * m ^ 2 := by
3475          rw [hC, ENNReal.ofReal_mul (by positivity), ENNReal.ofReal_pow hmpos.le, hms]
3476          ring
3477
3478end Moment4
3479
3480end Aux_s9

3485section HolderPart

3488instance holderTriple_442 : ENNReal.HolderTriple 4 4 2 where

3494instance holderTriple_221 : ENNReal.HolderTriple 2 2 1 where

3498lemma lintegral_mul3_le {E1 E2 E3 : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E1] [NormedAddCommGroup E2]
3499    [NormedAddCommGroup E3] {f1 : Ω → E1} {f2 : Ω → E2} {f3 : Ω → E3}
3500    (h1 : AEStronglyMeasurable f1 P) (h2 : AEStronglyMeasurable f2 P)
3501    (h3 : AEStronglyMeasurable f3 P) :
3502    ∫⁻ ω, ‖f1 ω‖ₑ * ‖f2 ω‖ₑ * ‖f3 ω‖ₑ ∂P ≤ eLpNorm f1 2 P * eLpNorm f2 4 P * eLpNorm f3 4 P := by
3503  have hA : eLpNorm (fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖) 2 P ≤ eLpNorm f2 4 P * eLpNorm f3 4 P := by
3504    have := eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_mul_eLpNorm_of_norm (p := 4) (q := 4) (r := 2) (μ := P)
3505      (f := fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖) (g := fun ω => ‖f3 ω‖) (fun a b : ℝ => a * b) 1
3506      (by fun_prop) h2.norm h3.norm (by simp)
3507    rw [eLpNorm_norm _ h2, eLpNorm_norm _ h3] at this
3508    simpa using this
3509  have hB : eLpNorm (fun ω => ‖f1 ω‖ * (‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖)) 1 P ≤
3510      eLpNorm f1 2 P * eLpNorm (fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖) 2 P := by
3511    have := eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_mul_eLpNorm_of_norm (p := 2) (q := 2) (r := 1) (μ := P)
3512      (f := fun ω => ‖f1 ω‖) (g := fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖) (fun a b : ℝ => a * b) 1
3513      (by fun_prop) h1.norm (h2.norm.mul h3.norm) (by simp)
3514    rw [eLpNorm_norm _ h1] at this
3515    simpa using this
3516  rw [eLpNorm_one_eq_lintegral_enorm] at hB
3517  calc ∫⁻ ω, ‖f1 ω‖ₑ * ‖f2 ω‖ₑ * ‖f3 ω‖ₑ ∂P
3518      = ∫⁻ ω, ‖‖f1 ω‖ * (‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖)‖ₑ ∂P := by
3519        refine lintegral_congr fun ω => ?_
3520        simp [enorm_mul, mul_assoc]
3521    _ ≤ eLpNorm f1 2 P * eLpNorm (fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖ * ‖f3 ω‖) 2 P := hB
3522    _ ≤ eLpNorm f1 2 P * (eLpNorm f2 4 P * eLpNorm f3 4 P) := by gcongr
3523    _ = _ := by ring
3524  exact h1.norm.mul (h2.norm.mul h3.norm)
3525
3526lemma lintegral_mul2_le {E1 E2 : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E1] [NormedAddCommGroup E2]
3527    {f1 : Ω → E1} {f2 : Ω → E2}
3528    (h1 : AEStronglyMeasurable f1 P) (h2 : AEStronglyMeasurable f2 P) :
3529    ∫⁻ ω, ‖f1 ω‖ₑ * ‖f2 ω‖ₑ ∂P ≤ eLpNorm f1 2 P * eLpNorm f2 2 P := by
3530  have := eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_mul_eLpNorm_of_norm (p := 2) (q := 2) (r := 1) (μ := P)
3531      (f := fun ω => ‖f1 ω‖) (g := fun ω => ‖f2 ω‖) (fun a b : ℝ => a * b) 1
3532      (by fun_prop) h1.norm h2.norm (by simp)
3533  rw [eLpNorm_one_eq_lintegral_enorm] at this
3534  rw [eLpNorm_norm _ h1, eLpNorm_norm _ h2] at this
3535  · simpa [enorm_mul] using this
3536  · exact h1.norm.mul h2.norm
3537
3538end HolderPart
3539
3540end Aux_s10

3542section Aux_s11
3543open ProbabilityTheory

3545section PointwiseBounds
3546variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B]
3547
3548lemma tri_add1 (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (x x' y z : B) :
3549    M ![x + x', y, z] = M ![x, y, z] + M ![x', y, z] := by
3550  have h := M.map_update_add ![x, y, z] 0 x x'
3551  have e : ∀ w : B, Function.update (![x, y, z] : Fin 3 → B) 0 w = ![w, y, z] := by
3552    intro w; ext i; fin_cases i <;> simp [Function.update]
3553  simpa [e] using h
3554
3555lemma tri_sub1 (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (x x' y z : B) :
3556    M ![x - x', y, z] = M ![x, y, z] - M ![x', y, z] := by
3557  have := tri_add1 M (x - x') x' y z
3558  rw [sub_add_cancel] at this
3559  linarith
3560
3561lemma tri_sub2 (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (c x x' y : B) :
3562    M ![c, x - x', y] = M ![c, x, y] - M ![c, x', y] := by
3563  have := tri_add2 M c (x - x') x' y
3564  rw [sub_add_cancel] at this
3565  linarith
3566
3567lemma tri_sub3 (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (c x y y' : B) :
3568    M ![c, x, y - y'] = M ![c, x, y] - M ![c, x, y'] := by
3569  have := tri_add3 M c x (y - y') y'
3570  rw [sub_add_cancel] at this
3571  linarith
3572
3573lemma tri_diff_bound (M M' : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (d d' g g' : B) :
3574    |M ![d, g, g] - M' ![d', g', g']| ≤
3575      ‖M - M'‖ * (‖d'‖ * ‖g'‖ * ‖g'‖) +
3576        ‖M‖ * (‖d - d'‖ * ‖g‖ * ‖g‖ + ‖d'‖ * ‖g - g'‖ * ‖g‖ + ‖d'‖ * ‖g'‖ * ‖g - g'‖) := by
3577  have e : M ![d, g, g] - M' ![d', g', g'] =
3578      (M - M') ![d', g', g'] + (M ![d - d', g, g] + M ![d', g - g', g] + M ![d', g', g - g']) := by
3579    rw [tri_sub1, tri_sub2, tri_sub3]
3580    simp only [sub_apply]
3581    ring
3582  rw [e]
3583  have h1 := abs_tri_le (M - M') d' g' g'
3584  have h2 := abs_tri_le M (d - d') g g
3585  have h3 := abs_tri_le M d' (g - g') g
3586  have h4 := abs_tri_le M d' g' (g - g')
3587  have a1 := abs_add_le ((M - M') ![d', g', g']) (M ![d - d', g, g] + M ![d', g - g', g] + M ![d', g', g - g'])
3588  have a2 := abs_add_le (M ![d - d', g, g] + M ![d', g - g', g]) (M ![d', g', g - g'])
3589  have a3 := abs_add_le (M ![d - d', g, g]) (M ![d', g - g', g])
3590  nlinarith
3591
3592lemma enorm_tri_diff_le (M M' : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (d d' g g' : B) :
3593    ‖M ![d, g, g] - M' ![d', g', g']‖ₑ ≤
3594      ‖M - M'‖ₑ * (‖d'‖ₑ * ‖g'‖ₑ * ‖g'‖ₑ) +
3595        ‖M‖ₑ * (‖d - d'‖ₑ * ‖g‖ₑ * ‖g‖ₑ + ‖d'‖ₑ * ‖g - g'‖ₑ * ‖g‖ₑ + ‖d'‖ₑ * ‖g'‖ₑ * ‖g - g'‖ₑ) := by
3596  have h := tri_diff_bound M M' d d' g g'
3597  calc ‖M ![d, g, g] - M' ![d', g', g']‖ₑ
3598      = ENNReal.ofReal |M ![d, g, g] - M' ![d', g', g']| := by
3599        rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, ofReal_norm]
3600    _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h
3601    _ = _ := by
3602        simp (disch := positivity) only [ENNReal.ofReal_add, ENNReal.ofReal_mul, ofReal_norm]
3603
3604/-- Lipschitz bound for iterated derivatives. -/
3605lemma lip_iter {Φ : B → ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ (k + 1) Φ) {K : ℝ}
3606    (hK : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ (k + 1) Φ y‖ ≤ K) (x y : B) :
3607    ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ x - iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ y‖ ≤ K * ‖x - y‖ := by
3608  have hd : ∀ z : B, DifferentiableAt ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) z := fun z =>
3609    hΦ.differentiable_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast Nat.lt_succ_self k) z
3610  have := (convex_univ (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B)).norm_image_sub_le_of_norm_fderiv_le
3611    (f := iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) (C := K) (fun z _ => hd z)
3612    (fun z _ => by rw [norm_fderiv_iteratedFDeriv]; exact hK z) (Set.mem_univ y) (Set.mem_univ x)
3613  exact this
3614
3615lemma abs_fderiv_diff_le {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Φ) {K1 K2 : ℝ}
3616    (hK1 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y‖ ≤ K1) (hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K2)
3617    (y y' d d' : B) :
3618    |fderiv ℝ Φ y d - fderiv ℝ Φ y' d'| ≤ K1 * ‖d - d'‖ + K2 * (‖y - y'‖ * ‖d'‖) := by
3619  have e : fderiv ℝ Φ y d - fderiv ℝ Φ y' d' =
3620      fderiv ℝ Φ y (d - d') + (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y') ![d'] := by
3621    simp only [map_sub, sub_apply]
3622    simp
3623  rw [e]
3624  refine (abs_add_le _ _).trans (add_le_add (abs_fderiv_le hK1 _ _) ?_)
3625  have hl : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y'‖ ≤ K2 * ‖y - y'‖ :=
3626    lip_iter (k := 1) hΦ (fun z => hK2 z) y y'
3627  have := (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y').le_opNorm ![d']
3628  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
3629  refine this.trans ?_
3630  simp only [Finset.univ_unique, Fin.default_eq_zero, Finset.prod_singleton, Matrix.cons_val_zero]
3631  calc _ ≤ K2 * ‖y - y'‖ * ‖d'‖ := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hl (norm_nonneg _)
3632    _ = _ := by ring
3633
3634lemma enorm_fderiv_diff_le {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 2 Φ) {K1 K2 : ℝ} (hK10 : 0 ≤ K1)
3635    (hK20 : 0 ≤ K2)
3636    (hK1 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y‖ ≤ K1) (hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K2)
3637    (y y' d d' : B) :
3638    ‖fderiv ℝ Φ y d - fderiv ℝ Φ y' d'‖ₑ ≤
3639      ENNReal.ofReal K1 * ‖d - d'‖ₑ + ENNReal.ofReal K2 * (‖y - y'‖ₑ * ‖d'‖ₑ) := by
3640  have h := abs_fderiv_diff_le hΦ hK1 hK2 y y' d d'
3641  calc ‖fderiv ℝ Φ y d - fderiv ℝ Φ y' d'‖ₑ
3642      = ENNReal.ofReal |fderiv ℝ Φ y d - fderiv ℝ Φ y' d'| := by
3643        rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, ofReal_norm]
3644    _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h
3645    _ = _ := by
3646        simp (disch := positivity) only [ENNReal.ofReal_add, ENNReal.ofReal_mul, ofReal_norm]
3647
3648end PointwiseBounds

3652section Aux_s13

3655section Abstract
3656variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
3657  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
3658variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P]
3659
3660lemma tendsto_integral_of_lintegral {α E : Type*} [MeasurableSpace α] {μ : Measure α}
3661    [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [CompleteSpace E]
3662    {F : ℕ → α → E} {f : α → E} (hf : Integrable f μ) (hFm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (F m) μ)
3663    (h : Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ x, ‖F m x - f x‖ₑ ∂μ) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
3664    Tendsto (fun m => ∫ x, F m x ∂μ) atTop (𝓝 (∫ x, f x ∂μ)) := by
3665  refine tendsto_integral_of_L1 f hf.1 ?_ h
3666  filter_upwards [h.eventually (gt_mem_nhds zero_lt_one)] with m hm
3667  have h1 : Integrable (fun x => F m x - f x) μ :=
3668    ⟨(hFm m).sub hf.1, by
3669      simpa [HasFiniteIntegral] using hm.trans ENNReal.one_lt_top⟩
3670  exact (h1.add hf).congr (Eventually.of_forall fun x => by simp)
3671
3672lemma tendsto_zero_of_le {f g : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞} (hg : Tendsto g atTop (𝓝 0)) (h : ∀ m, f m ≤ g m) :
3673    Tendsto f atTop (𝓝 0) :=
3674  tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le tendsto_const_nhds hg (fun _ => zero_le) h
3675
3676lemma tendsto_zero_of_eventually_le {f g : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞} (hg : Tendsto g atTop (𝓝 0))
3677    (h : ∀ᶠ m in atTop, f m ≤ g m) : Tendsto f atTop (𝓝 0) :=
3678  tendsto_of_tendsto_of_tendsto_of_le_of_le' tendsto_const_nhds hg
3679    (Eventually.of_forall fun _ => zero_le) h
3680
3681lemma second_lhs_tendsto {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
3682    {G D : Ω → B} {Gm Dm : ℕ → Ω → B}
3683    (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P) (hD : AEStronglyMeasurable D P)
3684    (hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Gm m) P) (hDm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Dm m) P)
3685    (hG4 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
3686    (hD2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
3687    (hD2fin : eLpNorm D 2 P < ∞)
3688    (hint1 : Integrable (fun ω => fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω)) P) :
3689    Tendsto (fun m => ∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (Gm m ω) (Dm m ω) ∂P) atTop
3690      (𝓝 (∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω) ∂P)) := by
3691  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
3692  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3693  have hΦ2 : ContDiff ℝ 2 Φ := hΦ3.of_le (by norm_num)
3694  have hcont : Continuous (Function.uncurry fun a b : B => fderiv ℝ Φ a b) :=
3695    (isBoundedBilinearMap_apply (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B) (F := ℝ)).continuous.comp
3696      (((hΦ3.continuous_fderiv (by norm_num)).comp continuous_fst).prodMk continuous_snd)
3697  have hmeas : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => fderiv ℝ Φ (Gm m ω) (Dm m ω)) P := fun m =>
3698    hcont.comp_aestronglyMeasurable₂ (hGm m) (hDm m)
3699  have hmeas0 : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω)) P :=
3700    hcont.comp_aestronglyMeasurable₂ hG hD
3701  refine tendsto_integral_of_lintegral hint1 hmeas ?_
3702  have hK1 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 1 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 1 (by norm_num) y
3703  have hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 2 (by norm_num) y
3704  have hpt : ∀ m ω, ‖fderiv ℝ Φ (Gm m ω) (Dm m ω) - fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω)‖ₑ ≤
3705      ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ + ENNReal.ofReal K * (‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖D ω‖ₑ) :=
3706    fun m ω => enorm_fderiv_diff_le hΦ2 hK0 hK0 hK1 hK2 _ _ _ _
3707  have hbound : ∀ m, ∫⁻ ω, ‖fderiv ℝ Φ (Gm m ω) (Dm m ω) - fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω)‖ₑ ∂P ≤
3708      ENNReal.ofReal K * eLpNorm (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) 2 P +
3709        ENNReal.ofReal K * (eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P * eLpNorm D 2 P) := by
3710    intro m
3711    have hD' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) P := (hDm m).sub hD
3712    have hG' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) P := (hGm m).sub hG
3713    calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ ω, (ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ +
3714            ENNReal.ofReal K * (‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖D ω‖ₑ)) ∂P := lintegral_mono (hpt m)
3715      _ = ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ ∂P +
3716            ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖D ω‖ₑ ∂P := by
3717          rw [lintegral_add_left' (hD'.enorm.const_mul _), lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top,
3718            lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
3719      _ ≤ _ := by
3720          gcongr
3721          · rw [← eLpNorm_one_eq_lintegral_enorm hD']
3722            exact eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le (by norm_num)
3723          · refine (lintegral_mul2_le hG' hD).trans ?_
3724            gcongr
3725            exact eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le (by norm_num)
3726  refine tendsto_zero_of_le ?_ hbound
3727  have h1 : Tendsto (fun m => ENNReal.ofReal K * eLpNorm (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3728    simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul hD2 (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
3729  have h2 : Tendsto (fun m => ENNReal.ofReal K * (eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P * eLpNorm D 2 P))
3730      atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3731    have := ENNReal.Tendsto.mul_const hG4 (Or.inr hD2fin.ne)
3732    simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul this (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
3733  simpa using h1.add h2
3734
3735end Abstract
3736
3737end Aux_s13

3739section Aux_s14

3742section Abstract2
3743variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
3744  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
3745variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P]

3747lemma continuous_trieval :
3748    Continuous (fun x : (B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) × (B × B × B) => x.1 ![x.2.1, x.2.2.1, x.2.2.2]) := by
3749  refine ContinuousEval.continuous_eval.comp (continuous_fst.prodMk (continuous_pi fun k => ?_))
3750  fin_cases k
3751  · exact continuous_snd.fst
3752  · exact continuous_snd.snd.fst
3753  · exact continuous_snd.snd.snd
3754
3755lemma lintegral_prod_snd' {g : Ω → ℝ≥0∞} (hg : AEMeasurable g P) :
3756    ∫⁻ p : Ω × Ω, g p.2 ∂(P.prod P) = ∫⁻ ω, g ω ∂P := by
3757  have := lintegral_prod_mul (μ := P) (ν := P) (f := fun _ => (1 : ℝ≥0∞)) (g := g)
3758    aemeasurable_const hg
3759  simpa using this

3761lemma aesm_tri {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) {G D H : Ω → B} (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P)
3762    (hD : AEStronglyMeasurable D P) (hH : AEStronglyMeasurable H P) :
3763    AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, H p.2, H p.2])
3764      (P.prod P) := by
3765  have hc : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
3766  have h1 : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (P.prod P) :=
3767    hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable hG.comp_fst
3768  exact continuous_trieval.comp_aestronglyMeasurable
3769    (h1.prodMk (hD.comp_snd.prodMk (hH.comp_snd.prodMk hH.comp_snd)))
3770
3771lemma second_rhs_tendsto {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
3772    {G D : Ω → B} {Gm Dm : ℕ → Ω → B}
3773    (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P) (hD : AEStronglyMeasurable D P)
3774    (hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Gm m) P) (hDm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Dm m) P)
3775    (hae : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun m => Gm m ω) atTop (𝓝 (G ω)))
3776    (hG4 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
3777    (hD2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
3778    (hG4fin : eLpNorm G 4 P < ∞) (hD2fin : eLpNorm D 2 P < ∞)
3779    (hint3 : Integrable
3780      (fun p : Ω × Ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2]) (P.prod P)) :
3781    Tendsto (fun m => ∫ p : Ω × Ω,
3782      iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1) ![Dm m p.2, Gm m p.2, Gm m p.2] ∂(P.prod P)) atTop
3783      (𝓝 (∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2] ∂(P.prod P))) := by
3784  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
3785  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
3786  have hK3 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 3 le_rfl y
3787  have hc : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
3788  refine tendsto_integral_of_lintegral hint3 (fun m => aesm_tri hΦ3 (hGm m) (hDm m) (hGm m)) ?_
3789  -- the pieces
3790  set a : ℕ → Ω → ℝ≥0∞ :=
3791    fun m ω => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω) - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ with ha_def
3792  set g0 : Ω → ℝ≥0∞ := fun ω => ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ with hg0_def
3793  set g1 : ℕ → Ω → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m ω => ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ +
3794    ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ + ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ with hg1_def
3795  have ha_meas : ∀ m, AEMeasurable (a m) P := fun m =>
3796    ((hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable (hGm m)).sub (hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable hG)).enorm
3797  have hg0_meas : AEMeasurable g0 P := (hD.enorm.mul hG.enorm).mul hG.enorm
3798  have hg1_meas : ∀ m, AEMeasurable (g1 m) P := fun m => by
3799    have h1 := ((hDm m).sub hD).enorm
3800    have h2 := (hGm m).enorm
3801    have h3 := ((hGm m).sub hG).enorm
3802    have h4 := hD.enorm
3803    have h5 := hG.enorm
3804    exact (((h1.mul h2).mul h2).add ((h4.mul h3).mul h2)).add ((h4.mul h5).mul h3)
3805  have hpt : ∀ m (p : Ω × Ω),
3806      ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1) ![Dm m p.2, Gm m p.2, Gm m p.2] -
3807        iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2]‖ₑ ≤
3808        a m p.1 * g0 p.2 + ENNReal.ofReal K * g1 m p.2 := by
3809    intro m p
3810    refine (enorm_tri_diff_le _ _ _ _ _ _).trans ?_
3811    have hKe : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
3812      rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
3813    gcongr
3814  have hint : ∀ m, ∫⁻ p : Ω × Ω, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1) ![Dm m p.2, Gm m p.2, Gm m p.2] -
3815        iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2]‖ₑ ∂(P.prod P) ≤
3816      (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, g0 ω ∂P) + ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, g1 m ω ∂P := by
3817    intro m
3818    calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ p : Ω × Ω, (a m p.1 * g0 p.2 + ENNReal.ofReal K * g1 m p.2) ∂(P.prod P) :=
3819          lintegral_mono (hpt m)
3820      _ = _ := by
3821          have hAE : AEMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => a m p.1 * g0 p.2) (P.prod P) :=
3822            (ha_meas m).comp_fst.mul hg0_meas.comp_snd
3823          rw [lintegral_add_left' hAE,
3824            lintegral_prod_mul (ha_meas m) hg0_meas,
3825            lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top, lintegral_prod_snd' (hg1_meas m)]
3826  -- finiteness of the fixed factor
3827  have hg0_fin : ∫⁻ ω, g0 ω ∂P ≠ ∞ :=
3828    ne_top_of_le_ne_top (ENNReal.mul_lt_top (ENNReal.mul_lt_top hD2fin hG4fin) hG4fin).ne
3829      (lintegral_mul3_le hD hG hG)
3830  -- dominated convergence for the derivative part
3831  have ha_lim : Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3832    have := tendsto_lintegral_of_dominated_convergence' (μ := P) (F := a)
3833      (f := fun _ => (0 : ℝ≥0∞)) (fun _ => 2 * ENNReal.ofReal K) ha_meas ?_ ?_ ?_
3834    · simpa using this
3835    · intro m
3836      refine Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_
3837      have h1 : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
3838        rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
3839      have h2 : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
3840        rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
3841      calc a m ω ≤ ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω)‖ₑ + ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ :=
3842            enorm_sub_le
3843        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K + ENNReal.ofReal K := add_le_add h1 h2
3844        _ = 2 * ENNReal.ofReal K := by rw [two_mul]
3845    · simp [ENNReal.mul_eq_top]
3846    · filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω
3847      exact tendsto_iff_enorm_sub_tendsto_zero.1 ((hc.tendsto (G ω)).comp hω)
3848  -- L^4 bound on `Gm`
3849  have hev : ∀ᶠ m in atTop, eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P ≤ eLpNorm G 4 P + 1 := by
3850    filter_upwards [hG4.eventually (gt_mem_nhds zero_lt_one)] with m hm
3851    have : Gm m = (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) + G := by ext ω; simp
3852    calc eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P = eLpNorm ((fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) + G) 4 P := by rw [← this]
3853      _ ≤ eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P + eLpNorm G 4 P :=
3854          eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)
3855      _ ≤ 1 + eLpNorm G 4 P := by gcongr
3856      _ = _ := add_comm _ _
3857  set S : ℝ≥0∞ := eLpNorm G 4 P + 1 with hS
3858  have hSfin : S ≠ ∞ := (ENNReal.add_lt_top.2 ⟨hG4fin, ENNReal.one_lt_top⟩).ne
3859  set x : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) 2 P with hx
3860  set y : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P with hy
3861  set C1 : ℝ≥0∞ := S * S with hC1
3862  set C2 : ℝ≥0∞ := eLpNorm D 2 P * S + eLpNorm D 2 P * eLpNorm G 4 P with hC2
3863  have hC1fin : C1 ≠ ∞ := ENNReal.mul_ne_top hSfin hSfin
3864  have hC2fin : C2 ≠ ∞ :=
3865    ENNReal.add_ne_top.2 ⟨ENNReal.mul_ne_top hD2fin.ne hSfin, ENNReal.mul_ne_top hD2fin.ne hG4fin.ne⟩
3866  have hg1_le : ∀ᶠ m in atTop, ∫⁻ ω, g1 m ω ∂P ≤ x m * C1 + C2 * y m := by
3867    filter_upwards [hev] with m hm
3868    have hD' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Dm m ω - D ω) P := (hDm m).sub hD
3869    have hG' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) P := (hGm m).sub hG
3870    have m1 := lintegral_mul3_le hD' (hGm m) (hGm m)
3871    have m2 := lintegral_mul3_le hD hG' (hGm m)
3872    have m3 := lintegral_mul3_le hD hG hG'
3873    have e1 : AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ) P :=
3874      (hD'.enorm.mul (hGm m).enorm).mul (hGm m).enorm
3875    have e2 : AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ) P :=
3876      (hD.enorm.mul hG'.enorm).mul (hGm m).enorm
3877    have e3 : AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ) P :=
3878      (hD.enorm.mul hG.enorm).mul hG'.enorm
3879    calc ∫⁻ ω, g1 m ω ∂P
3880        = ∫⁻ ω, ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ ∂P +
3881          ∫⁻ ω, ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ ∂P +
3882          ∫⁻ ω, ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ ∂P := by
3883          simp only [hg1_def]
3884          have e12 : AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖Dm m ω - D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ +
3885              ‖D ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ) P := e1.add e2
3886          rw [lintegral_add_left' e12, lintegral_add_left' e1]
3887      _ ≤ x m * (eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P * eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P) +
3888          eLpNorm D 2 P * y m * eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P + eLpNorm D 2 P * eLpNorm G 4 P * y m := by
3889          rw [← mul_assoc]; gcongr
3890      _ ≤ x m * (S * S) + eLpNorm D 2 P * y m * S + eLpNorm D 2 P * eLpNorm G 4 P * y m := by
3891          have : eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P ≤ S := hm
3892          gcongr
3893      _ = x m * C1 + C2 * y m := by rw [hC1, hC2]; ring
3894  have hlim : Tendsto (fun m => (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, g0 ω ∂P) +
3895      ENNReal.ofReal K * (x m * C1 + C2 * y m)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3896    have t1 : Tendsto (fun m => (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, g0 ω ∂P)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3897      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.mul_const ha_lim (Or.inr hg0_fin)
3898    have t2 : Tendsto (fun m => x m * C1) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3899      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.mul_const hD2 (Or.inr hC1fin)
3900    have t3 : Tendsto (fun m => C2 * y m) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
3901      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul hG4 (Or.inr hC2fin)
3902    have t4 := ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul (a := ENNReal.ofReal K) (t2.add t3) (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
3903    simpa using t1.add t4
3904  refine tendsto_zero_of_eventually_le hlim ?_
3905  filter_upwards [hg1_le] with m hm
3906  exact (hint m).trans (by gcongr)
3907
3908end Abstract2

3910end Aux_s14

3915section LinPGen
3916
3917variable {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
3918variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}
3919
3920lemma eLpNorm_pow_eq {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] {f : Ω → E} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f P)
3921    {p : ℝ≥0∞} (hp0 : p ≠ 0)
3922    (hpt : p ≠ ∞) (n : ℕ) (hn : p.toReal = n) :
3923    eLpNorm f p P ^ n = ∫⁻ ω, ‖f ω‖ₑ ^ n ∂P := by
3924  rw [eLpNorm_eq_lintegral_rpow_enorm_toReal hp0 hpt hf, hn]
3925  have hn0 : (n : ℝ) ≠ 0 := by
3926    rw [← hn]; exact (ENNReal.toReal_pos hp0 hpt).ne'
3927  rw [← ENNReal.rpow_natCast, ← ENNReal.rpow_mul, one_div, inv_mul_cancel₀ hn0, ENNReal.rpow_one]
3928  simp [ENNReal.rpow_natCast]
3929
3930/-- The finite linear polynomial `∑_{i<n} ξ_i • f i`. -/
3931def linP (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → E) (ω : Ω) : E :=
3932  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ξ i ω • f i
3933
3934lemma linP_eq_Tmap (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → E) (ω : Ω) :
3935    linP ξ n f ω = Tmap (fun i : Fin n => f i) (Zvec ξ n ω) := by
3936  rw [Tmap_apply, linP, Finset.sum_range]
3937  rfl
3938
3939/-- Zero-padding of a coefficient sequence. -/
3940def padv (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → E) : ℕ → E := fun i => if i < n then f i else 0
3941
3942lemma linP_pad (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) {n N : ℕ} (h : n ≤ N) (f : ℕ → E) (ω : Ω) :
3943    linP ξ n f ω = linP ξ N (padv n f) ω := by
3944  unfold linP
3945  symm
3946  rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_subset_range.2 h)]
3947  · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
3948    simp [padv, Finset.mem_range.1 hi]
3949  · intro i _ hi
3950    have : ¬ i < n := fun hh => hi (Finset.mem_range.2 hh)
3951    simp [padv, this]
3952
3953lemma linP_sub (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (N : ℕ) (f f' : ℕ → E) (ω : Ω) :
3954    linP ξ N f ω - linP ξ N f' ω = linP ξ N (f - f') ω := by
3955  unfold linP
3956  rw [← Finset.sum_sub_distrib]
3957  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
3958  simp [smul_sub]
3959
3960lemma aesm_linP (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → E) :
3961    AEStronglyMeasurable (linP ξ n f) P := by
3962  have : linP ξ n f = fun ω => ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ξ i ω • f i := rfl
3963  rw [this]
3964  refine Finset.aestronglyMeasurable_fun_sum _ fun i _ => ?_
3965  exact (hξ.aemeasurable i).aestronglyMeasurable.smul aestronglyMeasurable_const
3966
3967lemma eLpNorm_linP_lt_top (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → E) :
3968    eLpNorm (linP ξ n f) 2 P < ∞ := by
3969  set w : Fin n → E := fun i => f i with hw
3970  set S : ℝ := ∑ i : Fin n, ‖w i‖ with hS
3971  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => norm_nonneg _
3972  have hint : Integrable (fun ω => ‖Tmap w (Zvec ξ n ω)‖ ^ 2) P := by
3973    refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ n (g := fun z => ‖Tmap w z‖ ^ 2)
3974      ((Tmap w).continuous.norm.pow 2) (C := S ^ 2) (k := 2) fun z => ?_
3975    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
3976    calc ‖Tmap w z‖ ^ 2 ≤ (S * ‖z‖) ^ 2 :=
3977          pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_Tmap_le w z) 2
3978      _ ≤ S ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
3979          rw [mul_pow]
3980          gcongr
3981          linarith
3982  have h1 := hint.lintegral_lt_top
3983  have hq : linP ξ n f = fun ω => Tmap w (Zvec ξ n ω) := funext fun ω => linP_eq_Tmap ξ n f ω
3984  have hs := aesm_linP hξ n f
3985  have : eLpNorm (linP ξ n f) 2 P ^ 2 < ∞ := by
3986    rw [eLpNorm_pow_eq hs (by norm_num) (by norm_num) 2 (by norm_num)]
3987    refine lt_of_le_of_lt (le_of_eq ?_) h1
3988    refine lintegral_congr fun ω => ?_
3989    rw [hq, ← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _)]
3990  by_contra hcon
3991  rw [not_lt, top_le_iff] at hcon
3992  rw [hcon] at this
3993  simp at this
3994
3995end LinPGen

3997section LinP
3998variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
3999  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
4000variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}
4001
4002lemma gpi_integral_coord (N : ℕ) (k : Fin N) : ∫ z, z k ∂(gpi N) = 0 := by
4003  have := pi_integral_eval N id continuous_id k
4004  simpa using this
4005
4006lemma gpi_integrable_coord (N : ℕ) (k : Fin N) : Integrable (fun z : Fin N → ℝ => z k) (gpi N) := by
4007  refine integrable_of_poly (C := 1) (k := 1) (continuous_apply k).aestronglyMeasurable fun z => ?_
4008  have := norm_le_pi_norm z k
4009  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
4010  simp only [one_mul, pow_one]
4011  linarith [norm_nonneg z]
4012
4013lemma isCenteredGaussian_Tmap (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (w : Fin N → B) :
4014    IsCenteredGaussian (P.map (fun ω => Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) := by
4015  have hmap : P.map (fun ω => Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)) = (gpi N).map (Tmap w) := by
4016    rw [← map_Zvec hξ N]
4017    exact ((Tmap w).continuous.measurable.aemeasurable.map_map_of_aemeasurable
4018      (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ N)).symm
4019  rw [hmap]
4020  refine ⟨inferInstance, fun L => ?_⟩
4021  rw [integral_map (Tmap w).continuous.measurable.aemeasurable L.continuous.aestronglyMeasurable]
4022  have e : ∀ z : Fin N → ℝ, L (Tmap w z) = ∑ k, z k * L (w k) := fun z => by
4023    rw [Tmap_apply, map_sum]; simp
4024  simp_rw [e]
4025  rw [integral_finset_sum _ (fun k _ => (gpi_integrable_coord N k).mul_const _)]
4026  simp [integral_mul_const, gpi_integral_coord]
4027
4028lemma isCenteredGaussian_linP (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ) (f : ℕ → B) :
4029    IsCenteredGaussian (P.map (linP ξ n f)) := by
4030  have : linP ξ n f = fun ω => Tmap (fun i : Fin n => f i) (Zvec ξ n ω) :=
4031    funext (linP_eq_Tmap ξ n f)
4032  rw [this]
4033  exact isCenteredGaussian_Tmap hξ _
4034
4035lemma isCenteredGaussian_linP_sub (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n n' : ℕ) (f f' : ℕ → B) :
4036    IsCenteredGaussian (P.map (fun ω => linP ξ n f ω - linP ξ n' f' ω)) := by
4037  have : (fun ω => linP ξ n f ω - linP ξ n' f' ω) = linP ξ (n + n') (padv n f - padv n' f') := by
4038    funext ω
4039    rw [linP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_right n n') f ω, linP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_left n' n) f' ω,
4040      linP_sub]
4041  rw [this]
4042  exact isCenteredGaussian_linP hξ _ _
4043
4044end LinP

4052section GaussL4
4053variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
4054  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
4055variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}
4056
4057lemma eLpNorm_four_pow {f : Ω → B} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f P) :
4058    eLpNorm f 4 P ^ 4 = ∫⁻ ω, ‖f ω‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂P :=
4059  eLpNorm_pow_eq hf (by norm_num) (by norm_num) 4 (by norm_num)
4060
4061lemma eLpNorm_two_pow {f : Ω → B} (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f P) :
4062    eLpNorm f 2 P ^ 2 = ∫⁻ ω, ‖f ω‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂P :=
4063  eLpNorm_pow_eq hf (by norm_num) (by norm_num) 2 (by norm_num)
4064
4065/-- The moment-4 constant. -/
4066def gK : ℝ≥0∞ := ENNReal.ofReal (32 / logRatio (3 / 4 : ℝ≥0∞) ^ 2) * fernK1
4067
4068lemma gK_ne_top : gK ≠ ∞ := ENNReal.mul_ne_top ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top fernK1_lt_top.ne
4069
4070lemma gaussian_lintegral_sq_lt_top {μ : Measure B} [IsProbabilityMeasure μ]
4071    (hμ : IsCenteredGaussian μ) : ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ < ∞ := by
4072  have h2 := (gaussian_sqnorm_integrable hμ).lintegral_lt_top
4073  refine lt_of_le_of_lt (le_of_eq ?_) h2
4074  refine lintegral_congr fun x => ?_
4075  rw [← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _)]
4076
4077lemma gauss_N4_le {X : Ω → B} (hX : AEMeasurable X P) (hg : IsCenteredGaussian (P.map X)) :
4078    eLpNorm X 2 P < ∞ ∧ eLpNorm X 4 P ^ 4 ≤ gK * eLpNorm X 2 P ^ 4 := by
4079  have hXs : AEStronglyMeasurable X P := hX.aestronglyMeasurable
4080  have hm : ∀ k : ℕ, ∫⁻ x, ‖x‖ₑ ^ k ∂(P.map X) = ∫⁻ ω, ‖X ω‖ₑ ^ k ∂P := fun k =>
4081    lintegral_map' (Measurable.aemeasurable (by fun_prop)) hX
4082  have h4 := gaussian_moment4_le hg
4083  have h2 := gaussian_lintegral_sq_lt_top hg
4084  rw [hm, hm] at h4
4085  rw [hm] at h2
4086  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
4087  · rw [← eLpNorm_two_pow hXs] at h2
4088    by_contra hcon
4089    rw [not_lt, top_le_iff] at hcon
4090    rw [hcon] at h2
4091    simp at h2
4092  · rw [eLpNorm_four_pow hXs, show eLpNorm X 2 P ^ 4 = (eLpNorm X 2 P ^ 2) ^ 2 by ring,
4093      eLpNorm_two_pow hXs]
4094    simpa [gK, mul_assoc] using h4
4095
4096lemma le_of_pow4_le {x y K : ℝ≥0∞} (h : x ^ 4 ≤ K * y ^ 4) : x ≤ K ^ (1 / 4 : ℝ) * y := by
4097  have := ENNReal.rpow_le_rpow h (z := 1 / 4) (by norm_num)
4098  rw [ENNReal.mul_rpow_of_nonneg _ _ (by norm_num)] at this
4099  have e : ∀ t : ℝ≥0∞, (t ^ 4) ^ (1 / 4 : ℝ) = t := fun t => by
4100    rw [← ENNReal.rpow_natCast, ← ENNReal.rpow_mul]
4101    norm_num
4102  rwa [e, e] at this
4103
4104lemma ne_top_of_pow4_ne_top {x : ℝ≥0∞} (h : x ^ 4 ≠ ∞) : x ≠ ∞ := by
4105  rintro rfl
4106  simp at h
4107
4108lemma chaos1_L4 (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ → ℕ) (c : ℕ → ℕ → B) {G : Ω → B}
4109    (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P)
4110    (hae : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun m => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω) atTop (𝓝 (G ω)))
4111    (h2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - G ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
4112    Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - G ω) 4 P) atTop (𝓝 0) ∧
4113      eLpNorm G 4 P < ∞ := by
4114  set Gm : ℕ → Ω → B := fun m => linP ξ (n m) (c m) with hGm_def
4115  have hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Gm m) P := fun m => aesm_linP hξ _ _
4116  -- Step A
4117  have stepA : ∀ m, eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P ^ 4 ≤
4118      gK * eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P ^ 4 := by
4119    intro m
4120    set a := eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P with ha
4121    set e : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun k => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm k ω - G ω) 2 P with he
4122    have hdm : ∀ k, ∫⁻ ω, ‖Gm m ω - Gm k ω‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂P ≤ gK * (a + e k) ^ 4 := by
4123      intro k
4124      have hX : AEMeasurable (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - linP ξ (n k) (c k) ω) P :=
4125        ((hGm m).sub (hGm k)).aemeasurable
4126      have hg := isCenteredGaussian_linP_sub hξ (n m) (n k) (c m) (c k)
4127      obtain ⟨_, h4⟩ := gauss_N4_le hX hg
4128      have hXs := hX.aestronglyMeasurable
4129      rw [eLpNorm_four_pow hXs] at h4
4130      refine h4.trans ?_
4131      gcongr
4132      have : (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - linP ξ (n k) (c k) ω) =
4133          (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) + (G - Gm k) := by ext ω; simp [hGm_def]
4134      rw [this]
4135      refine (eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)).trans ?_
4136      gcongr
4137      rw [he]
4138      simp only
4139      rw [show (G - Gm k) = -(fun ω => Gm k ω - G ω) by ext ω; simp, eLpNorm_neg]
4140    have hlimsup : Tendsto (fun k => gK * (a + e k) ^ 4) atTop (𝓝 (gK * a ^ 4)) := by
4141      have t1 : Tendsto (fun k => a + e k) atTop (𝓝 (a + 0)) := tendsto_const_nhds.add h2
4142      have t2 : Tendsto (fun k => (a + e k) ^ 4) atTop (𝓝 ((a + 0) ^ 4)) :=
4143        ((ENNReal.continuous_pow 4).tendsto _).comp t1
4144      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul t2 (Or.inr gK_ne_top)
4145    have hmeasd : ∀ k, AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖Gm m ω - Gm k ω‖ₑ ^ 4) P := fun k =>
4146      ((hGm m).sub (hGm k)).enorm.pow_const _
4147    have hpt : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun k => ‖Gm m ω - Gm k ω‖ₑ ^ 4) atTop
4148        (𝓝 (‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ ^ 4)) := by
4149      filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω
4150      have : Tendsto (fun k => Gm m ω - Gm k ω) atTop (𝓝 (Gm m ω - G ω)) :=
4151        tendsto_const_nhds.sub hω
4152      exact ((ENNReal.continuous_pow 4).tendsto _).comp ((continuous_enorm.tendsto _).comp this)
4153    have hG' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) P := (hGm m).sub hG
4154    rw [eLpNorm_four_pow hG']
4155    calc ∫⁻ ω, ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂P
4156        = ∫⁻ ω, liminf (fun k => ‖Gm m ω - Gm k ω‖ₑ ^ 4) atTop ∂P := by
4157          refine lintegral_congr_ae ?_
4158          filter_upwards [hpt] with ω hω
4159          exact hω.liminf_eq.symm
4160      _ ≤ liminf (fun k => ∫⁻ ω, ‖Gm m ω - Gm k ω‖ₑ ^ 4 ∂P) atTop := lintegral_liminf_le' hmeasd
4161      _ ≤ liminf (fun k => gK * (a + e k) ^ 4) atTop := liminf_le_liminf (Eventually.of_forall hdm)
4162      _ = gK * a ^ 4 := hlimsup.liminf_eq
4163  -- Step B, C
4164  have stepB : ∀ m, eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P ≤
4165      gK ^ (1 / 4 : ℝ) * eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P := fun m => le_of_pow4_le (stepA m)
4166  have hKne : gK ^ (1 / 4 : ℝ) ≠ ∞ := ENNReal.rpow_ne_top_of_nonneg (by norm_num) gK_ne_top
4167  have hlim4 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4168    refine tendsto_zero_of_le ?_ stepB
4169    simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul h2 (Or.inr hKne)
4170  refine ⟨hlim4, ?_⟩
4171  -- finiteness of the fourth moment of `G`
4172  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (hlim4.eventually (gt_mem_nhds zero_lt_one)).exists
4173  have hGmfin : eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P < ∞ := by
4174    obtain ⟨h2fin, h4⟩ := gauss_N4_le (hGm m).aemeasurable (isCenteredGaussian_linP hξ (n m) (c m))
4175    refine lt_top_iff_ne_top.2 (ne_top_of_pow4_ne_top ?_)
4176    refine ne_top_of_le_ne_top (ENNReal.mul_ne_top gK_ne_top
4177      (ENNReal.pow_ne_top h2fin.ne)) h4
4178  have : G = (Gm m) - (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) := by ext ω; simp
4179  calc eLpNorm G 4 P = eLpNorm ((Gm m) - (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω)) 4 P := by rw [← this]
4180    _ ≤ eLpNorm (Gm m) 4 P + eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 4 P := eLpNorm_sub_le (by norm_num)
4181    _ < ∞ := ENNReal.add_lt_top.2 ⟨hGmfin, hm.trans ENNReal.one_lt_top⟩
4182
4183end GaussL4
4184
4185end Aux_s17

4190section QuadP
4191variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
4192  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
4193variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}

4195/-- The finite centered quadratic polynomial `∑_{i,j<n} (ξ_i ξ_j - δ_ij) • C i j`. -/
4196def quadP (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) (ω : Ω) : B :=
4197  ∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range n, (ξ i ω * ξ j ω - if i = j then 1 else 0) • C i j
4198
4199lemma quadP_eq_Dfin (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (n : ℕ) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) (ω : Ω) :
4200    quadP ξ n C ω = Dfin ξ n (fun i j : Fin n => C i j) ω := by
4201  unfold quadP Dfin
4202  rw [Finset.sum_range]
4203  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
4204  rw [Finset.sum_range]
4205  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
4206  simp [Fin.ext_iff]
4207
4208lemma aesm_quadP (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) :
4209    AEStronglyMeasurable (quadP ξ n C) P := by
4210  have : quadP ξ n C = fun ω => Qfun (fun i j : Fin n => C i j) (Zvec ξ n ω) :=
4211    funext fun ω => quadP_eq_Dfin ξ n C ω
4212  rw [this]
4213  exact ((continuous_Qfun _).measurable.comp_aemeasurable (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ n)).aestronglyMeasurable
4214
4215lemma eLpNorm_quadP_lt_top (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) (n : ℕ) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) :
4216    eLpNorm (quadP ξ n C) 2 P < ∞ := by
4217  set c : Fin n → Fin n → B := fun i j => C i j with hc
4218  set S : ℝ := ∑ i : Fin n, ∑ j : Fin n, ‖c i j‖ with hS
4219  have hS0 : 0 ≤ S := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => Finset.sum_nonneg fun j _ => norm_nonneg _
4220  have hint : Integrable (fun ω => ‖Qfun c (Zvec ξ n ω)‖ ^ 2) P := by
4221    refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ n (g := fun z => ‖Qfun c z‖ ^ 2)
4222      ((continuous_Qfun c).norm.pow 2) (C := S ^ 2) (k := 4) fun z => ?_
4223    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
4224    calc ‖Qfun c z‖ ^ 2 ≤ (S * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2) ^ 2 :=
4225          pow_le_pow_left₀ (norm_nonneg _) (norm_Qfun_le c z) 2
4226      _ = S ^ 2 * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 4 := by ring
4227  have h1 := hint.lintegral_lt_top
4228  have hq : quadP ξ n C = fun ω => Qfun c (Zvec ξ n ω) := funext fun ω => quadP_eq_Dfin ξ n C ω
4229  have hs := (aesm_quadP hξ n C)
4230  have : eLpNorm (quadP ξ n C) 2 P ^ 2 < ∞ := by
4231    rw [eLpNorm_two_pow hs]
4232    refine lt_of_le_of_lt (le_of_eq ?_) h1
4233    refine lintegral_congr fun ω => ?_
4234    rw [hq, ← ofReal_norm, ← ENNReal.ofReal_pow (norm_nonneg _)]
4235  by_contra hcon
4236  rw [not_lt, top_le_iff] at hcon
4237  rw [hcon] at this
4238  simp at this
4239
4240/-- Zero-padding of a double coefficient array. -/
4241def padm (n : ℕ) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) : ℕ → ℕ → B := fun i j => if i < n ∧ j < n then C i j else 0
4242
4243lemma quadP_pad (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) {n N : ℕ} (h : n ≤ N) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) (ω : Ω) :
4244    quadP ξ n C ω = quadP ξ N (padm n C) ω := by
4245  unfold quadP
4246  symm
4247  rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_subset_range.2 h)]
4248  · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i hi => ?_
4249    have hi' := Finset.mem_range.1 hi
4250    rw [← Finset.sum_subset (Finset.range_subset_range.2 h)]
4251    · refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => ?_
4252      simp [padm, hi', Finset.mem_range.1 hj]
4253    · intro j _ hj
4254      have : ¬ j < n := fun hh => hj (Finset.mem_range.2 hh)
4255      simp [padm, this]
4256  · intro i _ hi
4257    have : ¬ i < n := fun hh => hi (Finset.mem_range.2 hh)
4258    refine Finset.sum_eq_zero fun j _ => ?_
4259    simp [padm, this]
4260
4261end QuadP

4268section Glue
4269set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3
4270variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
4271  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
4272variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}

4274lemma second_finite (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) (n n' : ℕ)
4275    (f : ℕ → B) (C : ℕ → ℕ → B) :
4276    ∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (linP ξ n f ω) (quadP ξ n' C ω) ∂P =
4277      (1 / 2) * ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (linP ξ n f p.1)
4278        ![quadP ξ n' C p.2, linP ξ n f p.2, linP ξ n f p.2] ∂(P.prod P) := by
4279  set N := n + n' with hN
4280  set w : Fin N → B := fun i => padv n f i with hw
4281  set c : Fin N → Fin N → B := fun i j => padm n' C i j with hc
4282  have e1 : ∀ ω, linP ξ n f ω = Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω) := fun ω => by
4283    rw [linP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_right n n') f ω, linP_eq_Tmap]
4284  have e2 : ∀ ω, quadP ξ n' C ω = Dfin ξ N c ω := fun ω => by
4285    rw [quadP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_left n' n) C ω, quadP_eq_Dfin]
4286  simp_rw [e1, e2]
4287  exact fin2_identity hξ hΦ w c
4288
4289end Glue

4296section Final
4297variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
4298  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
4299variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P]

4301lemma eLpNorm_tendsto_of_lintegral {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] {F : ℕ → Ω → E}
4302    {f : Ω → E} {q : ℝ} (hq : 0 < q) (hF : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (F m) P)
4303    (hf : AEStronglyMeasurable f P)
4304    (h : Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖F m ω - f ω‖ₑ ^ q ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
4305    Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) (ENNReal.ofReal q) P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4306  have e : ∀ m, eLpNorm (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) (ENNReal.ofReal q) P =
4307      (∫⁻ ω, ‖F m ω - f ω‖ₑ ^ q ∂P) ^ (1 / q) := fun m => by
4308    rw [eLpNorm_eq_lintegral_rpow_enorm_toReal (by simpa using hq) ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top
4309      (show AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) P from (hF m).sub hf),
4310      ENNReal.toReal_ofReal hq.le]
4311  simp_rw [e]
4312  have := ((ENNReal.continuous_rpow_const (y := 1 / q)).tendsto 0).comp h
4313  have h0 : (0 : ℝ≥0∞) ^ (1 / q) = 0 := ENNReal.zero_rpow_of_pos (one_div_pos.2 hq)
4314  rw [h0] at this
4315  exact this
4316
4317lemma eLpNorm_lt_top_of_approx {E : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup E] {F : ℕ → Ω → E} {f : Ω → E}
4318    {p : ℝ≥0∞} (hp : 1 ≤ p) (hF : ∀ m, eLpNorm (F m) p P < ∞)
4319    (h : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) p P) atTop (𝓝 0)) :
4320    eLpNorm f p P < ∞ := by
4321  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (h.eventually (gt_mem_nhds zero_lt_one)).exists
4322  have : f = (F m) - (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) := by ext ω; simp
4323  calc eLpNorm f p P = eLpNorm ((F m) - (fun ω => F m ω - f ω)) p P := by rw [← this]
4324    _ ≤ eLpNorm (F m) p P + eLpNorm (fun ω => F m ω - f ω) p P := eLpNorm_sub_le hp
4325    _ < ∞ := ENNReal.add_lt_top.2 ⟨hF m, hm.trans ENNReal.one_lt_top⟩
4326
4327end Final

4329end Aux_s20
