4335/-- Lemma 7.3 `M:F:lem:contractions`, display `F:eq:secondcontract` (lines 7805-7819): for `G`
4336centered Gaussian measurable with respect to the Gaussian space of `ξ`, and `D` in its centered
4337second chaos (for sufficiently large finite `q`), with all displayed moments finite,
4338`E[DΦ(G)[D]] = ½ E[⟨D³Φ(G), E[D ⊗ G ⊗ G]⟩]`, the right side represented by an independent
4339copy `(D', G')` of `(D, G)`. -/
4340theorem gaussian_second_contraction (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ)
4341    (G : Ω → B) (hG : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G)) (hGmeas : InFirstChaos P ξ 2 G)
4342    (D : Ω → B) (q : ℝ) (hq : 3 ≤ q) (hD : InSecondChaos P ξ q D)
4343    (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
4344    (hint1 : Integrable (fun ω => fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω)) P)
4345    (hint3 : Integrable (fun p : Ω × Ω => iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2])
4346      (P.prod P)) :
4347    ∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω) ∂P =
4348      (1 / 2) * ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1) ![D p.2, G p.2, G p.2] ∂(P.prod P) := by
4349  obtain ⟨hGas, n, c, hGlim⟩ := hGmeas
4350  obtain ⟨hDas, n', C, hDlim⟩ := hD
4351  have hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (linP ξ (n m) (c m)) P := fun m => aesm_linP hξ _ _
4352  have hDm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (quadP ξ (n' m) (C m)) P := fun m => aesm_quadP hξ _ _
4353  have hG2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - G ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4354    have := eLpNorm_tendsto_of_lintegral (q := 2) (by norm_num) hGm hGas hGlim
4355    simpa using this
4356  have hD2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => quadP ξ (n' m) (C m) ω - D ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4357    have := eLpNorm_tendsto_of_lintegral (q := q) (by linarith) hDm hDas hDlim
4358    refine tendsto_zero_of_le this fun m => eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le ?_
4359    calc (2 : ℝ≥0∞) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal 3 := by norm_num
4360      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal q := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hq
4361  -- a.e.-convergent subsequence
4362  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hae⟩ := (tendstoInMeasure_of_tendsto_eLpNorm (f := fun m => linP ξ (n m) (c m))
4363    (g := G) (p := 2) (by norm_num) hG2).exists_seq_tendsto_ae
4364  have hG2φ := hG2.comp hφ.tendsto_atTop
4365  have hD2φ := hD2.comp hφ.tendsto_atTop
4366  obtain ⟨hG4, hG4fin⟩ := chaos1_L4 hξ (fun k => n (φ k)) (fun k => c (φ k)) hGas hae hG2φ
4367  have hD2fin : eLpNorm D 2 P < ∞ :=
4368    eLpNorm_lt_top_of_approx (F := fun k => quadP ξ (n' (φ k)) (C (φ k))) (by norm_num)
4369      (fun k => eLpNorm_quadP_lt_top hξ _ _) hD2φ
4370  have hL := second_lhs_tendsto hΦ hGas hDas (fun k => hGm (φ k)) (fun k => hDm (φ k)) hG4 hD2φ
4371    hD2fin hint1
4372  have hR := second_rhs_tendsto hΦ hGas hDas (fun k => hGm (φ k)) (fun k => hDm (φ k)) hae hG4
4373    hD2φ hG4fin hD2fin hint3
4374  have hR' := hR.const_mul (1 / 2 : ℝ)
4375  have hL' : Tendsto (fun k => (1 / 2 : ℝ) * ∫ p : Ω × Ω, iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ
4376      (linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k)) p.1) ![quadP ξ (n' (φ k)) (C (φ k)) p.2,
4377        linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k)) p.2, linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k)) p.2] ∂(P.prod P)) atTop
4378      (𝓝 (∫ ω, fderiv ℝ Φ (G ω) (D ω) ∂P)) :=
4379    hL.congr fun k => (second_finite hξ hΦ _ _ _ _)
4380  exact tendsto_nhds_unique hL' hR'
4381
4382/-! #### Representation of `B ⊗̂π B` and `B^{⊗̂π 3}` for display `F:eq:firstcontract`
4383
4384The source (M L7805-7819) takes `J ∈ B^{⊗̂π 2}` (completed projective tensor square) in the first
4385chaos and pairs `D²Φ(G)` with `J` and `D³Φ(G)` with `E[J ⊗ G] ∈ B^{⊗̂π 3}`. Mathlib at this pin has
4386the projective seminorm only on the algebraic tensor product (`PiTensorProduct.projectiveSeminorm`,
4387with `PiTensorProduct.norm_eval_le_projectiveSeminorm : ‖⟨M, x⟩‖ ≤ ‖M‖ ‖x‖_π`), no completion and
4388no `J ⊗ G`. We therefore represent `B^{⊗̂π k}` through its canonical isometric image in the dual of
4389the bounded `k`-linear forms:
4390
4391* `TensorDual B k := (B [×k]→L[ℝ] ℝ) →L[ℝ] ℝ`; the elementary tensor `x₁ ⊗ ⋯ ⊗ x_k` is the
4392  evaluation functional `elemTensor x = (M ↦ M x)`;
4393* `projTensor B k` is the closed linear span of the elementary tensors in `TensorDual B k`.
4394
4395This is faithful: `(B^{⊗̂π k})* = (B [×k]→L[ℝ] ℝ)` isometrically (Ryan, *Introduction to Tensor
4396Products of Banach Spaces*, Thm. 2.9), so by Hahn–Banach the dual norm of an algebraic tensor
4397`∑ x₁ ⊗ ⋯ ⊗ x_k` in `TensorDual B k` is its projective norm; the completion `B^{⊗̂π k}` is thus
4398isometric to the closure `projTensor B k`, the pairing `⟨M, u⟩` is the evaluation `u M`, and the
4399projective inequality `|⟨M, u⟩| ≤ ‖M‖ ‖u‖_π` used in the source (M L7829-7830) is the operator
4400norm bound of `u`. For `u ∈ B ⊗̂π B` and `g ∈ B` the tensor `u ⊗ g ∈ B^{⊗̂π 3}` acts on a trilinear
4401form `M` by `⟨M, u ⊗ g⟩ = u (lastSlot M g)` with `lastSlot M g = ((a, b) ↦ M[a, b, g])`
4402(true on elementary tensors, extended by linearity and continuity). -/
4403
4404variable (B) in
4405/-- The dual of the bounded `k`-linear forms on `B`; carries `B^{⊗̂π k}` (see `projTensor`). -/
4406abbrev TensorDual (k : ℕ) : Type _ := (B [×k]→L[ℝ] ℝ) →L[ℝ] ℝ
4407
4408/-- The elementary tensor `x₁ ⊗ ⋯ ⊗ x_k` as the evaluation functional `M ↦ M[x₁, …, x_k]`. -/
4409def elemTensor {k : ℕ} (v : Fin k → B) : TensorDual B k :=
4410  ContinuousMultilinearMap.apply ℝ (fun _ : Fin k => B) ℝ v
4411
4412set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3 in
4413variable (B) in
4414/-- `B^{⊗̂π k}` (source `B^{\piten 2}`, M L7808): the closed linear span of the elementary
4415tensors in `TensorDual B k`; its norm is the projective norm (see the section comment). -/
4416def projTensor (k : ℕ) : Submodule ℝ (TensorDual B k) :=
4417  (Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range (elemTensor (B := B) (k := k)))).topologicalClosure
4418
4419set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3 in
4420instance projTensor.instNormedAddCommGroup (k : ℕ) : NormedAddCommGroup (projTensor B k) :=
4421  Submodule.normedAddCommGroup _
4422
4423set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3 in
4424instance projTensor.instNormedSpace (k : ℕ) : NormedSpace ℝ (projTensor B k) :=
4425  Submodule.normedSpace _
4426
4427/-- `lastSlot M g = ((a, b) ↦ M[a, b, g])`: the bounded bilinear form obtained by filling the last
4428slot of the trilinear form `M` with `g`; `⟨M, u ⊗ g⟩ = u (lastSlot M g)` for `u ∈ B ⊗̂π B`. -/
4429def lastSlot (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g : B) : B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ :=
4430  (ContinuousLinearMap.apply ℝ ℝ g).compContinuousMultilinearMap
4431    (continuousMultilinearCurryRightEquiv' ℝ 2 B ℝ M)
4432
4433omit [CompleteSpace B] [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B] in
4434@[simp] lemma elemTensor_apply {k : ℕ} (v : Fin k → B) (M : B [×k]→L[ℝ] ℝ) :
4435    elemTensor v M = M v := rfl
4436
4437omit [CompleteSpace B] [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B] in
4438@[simp] lemma lastSlot_apply (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g a b : B) :
4439    lastSlot M g ![a, b] = M ![a, b, g] := by
4440  have h : (Fin.snoc ![a, b] g : Fin 3 → B) = ![a, b, g] := by
4441    ext i; fin_cases i <;> rfl
4442  simp only [lastSlot, ContinuousLinearMap.compContinuousMultilinearMap_coe, Function.comp_apply,
4443    ContinuousLinearMap.apply_apply]
4444  show M (Fin.snoc ![a, b] g) = M ![a, b, g]
4445  rw [h]
4446

4459lemma hasDerivAt_iterM {Φ : B → ℝ} {k : ℕ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ (k + 1) Φ) {N : ℕ}
4460    (T : (Fin N → ℝ) →L[ℝ] B) (z : Fin N → ℝ) (i : Fin N) :
4461    HasDerivAt (fun x => iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ (T (Function.update z i x)))
4462      (fderiv ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) (T z) (T (Pi.single i 1))) (z i) := by
4463  have hu : HasDerivAt (fun x => T (Function.update z i x)) (T (Pi.single i 1)) (z i) := by
4464    have h1 := hasDerivAt_update z i (z i)
4465    have h2 := T.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt (z i) h1
4466    exact h2
4467  have hD : DifferentiableAt ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ k Φ) (T z) :=
4468    (hΦ.differentiable_iteratedFDeriv (by exact_mod_cast Nat.lt_succ_self k)) (T z)
4469  have hz : T (Function.update z i (z i)) = T z := by rw [Function.update_eq_self]
4470  have hF := hD.hasFDerivAt
4471  rw [← hz] at hF
4472  have h3 := hF.comp_hasDerivAt (z i) hu
4473  rw [hz] at h3
4474  exact h3

4476lemma fderiv_iter2_eq {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) (y a : B) :
4477    fderiv ℝ (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ) y a = lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y) a := by
4478  ext m
4479  have hm : m = ![m 0, m 1] := by ext k; fin_cases k <;> rfl
4480  have h1 := iteratedFDeriv_succ_apply_left (𝕜 := ℝ) (f := Φ) (x := y) (n := 2)
4481    (Fin.cons a ![m 0, m 1] : Fin 3 → B)
4482  have h2 : (Fin.cons a ![m 0, m 1] : Fin 3 → B) = ![a, m 0, m 1] := rfl
4483  rw [h2] at h1
4484  rw [hm]
4485  rw [lastSlot_apply, ← iter3_cyc hΦ3 y a (m 0) (m 1), h1]
4486  rfl
4487
4488lemma norm_lastSlot_le (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g : B) : ‖lastSlot M g‖ ≤ ‖M‖ * ‖g‖ := by
4489  refine ContinuousMultilinearMap.opNorm_le_bound (by positivity) fun m => ?_
4490  have hm : m = ![m 0, m 1] := by ext k; fin_cases k <;> rfl
4491  have h := abs_tri_le M (m 0) (m 1) g
4492  rw [Real.norm_eq_abs]
4493  conv_lhs => rw [hm, lastSlot_apply]
4494  simp only [Fin.prod_univ_two]
4495  calc |M ![m 0, m 1, g]| ≤ ‖M‖ * (‖m 0‖ * ‖m 1‖ * ‖g‖) := h
4496    _ = ‖M‖ * ‖g‖ * (‖m 0‖ * ‖m 1‖) := by ring
4497
4498/-- `M ↦ (g ↦ lastSlot M g)` as a continuous map into continuous linear maps. -/
4499def lastSlotL (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) : B →L[ℝ] (B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ) :=
4500  (ContinuousLinearMap.flipMultilinearEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin 2 => B) B ℝ).symm
4501    (continuousMultilinearCurryRightEquiv' ℝ 2 B ℝ M)
4502
4503lemma lastSlotL_apply (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g : B) : lastSlotL M g = lastSlot M g := by
4504  ext m
4505  rfl
4506
4507lemma continuous_lastSlotL : Continuous fun M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ => lastSlotL M :=
4508  (ContinuousLinearMap.flipMultilinearEquiv ℝ (fun _ : Fin 2 => B) B ℝ).symm.continuous.comp
4509    (continuousMultilinearCurryRightEquiv' ℝ 2 B ℝ).continuous

4511lemma continuous_lastSlot2 {X : Type*} [TopologicalSpace X] {f : X → B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ} {h : X → B}
4512    (hf : Continuous f) (hh : Continuous h) : Continuous fun x => lastSlot (f x) (h x) := by
4513  have := (isBoundedBilinearMap_apply (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B) (F := B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ)).continuous
4514  exact this.comp ((continuous_lastSlotL.comp hf).prodMk hh)

4516lemma lastSlot_sum {ι : Type*} (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (s : Finset ι) (f : ι → ℝ) (w : ι → B) :
4517    lastSlot M (∑ i ∈ s, f i • w i) = ∑ i ∈ s, f i • lastSlot M (w i) := by
4518  simp only [← lastSlotL_apply, map_sum, map_smul]

4521/-- the polynomial `∑ z_i • t_i` with values in `TensorDual B 2` -/
4522def Jfun {N : ℕ} (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) (z : Fin N → ℝ) : TensorDual B 2 :=
4523  ∑ i, z i • t i
4524
4525lemma continuous_Jfun {N : ℕ} (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) : Continuous (Jfun t) := by
4526  unfold Jfun
4527  exact continuous_finsetSum _ fun i _ => (continuous_apply i).smul continuous_const
4528
4529lemma norm_Jfun_le {N : ℕ} (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) (z : Fin N → ℝ) :
4530    ‖Jfun t z‖ ≤ (∑ i, ‖t i‖) * ‖z‖ := by
4531  unfold Jfun
4532  calc ‖∑ i, z i • t i‖ ≤ ∑ i, ‖z i • t i‖ := norm_sum_le _ _
4533    _ ≤ ∑ i, ‖z‖ * ‖t i‖ := Finset.sum_le_sum fun i _ => by
4534        rw [norm_smul]
4535        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (norm_le_pi_norm z i) (norm_nonneg _)
4536    _ = (∑ i, ‖t i‖) * ‖z‖ := by rw [← Finset.mul_sum]; ring
4537
4538lemma Jfun_apply {N : ℕ} (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) (z : Fin N → ℝ)
4539    (m : B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ) : Jfun t z m = ∑ i, z i * t i m := by
4540  simp [Jfun]

4542lemma integrable_fin1A (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4543    (w : Fin N → B) (tt : TensorDual B 2) (i : Fin N) :
4544    Integrable (fun ω => ξ i ω * tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω)))) P := by
4545  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4546  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4547  have hc2 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4548  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => z i * tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w z)))
4549    ((continuous_apply i).mul (tt.continuous.comp (hc2.comp (Tmap w).continuous)))
4550    (C := ‖tt‖ * K) (k := 1) (fun z => ?_)
4551  rw [abs_mul]
4552  have h1 : |z i| ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := by
4553    have := norm_le_pi_norm z i
4554    rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this; linarith [norm_nonneg z]
4555  have h2 : |tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w z))| ≤ ‖tt‖ * K := by
4556    rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
4557    exact (tt.le_opNorm _).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hK 2 (by norm_num) _) (norm_nonneg _))
4558  calc |z i| * |tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w z))| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) * (‖tt‖ * K) :=
4559        mul_le_mul h1 h2 (abs_nonneg _) (by linarith [norm_nonneg z])
4560    _ = ‖tt‖ * K * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 1 := by ring
4561
4562lemma integrable_fin1C (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4563    (w : Fin N → B) (tt : TensorDual B 2) (u : B) :
4564    Integrable (fun ω => tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) u)) P := by
4565  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4566  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4567  have hc3 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4568  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N
4569    (g := fun z => tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w z)) u))
4570    (tt.continuous.comp (continuous_lastSlot2 (hc3.comp (Tmap w).continuous) continuous_const))
4571    (C := ‖tt‖ * (K * ‖u‖)) (k := 0) (fun z => ?_)
4572  rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
4573  calc ‖tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w z)) u)‖
4574      ≤ ‖tt‖ * ‖lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w z)) u‖ := tt.le_opNorm _
4575    _ ≤ ‖tt‖ * (K * ‖u‖) := by
4576        apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ (norm_nonneg _)
4577        exact (norm_lastSlot_le _ _).trans
4578          (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hK 3 (le_refl _) _) (norm_nonneg _))
4579    _ = ‖tt‖ * (K * ‖u‖) * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 0 := by ring

4581lemma fin1_termA (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4582    (w : Fin N → B) (tt : TensorDual B 2) (i : Fin N) :
4583    ∫ ω, ξ i ω * tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) ∂P =
4584      ∫ ω, tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) (w i)) ∂P := by
4585  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4586  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4587  have hc2 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4588  have hc3 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4589  set T := Tmap w with hT
4590  refine stein_Z hξ i (F := fun z => tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (T z)))
4591    (F' := fun z => tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T z)) (w i))) (fun z => ?_)
4592    (tt.continuous.comp (hc2.comp T.continuous))
4593    (tt.continuous.comp (continuous_lastSlot2 (hc3.comp T.continuous) continuous_const))
4594    (C := ‖tt‖ * (K * (1 + ‖w i‖))) (p := 0) (fun z => ?_)
4595  · have h1 := hasDerivAt_iterM (Φ := Φ) (k := 2) (hΦ3.of_le (by norm_num)) T z i
4596    rw [Tmap_single, fderiv_iter2_eq hΦ3] at h1
4597    exact tt.hasFDerivAt.comp_hasDerivAt (z i) h1
4598  · have e1 : |tt (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (T z))| ≤ ‖tt‖ * K := by
4599      rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
4600      exact (tt.le_opNorm _).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hK 2 (by norm_num) _)
4601        (norm_nonneg _))
4602    have e2 : |tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T z)) (w i))| ≤ ‖tt‖ * (K * ‖w i‖) := by
4603      rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs]
4604      calc ‖tt (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T z)) (w i))‖
4605          ≤ ‖tt‖ * ‖lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T z)) (w i)‖ := tt.le_opNorm _
4606        _ ≤ ‖tt‖ * (K * ‖w i‖) := by
4607            apply mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left _ (norm_nonneg _)
4608            exact (norm_lastSlot_le _ _).trans
4609              (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hK 3 (le_refl _) _) (norm_nonneg _))
4610    have hn := norm_nonneg tt
4611    have hw := norm_nonneg (w i)
4612    simp only [pow_zero, mul_one]
4613    constructor
4614    · refine e1.trans ?_
4615      nlinarith [mul_nonneg hn hK0, mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hn hK0) hw]
4616    · refine e2.trans ?_
4617      nlinarith [mul_nonneg hn hK0, mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hn hK0) hw]
4618

4622lemma E_xi_xi (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (i k : Fin N) :
4623    ∫ ω, ξ i ω * ξ k ω ∂P = if k = i then 1 else 0 := by
4624  have := stein_Z hξ i (N := N) (F := fun z => z k) (F' := fun _ => if k = i then 1 else 0)
4625    (fun z => by
4626      by_cases h : k = i
4627      · subst h
4628        simp only [ite_true]
4629        convert hasDerivAt_id (z k) using 1
4630        ext x; simp
4631      · simp only [h, ite_false]
4632        have : (fun x => (Function.update z i x) k) = fun _ => z k := by
4633          ext x; simp [Function.update_of_ne h]
4634        simp only [this]
4635        exact hasDerivAt_const _ _)
4636    (continuous_apply k) continuous_const (C := 1) (p := 1) (fun z => by
4637      have h : |z k| ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := by
4638        have := norm_le_pi_norm z k
4639        rw [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this; linarith [norm_nonneg z]
4640      refine ⟨by simpa using h, ?_⟩
4641      have : (|(if k = i then (1 : ℝ) else 0)|) ≤ 1 := by split_ifs <;> simp
4642      have h1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ 1 + ‖z‖ := by linarith [norm_nonneg z]
4643      simpa using this.trans h1)
4644  simpa [Zvec] using this

4646lemma fin1_A (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4647    (w : Fin N → B) (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) :
4648    ∫ ω, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N ω) (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) ∂P =
4649      ∑ i : Fin N, ∫ ω, t i (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) (w i)) ∂P := by
4650  simp_rw [Jfun_apply, Zvec_apply]
4651  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_fin1A hξ hΦ w (t i) i)]
4652  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => fin1_termA hξ hΦ w (t i) i

4654lemma integrable_prod1 (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4655    (w : Fin N → B) (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) :
4656    Integrable (fun p : Ω × Ω => Jfun t (Zvec ξ N p.2)
4657      (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))) (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2))))
4658      (P.prod P) := by
4659  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4660  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4661  have hc3 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4662  set T := Tmap w with hT
4663  set A : ℝ := ∑ k, ‖w k‖ with hA
4664  set Ct : ℝ := ∑ i, ‖t i‖ with hCt
4665  have hA0 : 0 ≤ A := Finset.sum_nonneg fun k _ => norm_nonneg _
4666  have hCt0 : 0 ≤ Ct := Finset.sum_nonneg fun i _ => norm_nonneg _
4667  let F : (Fin N → ℝ) × (Fin N → ℝ) → ℝ := fun q =>
4668    Jfun t q.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T q.1)) (T q.2))
4669  have hFc : Continuous F := by
4670    have := (isBoundedBilinearMap_apply (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (F := ℝ)).continuous
4671    exact this.comp (((continuous_Jfun t).comp continuous_snd).prodMk
4672      (continuous_lastSlot2 (hc3.comp (T.continuous.comp continuous_fst))
4673        (T.continuous.comp continuous_snd)))
4674  have hmeas : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => F (Zvec ξ N p.1, Zvec ξ N p.2))
4675      (P.prod P) := by
4676    have hZ : AEStronglyMeasurable (Zvec ξ N) P := (aemeasurable_Zvec hξ N).aestronglyMeasurable
4677    exact hFc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable (hZ.comp_fst.prodMk hZ.comp_snd)
4678  have hbd : Integrable (fun y : Ω => K * (Ct * ‖Zvec ξ N y‖) * ‖T (Zvec ξ N y)‖) P := by
4679    refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => K * (Ct * ‖z‖) * ‖T z‖)
4680      ((continuous_const.mul (continuous_const.mul continuous_norm)).mul T.continuous.norm)
4681      (C := K * Ct * A) (k := 2) (fun z => ?_)
4682    have hz0 := norm_nonneg z
4683    have hT' : ‖T z‖ ≤ A * ‖z‖ := norm_Tmap_le w z
4684    rw [abs_of_nonneg (by positivity)]
4685    calc K * (Ct * ‖z‖) * ‖T z‖ ≤ K * (Ct * ‖z‖) * (A * ‖z‖) := by gcongr
4686      _ = K * Ct * A * ‖z‖ ^ 2 := by ring
4687      _ ≤ K * Ct * A * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by gcongr; nlinarith
4688  refine Integrable.mono' (hbd.comp_snd P) hmeas (Eventually.of_forall fun p => ?_)
4689  show ‖F (Zvec ξ N p.1, Zvec ξ N p.2)‖ ≤ _
4690  simp only [F]
4691  calc ‖Jfun t (Zvec ξ N p.2) (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T (Zvec ξ N p.1)))
4692        (T (Zvec ξ N p.2)))‖
4693      ≤ ‖Jfun t (Zvec ξ N p.2)‖ * ‖lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (T (Zvec ξ N p.1)))
4694        (T (Zvec ξ N p.2))‖ := (Jfun t _).le_opNorm _
4695    _ ≤ (Ct * ‖Zvec ξ N p.2‖) * (K * ‖T (Zvec ξ N p.2)‖) := by
4696        refine mul_le_mul (norm_Jfun_le t _) ((norm_lastSlot_le _ _).trans
4697          (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right (hK 3 (le_refl _) _) (norm_nonneg _))) (norm_nonneg _)
4698          (by positivity)
4699    _ = K * (Ct * ‖Zvec ξ N p.2‖) * ‖T (Zvec ξ N p.2)‖ := by ring
4700
4701
4702lemma integrable_xx (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {N : ℕ} (i k : Fin N) (c : ℝ) :
4703    Integrable (fun ω => ξ i ω * ξ k ω * c) P := by
4704  refine integrable_comp_Zvec hξ N (g := fun z => z i * z k * c)
4705    (((continuous_apply i).mul (continuous_apply k)).mul continuous_const)
4706    (C := |c|) (k := 2) (fun z => ?_)
4707  have hi : |z i| ≤ ‖z‖ := by simpa using norm_le_pi_norm z i
4708  have hk : |z k| ≤ ‖z‖ := by simpa using norm_le_pi_norm z k
4709  have h0 := norm_nonneg z
4710  rw [abs_mul, abs_mul]
4711  have : |z i| * |z k| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by
4712    calc |z i| * |z k| ≤ ‖z‖ * ‖z‖ := mul_le_mul hi hk (abs_nonneg _) h0
4713      _ ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by nlinarith
4714  calc |z i| * |z k| * |c| ≤ (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 * |c| :=
4715        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right this (abs_nonneg _)
4716    _ = |c| * (1 + ‖z‖) ^ 2 := by ring
4717
4718lemma fin1_B (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4719    (w : Fin N → B) (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) :
4720    ∫ p : Ω × Ω, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N p.2)
4721      (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))) (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2)))
4722        ∂(P.prod P) =
4723      ∑ i : Fin N, ∫ ω, t i (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) (w i)) ∂P := by
4724  rw [integral_prod _ (integrable_prod1 hξ hΦ w t)]
4725  have inner : ∀ x : Ω, ∫ y, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N y)
4726      (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x))) (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y))) ∂P =
4727      ∑ i : Fin N, t i (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x))) (w i)) := by
4728    intro x
4729    set M := iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N x)) with hM
4730    have e : ∀ y, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N y) (lastSlot M (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N y))) =
4731        ∑ i : Fin N, ∑ k : Fin N, ξ i y * ξ k y * t i (lastSlot M (w k)) := by
4732      intro y
4733      rw [Jfun_apply, Tmap_apply]
4734      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
4735      rw [lastSlot_sum, map_sum, Finset.mul_sum]
4736      refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
4737      rw [map_smul, smul_eq_mul, Zvec_apply, Zvec_apply]
4738      ring
4739    simp_rw [e]
4740    rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_finsetSum _ fun k _ =>
4741      integrable_xx hξ i k _)]
4742    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
4743    rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun k _ => integrable_xx hξ i k _)]
4744    simp_rw [integral_mul_const, E_xi_xi hξ]
4745    rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i]
4746    · simp
4747    · intro k _ hk; simp [hk]
4748    · simp
4749  simp_rw [inner]
4750  rw [integral_finsetSum _ (fun i _ => integrable_fin1C hξ hΦ w (t i) (w i))]
4751
4752lemma fin1_identity (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) {N : ℕ}
4753    (w : Fin N → B) (t : Fin N → TensorDual B 2) :
4754    ∫ ω, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N ω) (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω))) ∂P =
4755      ∫ p : Ω × Ω, Jfun t (Zvec ξ N p.2)
4756        (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.1))) (Tmap w (Zvec ξ N p.2)))
4757          ∂(P.prod P) := by
4758  rw [fin1_A hξ hΦ w t, fin1_B hξ hΦ w t]
4759
4760end Stage1

4771lemma lastSlot_sub_right (M : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g g' : B) :
4772    lastSlot M (g - g') = lastSlot M g - lastSlot M g' := by
4773  ext m
4774  have hm : m = ![m 0, m 1] := by ext k; fin_cases k <;> rfl
4775  rw [hm]
4776  simp only [lastSlot_apply, sub_apply]
4777  exact tri_sub3 M _ _ _ _
4778
4779lemma lastSlot_sub_left (M M' : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g : B) :
4780    lastSlot (M - M') g = lastSlot M g - lastSlot M' g := by
4781  ext m
4782  have hm : m = ![m 0, m 1] := by ext k; fin_cases k <;> rfl
4783  rw [hm]
4784  simp only [lastSlot_apply, sub_apply]
4785
4786lemma abs_J_lastSlot_diff_le (J J' : TensorDual B 2) (M M' : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g g' : B) :
4787    |J (lastSlot M g) - J' (lastSlot M' g')| ≤
4788      ‖J - J'‖ * (‖M‖ * ‖g‖) + ‖J'‖ * (‖M‖ * ‖g - g'‖) + ‖J'‖ * (‖M - M'‖ * ‖g'‖) := by
4789  have e : J (lastSlot M g) - J' (lastSlot M' g') =
4790      (J - J') (lastSlot M g) + (J' (lastSlot M (g - g')) + J' (lastSlot (M - M') g')) := by
4791    rw [lastSlot_sub_right, lastSlot_sub_left]
4792    simp only [sub_apply, map_sub]
4793    ring
4794  rw [e]
4795  have hJ : ∀ (K : TensorDual B 2) (m : B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ), |K m| ≤ ‖K‖ * ‖m‖ := fun K m => by
4796    have := K.le_opNorm m
4797    rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
4798  have h1 := (hJ (J - J') (lastSlot M g)).trans
4799    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (norm_lastSlot_le M g) (norm_nonneg _))
4800  have h2 := (hJ J' (lastSlot M (g - g'))).trans
4801    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (norm_lastSlot_le M (g - g')) (norm_nonneg _))
4802  have h3 := (hJ J' (lastSlot (M - M') g')).trans
4803    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (norm_lastSlot_le (M - M') g') (norm_nonneg _))
4804  have a1 := abs_add_le ((J - J') (lastSlot M g)) (J' (lastSlot M (g - g')) + J' (lastSlot (M - M') g'))
4805  have a2 := abs_add_le (J' (lastSlot M (g - g'))) (J' (lastSlot (M - M') g'))
4806  nlinarith
4807
4808lemma enorm_J_lastSlot_diff_le (J J' : TensorDual B 2) (M M' : B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (g g' : B) :
4809    ‖J (lastSlot M g) - J' (lastSlot M' g')‖ₑ ≤
4810      ‖J - J'‖ₑ * (‖M‖ₑ * ‖g‖ₑ) + ‖J'‖ₑ * (‖M‖ₑ * ‖g - g'‖ₑ) + ‖J'‖ₑ * (‖M - M'‖ₑ * ‖g'‖ₑ) := by
4811  have h := abs_J_lastSlot_diff_le J J' M M' g g'
4812  calc ‖J (lastSlot M g) - J' (lastSlot M' g')‖ₑ
4813      = ENNReal.ofReal |J (lastSlot M g) - J' (lastSlot M' g')| := by
4814        rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, ofReal_norm]
4815    _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h
4816    _ = _ := by
4817        simp (disch := positivity) only [ENNReal.ofReal_add, ENNReal.ofReal_mul, ofReal_norm]
4818
4819lemma abs_J_iter2_diff_le {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) {K2 K3 : ℝ}
4820    (hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K2) (hK3 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y‖ ≤ K3)
4821    (J J' : TensorDual B 2) (y y' : B) :
4822    |J (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) - J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y')| ≤
4823      K2 * ‖J - J'‖ + K3 * (‖J'‖ * ‖y - y'‖) := by
4824  have hJ : ∀ (K : TensorDual B 2) (m : B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ), |K m| ≤ ‖K‖ * ‖m‖ := fun K m => by
4825    have := K.le_opNorm m
4826    rwa [Real.norm_eq_abs] at this
4827  have e : J (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) - J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y') =
4828      (J - J') (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) +
4829        J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y') := by
4830    simp only [sub_apply, map_sub]; ring
4831  rw [e]
4832  have hl : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y'‖ ≤ K3 * ‖y - y'‖ :=
4833    lip_iter (k := 2) hΦ (fun z => hK3 z) y y'
4834  have h1 := (hJ (J - J') (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y)).trans
4835    (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left (hK2 y) (norm_nonneg _))
4836  have h2 := (hJ J' _).trans (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hl (norm_nonneg _))
4837  have a1 := abs_add_le ((J - J') (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y))
4838    (J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y'))
4839  nlinarith
4840
4841lemma enorm_J_iter2_diff_le {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ) {K2 K3 : ℝ} (hK20 : 0 ≤ K2)
4842    (hK30 : 0 ≤ K3)
4843    (hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K2) (hK3 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y‖ ≤ K3)
4844    (J J' : TensorDual B 2) (y y' : B) :
4845    ‖J (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) - J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y')‖ₑ ≤
4846      ENNReal.ofReal K2 * ‖J - J'‖ₑ + ENNReal.ofReal K3 * (‖J'‖ₑ * ‖y - y'‖ₑ) := by
4847  have h := abs_J_iter2_diff_le hΦ hK2 hK3 J J' y y'
4848  calc ‖J (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) - J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y')‖ₑ
4849      = ENNReal.ofReal |J (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y) - J' (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y')| := by
4850        rw [← Real.norm_eq_abs, ofReal_norm]
4851    _ ≤ _ := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal h
4852    _ = _ := by
4853        simp (disch := positivity) only [ENNReal.ofReal_add, ENNReal.ofReal_mul, ofReal_norm]
4854
4855end PointwiseFirst

4868lemma continuous_evalJ :
4869    Continuous (fun x : TensorDual B 2 × (B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ) => x.1 x.2) :=
4870  (isBoundedBilinearMap_apply (𝕜 := ℝ) (E := B [×2]→L[ℝ] ℝ) (F := ℝ)).continuous

4872lemma continuous_evalJ3 :
4873    Continuous (fun x : TensorDual B 2 × ((B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) × B) => x.1 (lastSlot x.2.1 x.2.2)) :=
4874  continuous_evalJ.comp (continuous_fst.prodMk
4875    (continuous_lastSlot2 (f := fun x : TensorDual B 2 × ((B [×3]→L[ℝ] ℝ) × B) => x.2.1)

4878lemma first_lhs_tendsto {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
4879    {G : Ω → B} {Gm : ℕ → Ω → B} {J : Ω → TensorDual B 2} {Jm : ℕ → Ω → TensorDual B 2}
4880    (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P) (hJ : AEStronglyMeasurable J P)
4881    (hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Gm m) P) (hJm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Jm m) P)
4882    (hG2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
4883    (hJ2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
4884    (hJ2fin : eLpNorm J 2 P < ∞)
4885    (hint2 : Integrable (fun ω => J ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω))) P) :
4886    Tendsto (fun m => ∫ ω, Jm m ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Gm m ω)) ∂P) atTop
4887      (𝓝 (∫ ω, J ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω)) ∂P)) := by
4888  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4889  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4890  have hc2 : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4891  have hmeas : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Jm m ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Gm m ω))) P :=
4892    fun m => continuous_evalJ.comp_aestronglyMeasurable
4893      ((hJm m).prodMk (hc2.comp_aestronglyMeasurable (hGm m)))
4894  refine tendsto_integral_of_lintegral hint2 hmeas ?_
4895  have hK2 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 2 (by norm_num) y
4896  have hK3 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 3 (by norm_num) y
4897  have hpt : ∀ m ω, ‖Jm m ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Gm m ω)) - J ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω))‖ₑ ≤
4898      ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ + ENNReal.ofReal K * (‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ) :=
4899    fun m ω => enorm_J_iter2_diff_le hΦ3 hK0 hK0 hK2 hK3 _ _ _ _
4900  have hbound : ∀ m, ∫⁻ ω, ‖Jm m ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (Gm m ω)) -
4901      J ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω))‖ₑ ∂P ≤
4902      ENNReal.ofReal K * eLpNorm (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) 2 P +
4903        ENNReal.ofReal K * (eLpNorm J 2 P * eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P) := by
4904    intro m
4905    have hJ' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) P := (hJm m).sub hJ
4906    have hG' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) P := (hGm m).sub hG
4907    calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ ω, (ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ +
4908            ENNReal.ofReal K * (‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ)) ∂P := lintegral_mono (hpt m)
4909      _ = ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ ∂P +
4910            ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, ‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ ∂P := by
4911          rw [lintegral_add_left' (hJ'.enorm.const_mul _),
4912            lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top,
4913            lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top]
4914      _ ≤ _ := by
4915          gcongr
4916          · rw [← eLpNorm_one_eq_lintegral_enorm hJ']
4917            exact eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le (by norm_num)
4918          · exact lintegral_mul2_le hJ hG'
4919  refine tendsto_zero_of_le ?_ hbound
4920  have h1 : Tendsto (fun m => ENNReal.ofReal K * eLpNorm (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) 2 P) atTop
4921      (𝓝 0) := by
4922    simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul hJ2 (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
4923  have h2 : Tendsto (fun m => ENNReal.ofReal K *
4924      (eLpNorm J 2 P * eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4925    have := ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul (a := eLpNorm J 2 P) hG2 (Or.inr hJ2fin.ne)
4926    simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul (a := ENNReal.ofReal K) this (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
4927  simpa using h1.add h2
4928
4929lemma first_rhs_tendsto {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
4930    {G : Ω → B} {Gm : ℕ → Ω → B} {J : Ω → TensorDual B 2} {Jm : ℕ → Ω → TensorDual B 2}
4931    (hG : AEStronglyMeasurable G P) (hJ : AEStronglyMeasurable J P)
4932    (hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Gm m) P) (hJm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (Jm m) P)
4933    (hae : ∀ᵐ ω ∂P, Tendsto (fun m => Gm m ω) atTop (𝓝 (G ω)))
4934    (hG2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
4935    (hJ2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0))
4936    (hG2fin : eLpNorm G 2 P < ∞) (hJ2fin : eLpNorm J 2 P < ∞)
4937    (hint3 : Integrable (fun p : Ω × Ω =>
4938      J p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2))) (P.prod P)) :
4939    Tendsto (fun m => ∫ p : Ω × Ω,
4940      Jm m p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1)) (Gm m p.2)) ∂(P.prod P)) atTop
4941      (𝓝 (∫ p : Ω × Ω, J p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2)) ∂(P.prod P))) := by
4942  obtain ⟨K, hK0, hK⟩ := memCb_bound hΦ
4943  have hΦ3 : ContDiff ℝ 3 Φ := hΦ.1
4944  have hK3 : ∀ y, ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ y‖ ≤ K := fun y => hK 3 le_rfl y
4945  have hc : Continuous (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ) := hΦ3.continuous_iteratedFDeriv (by norm_num)
4946  have hmeasT : ∀ (Gx : Ω → B) (Jx : Ω → TensorDual B 2), AEStronglyMeasurable Gx P →
4947      AEStronglyMeasurable Jx P → ∀ (Hx : Ω → B), AEStronglyMeasurable Hx P →
4948      AEStronglyMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω =>
4949        Jx p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gx p.1)) (Hx p.2))) (P.prod P) :=
4950    fun Gx Jx hGx hJx Hx hHx => continuous_evalJ3.comp_aestronglyMeasurable
4951      (hJx.comp_snd.prodMk ((hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable hGx.comp_fst).prodMk hHx.comp_snd))
4952  refine tendsto_integral_of_lintegral hint3 (fun m => hmeasT (Gm m) (Jm m) (hGm m) (hJm m) (Gm m) (hGm m)) ?_
4953  set a : ℕ → Ω → ℝ≥0∞ :=
4954    fun m ω => ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω) - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ with ha_def
4955  set h0 : Ω → ℝ≥0∞ := fun ω => ‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖G ω‖ₑ with hh0_def
4956  set h1 : ℕ → Ω → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m ω => ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ + ‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ
4957    with hh1_def
4958  have ha_meas : ∀ m, AEMeasurable (a m) P := fun m =>
4959    ((hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable (hGm m)).sub (hc.comp_aestronglyMeasurable hG)).enorm
4960  have hh0_meas : AEMeasurable h0 P := hJ.enorm.mul hG.enorm
4961  have hh1_meas : ∀ m, AEMeasurable (h1 m) P := fun m =>
4962    (((hJm m).sub hJ).enorm.mul (hGm m).enorm).add (hJ.enorm.mul ((hGm m).sub hG).enorm)
4963  have hpt : ∀ m (p : Ω × Ω),
4964      ‖Jm m p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1)) (Gm m p.2)) -
4965        J p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2))‖ₑ ≤
4966        a m p.1 * h0 p.2 + ENNReal.ofReal K * h1 m p.2 := by
4967    intro m p
4968    refine (enorm_J_lastSlot_diff_le _ _ _ _ _ _).trans ?_
4969    have hKe : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
4970      rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
4971    calc _ ≤ ‖Jm m p.2 - J p.2‖ₑ * (ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Gm m p.2‖ₑ) +
4972          ‖J p.2‖ₑ * (ENNReal.ofReal K * ‖Gm m p.2 - G p.2‖ₑ) +
4973          ‖J p.2‖ₑ * (‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1) - iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)‖ₑ *
4974            ‖G p.2‖ₑ) := by gcongr
4975      _ = _ := by simp only [ha_def, hh0_def, hh1_def]; ring
4976  have hint : ∀ m, ∫⁻ p : Ω × Ω, ‖Jm m p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m p.1)) (Gm m p.2)) -
4977        J p.2 (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2))‖ₑ ∂(P.prod P) ≤
4978      (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, h0 ω ∂P) + ENNReal.ofReal K * ∫⁻ ω, h1 m ω ∂P := by
4979    intro m
4980    calc _ ≤ ∫⁻ p : Ω × Ω, (a m p.1 * h0 p.2 + ENNReal.ofReal K * h1 m p.2) ∂(P.prod P) :=
4981          lintegral_mono (hpt m)
4982      _ = _ := by
4983          have hAE : AEMeasurable (fun p : Ω × Ω => a m p.1 * h0 p.2) (P.prod P) :=
4984            (ha_meas m).comp_fst.mul hh0_meas.comp_snd
4985          rw [lintegral_add_left' hAE,
4986            lintegral_prod_mul (ha_meas m) hh0_meas,
4987            lintegral_const_mul' _ _ ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top, lintegral_prod_snd' (hh1_meas m)]
4988  have hh0_fin : ∫⁻ ω, h0 ω ∂P ≠ ∞ :=
4989    ne_top_of_le_ne_top (ENNReal.mul_lt_top hJ2fin hG2fin).ne (lintegral_mul2_le hJ hG)
4990  have ha_lim : Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
4991    have := tendsto_lintegral_of_dominated_convergence' (μ := P) (F := a)
4992      (f := fun _ => (0 : ℝ≥0∞)) (fun _ => 2 * ENNReal.ofReal K) ha_meas ?_ ?_ ?_
4993    · simpa using this
4994    · intro m
4995      refine Eventually.of_forall fun ω => ?_
4996      have h1 : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
4997        rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
4998      have h2 : ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K := by
4999        rw [← ofReal_norm]; exact ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal (hK3 _)
5000      calc a m ω ≤ ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (Gm m ω)‖ₑ + ‖iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G ω)‖ₑ :=
5001            enorm_sub_le
5002        _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal K + ENNReal.ofReal K := add_le_add h1 h2
5003        _ = 2 * ENNReal.ofReal K := by rw [two_mul]
5004    · simp [ENNReal.mul_eq_top]
5005    · filter_upwards [hae] with ω hω
5006      exact tendsto_iff_enorm_sub_tendsto_zero.1 ((hc.tendsto (G ω)).comp hω)
5007  have hev : ∀ᶠ m in atTop, eLpNorm (Gm m) 2 P ≤ eLpNorm G 2 P + 1 := by
5008    filter_upwards [hG2.eventually (gt_mem_nhds zero_lt_one)] with m hm
5009    have : Gm m = (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) + G := by ext ω; simp
5010    calc eLpNorm (Gm m) 2 P = eLpNorm ((fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) + G) 2 P := by rw [← this]
5011      _ ≤ eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P + eLpNorm G 2 P := eLpNorm_add_le (by norm_num)
5012      _ ≤ 1 + eLpNorm G 2 P := by gcongr
5013      _ = _ := add_comm _ _
5014  set S : ℝ≥0∞ := eLpNorm G 2 P + 1 with hS
5015  have hSfin : S ≠ ∞ := (ENNReal.add_lt_top.2 ⟨hG2fin, ENNReal.one_lt_top⟩).ne
5016  set x : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) 2 P with hx
5017  set y : ℕ → ℝ≥0∞ := fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) 2 P with hy
5018  have hh1_le : ∀ᶠ m in atTop, ∫⁻ ω, h1 m ω ∂P ≤ x m * S + eLpNorm J 2 P * y m := by
5019    filter_upwards [hev] with m hm
5020    have hJ' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Jm m ω - J ω) P := (hJm m).sub hJ
5021    have hG' : AEStronglyMeasurable (fun ω => Gm m ω - G ω) P := (hGm m).sub hG
5022    have m1 := lintegral_mul2_le hJ' (hGm m)
5023    have m2 := lintegral_mul2_le hJ hG'
5024    have e1 : AEMeasurable (fun ω => ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ) P :=
5025      hJ'.enorm.mul (hGm m).enorm
5026    calc ∫⁻ ω, h1 m ω ∂P
5027        = ∫⁻ ω, ‖Jm m ω - J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω‖ₑ ∂P + ∫⁻ ω, ‖J ω‖ₑ * ‖Gm m ω - G ω‖ₑ ∂P := by
5028          simp only [hh1_def]
5029          rw [lintegral_add_left' e1]
5030      _ ≤ x m * eLpNorm (Gm m) 2 P + eLpNorm J 2 P * y m := add_le_add m1 m2
5031      _ ≤ x m * S + eLpNorm J 2 P * y m := by gcongr
5032  have hlim : Tendsto (fun m => (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, h0 ω ∂P) +
5033      ENNReal.ofReal K * (x m * S + eLpNorm J 2 P * y m)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5034    have t1 : Tendsto (fun m => (∫⁻ ω, a m ω ∂P) * (∫⁻ ω, h0 ω ∂P)) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5035      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.mul_const ha_lim (Or.inr hh0_fin)
5036    have t2 : Tendsto (fun m => x m * S) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5037      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.mul_const hJ2 (Or.inr hSfin)
5038    have t3 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm J 2 P * y m) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5039      simpa using ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul hG2 (Or.inr hJ2fin.ne)
5040    have t4 := ENNReal.Tendsto.const_mul (a := ENNReal.ofReal K) (t2.add t3)
5041      (Or.inr ENNReal.ofReal_ne_top)
5042    simpa using t1.add t4
5043  refine tendsto_zero_of_eventually_le hlim ?_
5044  filter_upwards [hh1_le] with m hm
5045  exact (hint m).trans (by gcongr)
5046
5047end Abstract3

5049end Aux_s15

5054section Glue
5055set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3
5056variable {B : Type*} [NormedAddCommGroup B] [NormedSpace ℝ B] [CompleteSpace B]
5057  [MeasurableSpace B] [BorelSpace B] [SecondCountableTopology B]
5058variable {Ω : Type*} [MeasurableSpace Ω] {P : Measure Ω} [IsProbabilityMeasure P] {ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ}

5060lemma first_finite (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ) {Φ : B → ℝ} (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ) (n n' : ℕ)
5061    (f : ℕ → B) (t : ℕ → TensorDual B 2) :
5062    ∫ ω, linP ξ n' t ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (linP ξ n f ω)) ∂P =
5063      ∫ p : Ω × Ω, linP ξ n' t p.2
5064        (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (linP ξ n f p.1)) (linP ξ n f p.2)) ∂(P.prod P) := by
5065  set N := n + n' with hN
5066  set w : Fin N → B := fun i => padv n f i with hw
5067  set s : Fin N → TensorDual B 2 := fun i => padv n' t i with hs
5068  have e1 : ∀ ω, linP ξ n f ω = Tmap w (Zvec ξ N ω) := fun ω => by
5069    rw [linP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_right n n') f ω, linP_eq_Tmap]
5070  have e2 : ∀ ω, linP ξ n' t ω = Jfun s (Zvec ξ N ω) := fun ω => by
5071    rw [linP_pad ξ (Nat.le_add_left n' n) t ω, linP_eq_Tmap, Tmap_apply]
5072    rfl
5073  simp_rw [e1, e2]
5074  exact fin1_identity hξ hΦ w s
5075
5076end Glue

5080end Contr

5082set_option maxSynthPendingDepth 3 in
5083/-- Lemma 7.3 `M:F:lem:contractions`, display `F:eq:firstcontract` (M L7805-7819): for `G`
5084centered Gaussian in the Gaussian space of `ξ` and `J ∈ B ⊗̂π B` in its first chaos (an `L^q`
5085limit of linear polynomials `∑ ξ_i • T_i` with `T_i ∈ B ⊗̂π B`, M L7816-7818, for a finite `q`;
5086`q ≥ 2` suffices for the Hölder step of the proof), with the displayed moments finite
5087(`hint2`, `hint3`; M L7808-7809),
5088`E[⟨D²Φ(G), J⟩] = E[⟨D³Φ(G), E[J ⊗ G]⟩]`.
5089
5090Representation: `B ⊗̂π B` is `projTensor B 2 ⊆ TensorDual B 2` (see the section comment), so
5091`⟨D²Φ(G), J⟩ = J (D²Φ(G))`. The right side `E_ω[⟨D³Φ(G ω), E_{ω'}[J ω' ⊗ G ω']⟩]` is written, as
5092for `thirdMomentPairing` and `gaussian_second_contraction`, by an independent copy `(J', G')` of
5093`(J, G)` on `Ω × Ω`: the bounded functional `⟨D³Φ(G ω), ·⟩` passes through the Bochner integral
5094`E[J ⊗ G]` (which exists in `B^{⊗̂π 3}` since `‖J ⊗ G‖_π ≤ ‖J‖ ‖G‖ ∈ L¹`, M L7829-7830), and
5095`⟨M, J ω' ⊗ G ω'⟩ = J ω' (lastSlot M (G ω'))`. Nothing is lost: both sides are the source's
5096expectations, and the integrability hypotheses are exactly "assume the moments in the following
5097expressions are finite". -/
5098theorem gaussian_first_contraction (ξ : ℕ → Ω → ℝ) (hξ : IsStdGaussianSeq P ξ)
5099    (G : Ω → B) (hG : IsCenteredGaussian (law P G)) (hGmeas : InFirstChaos P ξ 2 G)
5100    (J : Ω → projTensor B 2) (q : ℝ) (hq : 2 ≤ q) (hJ : InFirstChaos P ξ q J)
5101    (Φ : B → ℝ) (hΦ : MemCb 3 Φ)
5102    (hint2 : Integrable (fun ω => (J ω : TensorDual B 2) (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω))) P)
5103    (hint3 : Integrable (fun p : Ω × Ω =>
5104      (J p.2 : TensorDual B 2) (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2))) (P.prod P)) :
5105    ∫ ω, (J ω : TensorDual B 2) (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω)) ∂P =
5106      ∫ p : Ω × Ω, (J p.2 : TensorDual B 2) (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (G p.1)) (G p.2))
5107        ∂(P.prod P) := by
5108  obtain ⟨hGas, n, c, hGlim⟩ := hGmeas
5109  obtain ⟨hJas, n', T, hJlim⟩ := hJ
5110  set J' : Ω → TensorDual B 2 := fun ω => (J ω : TensorDual B 2) with hJ'
5111  set t : ℕ → ℕ → TensorDual B 2 := fun m i => (T m i : TensorDual B 2) with ht
5112  have hJ'as : AEStronglyMeasurable J' P := continuous_subtype_val.comp_aestronglyMeasurable hJas
5113  have hGm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (linP ξ (n m) (c m)) P := fun m => aesm_linP hξ _ _
5114  have hJm : ∀ m, AEStronglyMeasurable (linP ξ (n' m) (t m)) P := fun m => aesm_linP hξ _ _
5115  have hpt : ∀ m ω, ‖(∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n' m), ξ i ω • T m i) - J ω‖ₑ =
5116      ‖linP ξ (n' m) (t m) ω - J' ω‖ₑ := by
5117    intro m ω
5118    have : (((∑ i ∈ Finset.range (n' m), ξ i ω • T m i) - J ω : projTensor B 2) :
5119        TensorDual B 2) = linP ξ (n' m) (t m) ω - J' ω := by
5120      simp [linP, ht, hJ']
5121    rw [← this]
5122    rfl
5123  have hJlim' : Tendsto (fun m => ∫⁻ ω, ‖linP ξ (n' m) (t m) ω - J' ω‖ₑ ^ q ∂P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5124    simpa only [hpt] using hJlim
5125  have hG2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => linP ξ (n m) (c m) ω - G ω) 2 P) atTop (𝓝 0) := by
5126    have := eLpNorm_tendsto_of_lintegral (q := 2) (by norm_num) hGm hGas hGlim
5127    simpa using this
5128  have hJ2 : Tendsto (fun m => eLpNorm (fun ω => linP ξ (n' m) (t m) ω - J' ω) 2 P) atTop
5129      (𝓝 0) := by
5130    have := eLpNorm_tendsto_of_lintegral (q := q) (by linarith) hJm hJ'as hJlim'
5131    refine tendsto_zero_of_le this fun m => eLpNorm_le_eLpNorm_of_exponent_le ?_
5132    calc (2 : ℝ≥0∞) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal 2 := by norm_num
5133      _ ≤ ENNReal.ofReal q := ENNReal.ofReal_le_ofReal hq
5134  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hae⟩ := (tendstoInMeasure_of_tendsto_eLpNorm (f := fun m => linP ξ (n m) (c m))
5135    (g := G) (p := 2) (by norm_num) hG2).exists_seq_tendsto_ae
5136  have hG2φ := hG2.comp hφ.tendsto_atTop
5137  have hJ2φ := hJ2.comp hφ.tendsto_atTop
5138  have hG2fin : eLpNorm G 2 P < ∞ :=
5139    eLpNorm_lt_top_of_approx (F := fun k => linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k))) (by norm_num)
5140      (fun k => eLpNorm_linP_lt_top hξ _ _) hG2φ
5141  have hJ2fin : eLpNorm J' 2 P < ∞ :=
5142    eLpNorm_lt_top_of_approx (F := fun k => linP ξ (n' (φ k)) (t (φ k))) (by norm_num)
5143      (fun k => eLpNorm_linP_lt_top hξ _ _) hJ2φ
5144  have hL := first_lhs_tendsto hΦ hGas hJ'as (fun k => hGm (φ k)) (fun k => hJm (φ k)) hG2φ hJ2φ
5145    hJ2fin hint2
5146  have hR := first_rhs_tendsto hΦ hGas hJ'as (fun k => hGm (φ k)) (fun k => hJm (φ k)) hae hG2φ
5147    hJ2φ hG2fin hJ2fin hint3
5148  have hL' : Tendsto (fun k => ∫ p : Ω × Ω, linP ξ (n' (φ k)) (t (φ k)) p.2
5149      (lastSlot (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 3 Φ (linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k)) p.1))
5150        (linP ξ (n (φ k)) (c (φ k)) p.2)) ∂(P.prod P)) atTop
5151      (𝓝 (∫ ω, J' ω (iteratedFDeriv ℝ 2 Φ (G ω)) ∂P)) :=
5152    hL.congr fun k => first_finite hξ hΦ _ _ _ _
5153  exact tendsto_nhds_unique hL' hR
5154
5155end Contractions
